First, GaoYao proposes a unified framework categorizing evaluation tasks into three cultural layers (General Multilingual, Cross-cultural, Monocultural) and nine cognitive sub-layers. Second, we achieve native-quality expansion by leveraging experts to rigorously localize subjective benchmarks into 19 languages and synthesizing cross-cultural test sets for 34 cultures, surpassing prior coverage by up to 111%. Third, we conduct an in-depth diagnostic analysis on 20+ flagship and compact LLMs. Our findings reveal significant geographical performance disparities and distinct gaps between tasks, offering a reliable map for future work. We release the benchmark (https://github.com/lunyiliu/GaoYao).

</details>


### [37] [Construction of a Battery Research Knowledge Graph using a Global Open Catalog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20241)
*Luca Foppiano,Sae Dieb,Malik Zain,Kazuki Kasama,Keitaro Sodeyama,Mikiko Tanifuji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个基于OpenAlex构建电池研究领域作者中心知识图谱的完整pipeline。通过结合OpenAlex的粗粒度概念与KeyBERT+ChatGPT提取的细粒度关键词，并考虑来源、作者排名和时间衰减等因素加权，生成189,581篇电池相关论文的作者研究描述向量，支持跨机构相似度计算、社区发现和探索性搜索，并以RDF格式链接Wikidata实现互联互通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池研究领域发展迅速且高度交叉学科化，导致难以跨机构追踪相关专家和识别潜在合作者。现有作者中心分析局限于机构知识库，缺乏跨机构尺度的语义相似性度量方法。

Method: 1) 基于OpenAlex构建作者中心知识图谱；2) 使用KeyBERT结合ChatGPT(gpt-3.5-turbo)从标题和摘要中提取细粒度关键词短语；3) 将粗粒度OpenAlex概念与细粒度关键词结合，生成加权研究描述向量；4) 向量分量按研究描述符来源、作者排名位置和时间近衰进行加权；5) 知识图谱以RDF序列化并链接Wikidata标识符。

Result: 构建了包含189,581篇电池相关学术作品的知识图谱；生成的向量支持作者相似度计算、社区检测和基于浏览器的探索性搜索；RDF序列化使知识图谱可与外部链接开放数据源互操作，并可扩展至电池领域之外。

Conclusion: 与以往局限于机构知识库的作者中心分析不同，该方法在跨机构规模上运行，并将相似性建立在领域语义基础上，而非单纯依赖引用或合著关系结构。

Abstract: Battery research is a rapidly growing and highly interdisciplinary field, making it increasingly difficult to track relevant expertise and identify potential collaborators across institutional boundaries. In this work, we present a pipeline for constructing an author-centric knowledge graph of battery research built on OpenAlex, a large-scale open bibliographic catalogue. For each author, we derive a weighted research descriptors vector that combines coarse-grained OpenAlex concepts with fine-grained keyphrases extracted from titles and abstracts using KeyBERT with ChatGPT (gpt-3.5-turbo) as the backend model, selected after evaluating multiple alternatives. Vector components are weighted by research descriptor origin, authorship position, and temporal recency. The framework is applied to a corpus of 189,581 battery-related works. The resulting vectors support author-author similarity computation, community detection, and exploratory search through a browser-based interface. The knowledge graph is then serialized in RDF and linked to Wikidata identifiers, making it interoperable with external linked open data sources and extensible beyond the battery domain. Unlike prior author-centric analyses confined to institutional repositories, our approach operates at cross-institutional scale and grounds similarity in domain semantics rather than citation or co-authorship structure alone.

</details>


### [38] [Hybrid Policy Distillation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20244)
*Wenhong Zhu,Ruobing Xie,Rui Wang,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出混合策略蒸馏（HPD），通过统一视角分析知识蒸馏方法，结合前向和反向KL散度的优势，并采用离线和近似在线采样策略，在数学推理、对话和代码生成任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏方法的有效性依赖于散度方向、优化策略和数据 regime 的交织选择，现有的KD方法缺乏统一框架来理解这些设计选择之间的关系。

Method: 1) 分解现有KD方法设计，建立统一视角，将KD重新表述为token级别的加权对数似然目标；2) 提出HPD，集成前向KL（覆盖模式）和反向KL（寻找模式）的互补优势；3) 结合离策略数据与轻量级近似在线采样。

Result: HPD在多种模型家族和规模上展现出改进的优化稳定性、计算效率和最终性能，适用于长生成数学推理任务以及短生成对话和代码任务。

Conclusion: 通过统一框架理解和结合不同KL散度的优势，HPD能够有效平衡模式覆盖与模式寻求，提升知识蒸馏在LLM压缩中的表现。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) is a powerful paradigm for compressing large language models (LLMs), whose effectiveness depends on intertwined choices of divergence direction, optimization strategy, and data regime. We break down the design of existing KD methods and present a unified view that establishes connections between them, reformulating KD as a reweighted log-likelihood objective at the token level. We further propose Hybrid Policy Distillation (HPD), which integrates the complementary advantages of forward and reverse KL to balance mode coverage and mode-seeking, and combines off-policy data with lightweight, approximate on-policy sampling. We validate HPD on long-generation math reasoning as well as short-generation dialogue and code tasks, demonstrating improved optimization stability, computational efficiency, and final performance across diverse model families and scales. The code related to this work is available at https://github.com/zwhong714/Hybrid-Policy-Distillation.

</details>


### [39] [RADS: Reinforcement Learning-Based Sample Selection Improves Transfer Learning in Low-resource and Imbalanced Clinical Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20256)
*Wei Han,David Martinez,Anna Khanina,Lawrence Cavedon,Karin Verspoor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对迁移学习中少样本微调的样本选择问题，提出RADS方法，利用强化学习在极端低资源和类别不平衡条件下智能选择最具信息量的训练样本，显著提升模型迁移能力并保持鲁棒性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前少样本微调策略依赖样本选择质量，但现有主动学习方法（不确定性采样、多样性采样）在极端低资源和类别不平衡条件下容易偏好异常值而非真正有信息量的样本，导致性能下降

Method: 提出基于强化学习的自适应领域采样策略RADS，通过强化学习智能识别最具有信息量的样本

Result: 在多个真实临床数据集上的实验表明，RADS能有效提升模型迁移能力，同时在极端类别不平衡条件下保持鲁棒性能

Conclusion: RADS方法在极端类别不平衡条件下仍能保持鲁棒性能，显著优于传统采样方法

Abstract: A common strategy in transfer learning is few shot fine-tuning, but its success is highly dependent on the quality of samples selected as training examples. Active learning methods such as uncertainty sampling and diversity sampling can select useful samples. However, under extremely low-resource and class-imbalanced conditions, they often favor outliers rather than truly informative samples, resulting in degraded performance. In this paper, we introduce RADS (Reinforcement Adaptive Domain Sampling), a robust sample selection strategy using reinforcement learning (RL) to identify the most informative samples. Experimental evaluations on several real world clinical datasets show our sample selection strategy enhances model transferability while maintaining robust performance under extreme class imbalance compared to traditional methods.

</details>


### [40] [Surrogate modeling for interpreting black-box LLMs in medical predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20331)
*Changho Han,Songsoo Kim,Dong Won Kim,Leo Anthony Celi,Jaewoong Kim,SungA Bae,Dukyong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种替代建模框架，通过大量提示模拟多种场景来定量解释大语言模型（LLM）编码的知识，揭示LLM对输入变量的感知程度。在医学预测的初步实验中，该框架成功识别出与医学知识相悖的关联以及LLM中持续存在的种族偏见，可作为LLM安全应用的"红色警戒"指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽然在海量数据上训练并编码了丰富的真实世界知识，但其黑箱特性使得知识编码的机制和程度难以解释。此外，LLM可能固化训练数据中的错误信息和 societal biases，引发安全担忧，亟需可解释性方法来揭示这些问题。

Method: 提出替代建模（surrogate modeling）框架：针对特定领域假设，通过跨广泛模拟场景的大量提示，利用输入-输出对近似LLM的潜在知识空间，从而量化LLM对各输入变量与输出关系的"感知"程度。

Result: 医学预测实验验证了框架有效性，成功揭示了：(1) 与既定医学知识相悖的关联；(2) 科学上已被驳斥的种族假设在LLM编码知识中的持续存在。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为红色警戒指标，帮助识别LLM中的错误知识和偏见，支持模型的安全可靠应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), trained on vast datasets, encode extensive real-world knowledge within their parameters, yet their black-box nature obscures the mechanisms and extent of this encoding. Surrogate modeling, which uses simplified models to approximate complex systems, can offer a path toward better interpretability of black-box models. We propose a surrogate modeling framework that quantitatively explains LLM-encoded knowledge. For a specific hypothesis derived from domain knowledge, this framework approximates the latent LLM knowledge space using observable elements (input-output pairs) through extensive prompting across a comprehensive range of simulated scenarios. Through proof-of-concept experiments in medical predictions, we demonstrate our framework's effectiveness in revealing the extent to which LLMs "perceive" each input variable in relation to the output. Particularly, given concerns that LLMs may perpetuate inaccuracies and societal biases embedded in their training data, our experiments using this framework quantitatively revealed both associations that contradict established medical knowledge and the persistence of scientifically refuted racial assumptions within LLM-encoded knowledge. By disclosing these issues, our framework can act as a red-flag indicator to support the safe and reliable application of these models.

</details>


### [41] [Graph2Counsel: Clinically Grounded Synthetic Counseling Dialogue Generation from Client Psychological Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20382)
*Aishik Mandal,Hiba Arnaout,Clarissa W. Ong,Juliet Bockhorst,Kate Sheehan,Rachael Moldow,Tanmoy Chakraborty,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Graph2Counsel是一个基于图结构生成合成心理咨询会话的框架，通过Client Psychological Graphs (CPGs)编码来访者的认知、情感和行为状态之间的关系，解决了现有方法中结构性依赖缺失、心理一致性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康需求增长，但隐私约束导致真实咨询数据稀缺；现有合成数据集方法依赖非结构化或半结构化文本，忽略来访者认知、情感、行为状态间的结构性依赖，导致心理不一致性和数据质量下降。

Method: 提出Graph2Counsel框架，包含两个核心创新：(1) Client Psychological Graphs (CPGs)：编码来访者思维、情感和行为的相互关系；(2) 结构化提示管道：结合咨询师策略和CPG引导；探索了CoT和Multi-Agent Feedback两种提示策略。生成76个CPG模板，产出760个咨询会话。

Result: 专家评估表明：Graph2Counsel数据集在特异性、咨询师胜任力、真实性、对话流畅性和安全性方面均优于先前数据集，评分者一致性良好（Krippendorff's α = 0.70）。基于该数据集微调的开源模型在CounselingBench和CounselBench上表现提升。

Conclusion: Graph2Counsel成功将图结构引入合成心理咨询数据生成，有效提升了数据的心理一致性和质量，为LLM在心理健康咨询领域的应用提供了高质量训练数据。

Abstract: Rising demand for mental health support has increased interest in using Large Language Models (LLMs) for counseling. However, adapting LLMs to this high-risk safety-critical domain is hindered by the scarcity of real-world counseling data due to privacy constraints. Synthetic datasets provide a promising alternative, but existing approaches often rely on unstructured or semi-structured text inputs and overlook structural dependencies between a client's cognitive, emotional, and behavioral states, often producing psychologically inconsistent interactions and reducing data realism and quality. We introduce Graph2Counsel, a framework for generating synthetic counseling sessions grounded in Client Psychological Graphs (CPGs) that encode relationships among clients' thoughts, emotions, and behaviors. Graph2Counsel employs a structured prompting pipeline guided by counselor strategies and CPG, and explores prompting strategies including CoT (Wei et al., 2022) and Multi-Agent Feedback (Li et al., 2025a). Graph2Counsel produces 760 sessions from 76 CPGs across diverse client profiles. In expert evaluation, our dataset outperforms prior datasets on specificity, counselor competence, authenticity, conversational flow, and safety, with substantial inter-annotator agreement (Krippendorff's $α$ = 0.70). Fine-tuning an open-source model on this dataset improves performance on CounselingBench (Nguyen et al., 2025) and CounselBench (Li et al., 2025b), showing downstream utility. We also make our code and data public.

</details>


### [42] [DialToM: A Theory of Mind Benchmark for Forecasting State-Driven Dialogue Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20443)
*Neemesh Yadav,Palakorn Achananuparp,Jing Jiang,Ee-Peng Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DialToM是一个基于自然人类对话构建的人类验证基准，用于评估LLMs的ToM能力。研究发现LLMs在心理状态识别上表现良好，但在利用心理状态预测社会轨迹上存在明显不足，暗示其ToM能力可能源于虚假相关性而非稳健推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs虽已表现出 Theory of Mind (ToM) 能力，但这些能力是否源于稳健推理还是虚假相关性仍不明确，需要新的评估基准来验证。

Method: DialToM benchmark采用自然人类对话的多选题框架，评估 Literal ToM（心理状态预测）和 Functional ToM（通过前瞻性诊断预测）。Functional ToM 通过探查模型是否仅凭心理状态画像就能识别状态一致的对话轨迹来评估。

Result: 发现显著的推理不对称性：LLMs擅长识别心理状态，但大多数（除Gemini 3 Pro外）无法利用这种理解来预测社会轨迹。此外，人类与LLM生成的推论之间仅存在弱语义相似性。

Conclusion: Current LLMs demonstrate significant limitations in ToM reasoning — while they can identify mental states, they largely fail to leverage this understanding for forecasting social trajectories, suggesting potential spurious correlations rather than robust reasoning. Gemini 3 Pro appears to be an exception.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been shown to possess Theory of Mind (ToM) abilities. However, it remains unclear whether this stems from robust reasoning or spurious correlations. We introduce DialToM, a human-verified benchmark built from natural human dialogue using a multiple-choice framework. We evaluate not only mental state prediction (Literal ToM) but also the functional utility of these states (Functional ToM) through Prospective Diagnostic Forecasting -- probing whether models can identify state-consistent dialogue trajectories solely from mental-state profiles. Our results reveal a significant reasoning asymmetry: while LLMs excel at identifying mental states, most (except for Gemini 3 Pro) fail to leverage this understanding to forecast social trajectories. Additionally, we find only weak semantic similarities between human and LLM-generated inferences. To facilitate reproducibility, the DialToM dataset and evaluation code are publicly available at https://github.com/Stealth-py/DialToM.

</details>


### [43] [Decoding Text Spans for Efficient and Accurate Named-Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20447)
*Andrea Maracani,Savas Ozkan,Junyi Zhu,Sinan Mutlu,Mete Ozay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SpanDec，一个高效的基于跨度的命名实体识别(NER)框架，通过在最终transformer层计算跨度表示交互，并引入跨度过滤机制剪枝低置信度候选，在保持竞争力的准确率同时显著提升吞吐量和降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的NER准确率由基于跨度的框架实现，但这些方法需枚举大量候选跨度并为每个候选生成标记增强输入，导致推理成本高、扩展性差，难以满足工业场景中的严格延迟和吞吐量约束。

Method: (1) 轻量级解码器：跨度表示交互仅在最终transformer层计算，避免早期层的冗余计算；(2) 跨度过滤机制：在枚举阶段剪枝低可能性候选，在昂贵处理前减少计算量。

Result: SpanDec在多个基准测试上与竞争性基线模型准确率相当，同时提升了吞吐量并降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: SpanDec实现了更好的准确率-效率权衡，适合大规模服务和高吞吐量场景，以及端侧设备部署。

Abstract: Named Entity Recognition (NER) is a key component in industrial information extraction pipelines, where systems must satisfy strict latency and throughput constraints in addition to strong accuracy. State-of-the-art NER accuracy is often achieved by span-based frameworks, which construct span representations from token encodings and classify candidate spans. However, many span-based methods enumerate large numbers of candidates and process each candidate with marker-augmented inputs, substantially increasing inference cost and limiting scalability in large-scale deployments. In this work, we propose SpanDec, an efficient span-based NER framework that targets this bottleneck. Our main insight is that span representation interactions can be computed effectively at the final transformer stage, avoiding redundant computation in earlier layers via a lightweight decoder dedicated to span representations. We further introduce a span filtering mechanism during enumeration to prune unlikely candidates before expensive processing. Across multiple benchmarks, SpanDec matches competitive span-based baselines while improving throughput and reducing computational cost, yielding a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off suitable for high-volume serving and on-device applications.

</details>


### [44] [Not all ANIMALs are equal: metaphorical framing through source domains and semantic frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20454)
*Yulia Otmakhova,Matteo Guida,Lea Frermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个计算框架，通过源域与语义框架的交互来分析隐喻如何塑造对复杂问题的理解。将该框架应用于气候变化新闻和移民话语分析，发现保守派和自由派在同一隐喻源域内系统性地使用不同语义框架，揭示了隐喻框架分析的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻是强有力的框架工具，但仅凭源域无法完全解释隐喻所唤起的具体联想。源域与语义框架之间的交互如何影响隐喻对复杂问题的理解，这一问题尚未被深入研究。

Method: 提出了一个计算框架，通过源域（source domains）和语义框架（semantic frames）的结合来推导话语隐喻。在气候变化的新闻语料和移民话语语料上进行了应用实验，并按政治意识形态（自由派vs保守派）分析了隐喻使用的差异。

Result: 1. 在气候变化新闻中，发现了已知的源域，并揭示了区分问题呈现方式的细微框架层面关联。2. 在移民话语中，发现自由派和保守派在同一源域内系统性地使用不同的语义框架：保守派倾向于使用强调不可控性的框架，而自由派选择中性的或更具"受害者"色彩的语义框架。

Conclusion: 本研究首次将概念隐喻理论与NLP方法相结合，提供了一种从话语中自动发现隐喻并进行细粒度分析的计算框架。研究揭示了隐喻不仅是简单的框架工具，其效果取决于源域与语义框架的交互作用，且政治意识形态在隐喻使用上存在系统性差异。

Abstract: Metaphors are powerful framing devices, yet their source domains alone do not fully explain the specific associations they evoke. We argue that the interplay between source domains and semantic frames determines how metaphors shape understanding of complex issues, and present a computational framework that allows to derive salient discourse metaphors through their source domains and semantic frames. Applying this framework to climate change news, we uncover not only well-known source domains but also reveal nuanced frame-level associations that distinguish how the issue is portrayed. In analyzing immigration discourse across political ideologies, we demonstrate that liberals and conservatives systematically employ different semantic frames within the same source domains, with conservatives favoring frames emphasizing uncontrollability and liberals choosing neutral or more ``victimizing'' semantic frames. Our work bridges conceptual metaphor theory and linguistics, providing the first NLP approach for discovery of discourse metaphors and fine-grained analysis of differences in metaphorical framing. Code, data and statistical scripts are available at https://github.com/julia-nixie/ConceptFrameMet.

</details>


### [45] [Knowledge Capsules: Structured Nonparametric Memory Units for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20487)
*Bin Ju,Shenfeng Weng,Danying Zhou,Kunkai Su,Rongkai Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出知识胶囊（Knowledge Capsules）作为LLM的新型外部记忆机制，通过将结构化关系知识编译为注意力兼容的键值表示，直接参与模型注意力计算，绕过传统RAG通过上下文扩展注入知识的间接方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM将知识编码在参数权重中，导致更新或扩展成本高昂；现有RAG方案仅通过上下文扩展注入知识，外部知识以token形式在注意力机制中竞争，影响间接且不稳定，尤其在长上下文和多跳推理场景下表现欠佳。

Method: 提出知识胶囊——结构化的非参数记忆单元，可从文档语料库直接由冻结的基础模型构建，表示归一化的关系知识；设计外部键值注入（KVI）框架，将胶囊编译为注意力兼容的键值表示，使外部知识直接参与模型注意力计算。

Result: 在多个QA基准测试中一致超越RAG和GraphRAG，长上下文和多跳推理场景下准确率和稳定性均显著提升，且无需任何参数更新。

Conclusion: 从上下文级增强转向记忆级交互的知识整合范式转变，为解决LLM知识更新问题提供了更稳定高效的新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) encode knowledge in parametric weights, making it costly to update or extend without retraining. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) mitigates this limitation by appending retrieved text to the input, but operates purely through context expansion, where external knowledge competes as tokens within the attention mechanism. As a result, its influence is indirect and often unstable, particularly in long context and multi hop reasoning scenarios. We propose Knowledge Capsules, structured nonparametric memory units that represent normalized relational knowledge and can be constructed directly from document corpora using a frozen base model. Instead of injecting knowledge as text, we introduce an External Key Value Injection (KVI) framework that compiles capsules into attention-compatible key value representations, enabling external knowledge to directly participate in the model's attention computation. By shifting knowledge integration from context-level augmentation to memory level interaction, the proposed framework consistently outperforms RAG and GraphRAG across multiple QA benchmarks, with improved stability and accuracy in long context and multi hop reasoning, while requiring no parameter updates.

</details>


### [46] [AutoGraph-R1: End-to-End Reinforcement Learning for Knowledge Graph Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15339)
*Hong Ting Tsang,Jiaxin Bai,Haoyu Huang,Qiao Xiao,Tianshi Zheng,Baixuan Xu,Shujie Liu,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Building effective knowledge graphs (KGs) for Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is pivotal for advancing question answering (QA) systems. However, its effectiveness is hindered by a fundamental disconnect: the knowledge graph (KG) construction process is decoupled from its downstream application, yielding suboptimal graph structures. To bridge this gap, we introduce AutoGraph-R1, the first framework to directly optimize KG construction for task performance using Reinforcement Learning (RL). AutoGraph-R1 trains an LLM constructor by framing graph generation as a policy learning problem, where the reward is derived from the graph's functional utility in a RAG pipeline. We design two novel, task-aware reward functions, one for graphs as knowledge carriers and another as knowledge indices. Across multiple QA benchmarks, AutoGraph-R1 consistently enables graph RAG methods to achieve significant performance gains over using task-agnostic baseline graphs. Our work shows it is possible to close the loop between construction and application, shifting the paradigm from building intrinsically ``good'' graphs to building demonstrably ``useful'' ones.

</details>


### [47] [Aligning Stuttered-Speech Research with End-User Needs: Scoping Review, Survey, and Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20535)
*Hawau Olamide Toyin,Mutiah Apampa,Toluwani Aremu,Humaid Alblooshi,Ana Rita Valente,Gonçalo Leal,Zhengjun Yue,Zeerak Talat,Hanan Aldarmaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过文献综述和利益相关者调研（70人），分析口吃语音识别领域的研究现状与用户需求之间的差距，旨在为口吃社区提供更贴合实际需求的技术解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音识别系统在实际应用中对手口吃语音识别效果不佳，现有的评估方法和研究优先级缺乏系统性地以终端用户需求为导向

Method: 结合文献综述与利益相关者调研：1）对话音识别中口吃语音的论文进行范围审查；2）对70名利益相关者（包括成年口吃者和言语语言病理学家）进行问卷调查

Result: 提出口吃语音研究分类法；识别当前研究方向与利益相关者需求之间的差距

Conclusion: 提出具体指南和方向，以满足口吃社区的真实需求

Abstract: Atypical speech is receiving greater attention in speech technology research, but much of this work unfolds with limited interdisciplinary dialogue. For stuttered speech in particular, it is widely recognised that current speech recognition systems fall short in practice, and current evaluation methods and research priorities are not systematically grounded in end-user experiences and needs. In this work, we analyse these gaps through 1) a scoping review of papers that deal with stuttered speech and 2) a survey of 70 stakeholders, including adults who stutter and speech-language pathologists. By analysing these two perspectives, we propose a taxonomy of stuttered-speech research, identify where current research directions diverge from the needs articulated by stakeholders, and conclude by outlining concrete guidelines and directions towards addressing the real needs of the stuttering community.

</details>


### [48] [Toward Cross-Lingual Quality Classifiers for Multilingual Pretraining Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20549)
*Yassine Turki,Vinko Sabolčec,Bettina Messmer,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现嵌入空间中的质量标记具有跨语言一致性，允许高资源语言补贴低资源语言的过滤任务。多语言混合池化策略（特别是结合Q3采样或保留率调优）可显著提升质量过滤效果，尤其对高资源语言效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM规模化，数据策展从最大化规模转向优化信噪比进行质量过滤；然而许多语言缺乏足够的高质量原生数据来训练鲁棒的质量分类器。

Method: 评估多种过滤策略，包括跨语言迁移、三分位数采样（Q3）和保留率调优；使用103B tokens训练1B模型进行实验。

Result: 大规模多语言混合池化在排序稳定性和聚合准确率上均优于单语基线，为高资源语言（法语）带来1.2%的聚合归一化准确率提升，低资源语言持平或超越单语基线；但规模本身不能保证稳定性。

Conclusion: 高质量语言的标记可以跨语言迁移以支持低资源语言的质量过滤；大规模多语言混合优于单语基线；但规模本身不能保证稳定性；对于高资源语言，需要通过Q3采样或保留率调优来优化决策边界，才能充分利用多语言信号的潜力。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) scale, data curation has shifted from maximizing volume to optimizing the signal-to-noise ratio by performing quality filtering. However, for many languages, native high quality data is insufficient to train robust quality classifiers. This work investigates the idea that quality markers in embedding space may show cross-lingual consistency, which would allow high-resource languages to subsidize the filtering of low-resource ones. We evaluate various filtering strategies, including cross-lingual transfer, third quartile sampling (Q3), and retention rate tuning. Our results demonstrate that massive multilingual pooling frequently outperforms monolingual baselines in both rank stability and aggregate accuracy for a 1B model trained on 103B tokens, delivering gains for high resource languages (1.2% increase in aggregate normalized accuracy for French) and matching or exceeding monolingual baselines for low-resource languages. However, we find that scale alone does not guarantee stability. Furthermore, for high-resource languages like French, we show that refining the decision boundary through third quartile sampling (Q3) or tuning the retention rate is necessary to fully leverage the multilingual signal.

</details>


### [49] [Where Reasoning Breaks: Logic-Aware Path Selection by Controlling Logical Connectives in LLMs Reasoning Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20564)
*Seunghyun Park,Yuanyuan Lei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在多步逻辑推理中的脆弱性问题，提出逻辑连接词是高熵分叉点的核心假设，并设计了针对逻辑关键转折点的多层干预框架，在准确率与效率权衡上优于全局推理扩展方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多步逻辑推理中表现脆弱，单个推理步骤的错误会沿推理链传播导致整体失败。研究者发现逻辑连接词是这种结构性脆弱性的主要来源——模型在这些高熵分叉点频繁难以确定正确的逻辑方向。

Method: 提出三层干预框架：(1) 基于梯度的逻辑导向(Gradient-based Logical Steering)，将LLM内部表征引导至有效推理子空间；(2) 局部分支搜索(Localized Branching)，通过定向前瞻搜索消解歧义；(3) 目标迁移偏好优化(Targeted Transition Preference Optimization)，一种外科手术式强化学习目标，在逻辑支点处选择性优化单token偏好。

Result: 通过仅聚焦逻辑关键转换的干预，框架在准确率-效率权衡上优于全局推理时间扩展方法（如束搜索和自洽性）。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了"干预逻辑连接词选择可引导LLM走向更正确的逻辑方向"的假设，为提升LLM结构化推理能力提供了新的针对性优化范式。

Abstract: While LLMs demonstrate impressive reasoning capabilities, they remain fragile in multi-step logical deduction, where a single transition error can propagate through the entire reasoning chain, leading to unstable performance. In this work, we identify logical connectives as primary points of this structural fragility. Through empirical analysis, we show that connective tokens function as high entropy forking points, at which models frequently struggle to determine the correct logical direction. Motivated by this observation, we hypothesize that intervening in logical connective selection can guide LLMs toward more correct logical direction, thereby improving the overall reasoning chain. To validate this hypothesis, we propose a multi-layered framework that intervenes specifically at these logic-critical junctions in the reasoning process. Our framework includes (1) Gradient-based Logical Steering to guide LLMs internal representations towards valid reasoning subspaces, (2) Localized Branching to resolve ambiguity via targeted look-ahead search, and (3) Targeted Transition Preference Optimization, a surgical reinforcement learning objective that selectively optimizes single-token preferences at logical pivots. Crucially, by concentrating intervention solely on logic-critical transitions, our framework achieves a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off compared to global inference time scaling methods like beam search and self-consistency.

</details>


### [50] [Ask Only When Needed: Proactive Retrieval from Memory and Skills for Experience-Driven Lifelong Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20572)
*Yuxuan Cai,Jie Zhou,Qin Chen,Liang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProactAgent是一种主动检索式的终身学习框架，通过ExpOnEvo实现策略更新与记忆 refinement，并通过ProactRL建模检索为显式策略动作，在SciWorld、AlfWorld和StuLife上分别达到73.50%、71.28%成功率和竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身学习方法将经验检索视为被动操作，仅在任务初始化或完成步骤后触发检索，导致智能体无法在交互过程中识别知识差距并主动检索最相关经验。

Method: 提出ProactAgent框架，包含两个核心组件：1) ExpOnEvo通过策略更新和记忆 refinement实现持续改进；2) ProactRL将检索建模为显式策略动作，通过配对分支过程奖励学习何时检索以及检索什么内容。

Result: 在SciWorld上成功率达73.50%，AlfWorld达71.28%，StuLife达到与闭源模型竞争的性能水平，同时显著降低检索开销。

Conclusion: 主动检索范式可有效解决终身学习中的经验利用问题，结构化经验库结合RL驱动的主动检索策略能够实现更高效的终身学习智能体。

Abstract: Online lifelong learning enables agents to accumulate experience across interactions and continually improve on long-horizon tasks. However, existing methods typically treat retrieval from past experience as a passive operation, triggering it only at task initialization or after completing a step. Consequently, agents often fail to identify knowledge gaps during interaction and proactively retrieve the most useful experience for the current decision. To address this limitation, we present ProactAgent, an experience-driven lifelong learning framework for proactive retrieval over a structured experience base. We first introduce Experience-Enhanced Online Evolution (ExpOnEvo), which enables continual improvement through both policy updates and memory refinement. The experience base organizes historical interactions into typed repositories, including factual memory, episodic memory, and behavioral skills, so that retrieval can provide both relevant evidence and actionable guidance. On top of this, we propose Proactive Reinforcement Learning-based Retrieval (ProactRL), which models retrieval as an explicit policy action and learns when and what to retrieve via paired-branch process rewards. By comparing continuations from identical interaction prefixes with and without retrieval, ProactRL provides step-level supervision for retrieval decisions, encouraging retrieval only when it leads to better task outcomes or higher efficiency. Experiments on SciWorld, AlfWorld, and StuLife show that ProactAgent consistently improves lifelong agent performance, achieving success rates of 73.50\% on SciWorld and 71.28\% on AlfWorld while substantially reducing retrieval overhead, and attains performance competitive with proprietary models on StuLife.

</details>


### [51] [Cooperative Profiles Predict Multi-Agent LLM Team Performance in AI for Science Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20658)
*Shivani Kumar,Adarsh Bharathwaj,David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过对35个开源LLM在六个行为经济学游戏中的表现进行基准测试，发现游戏中的合作行为能够预测多智能体AI系统在科学任务中的表现，合作倾向是LLM的独立可测量属性，可作为多智能体部署前的快速筛查工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统需要协调共享资源（GPU、信用额度等），但尚不清楚这些系统在标准化合作场景中的行为是否能预测其在实际协作任务中的表现。

Method: 对35个开源LLM在六个行为经济学游戏中进行基准测试，然后将游戏中的合作特征与AI-for-Science任务中的表现进行关联分析，控制了多个混淆变量。

Result: 能够在游戏中有效协调、投资于乘法团队生产（而非贪婪策略）的模型，在科学报告的准确性、质量和完成度三个维度上表现更好。控制多个因素后，这些关联依然显著。

Conclusion: 合作倾向是LLM的独立可测量属性，不能简单归因于通用能力。行为经济学游戏框架可作为多智能体系统部署前快速、低成本的"合作适应性"筛查工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built from teams of large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for collaborative scientific reasoning and problem-solving. These systems require agents to coordinate under shared constraints, such as GPUs or credit balances, where cooperative behavior matters. Behavioral economics provides a rich toolkit of games that isolate distinct cooperation mechanisms, yet it remains unknown whether a model's behavior in these stylized settings predicts its performance in realistic collaborative tasks. Here, we benchmark 35 open-weight LLMs across six behavioral economics games and show that game-derived cooperative profiles robustly predict downstream performance in AI-for-Science tasks, where teams of LLM agents collaboratively analyze data, build models, and produce scientific reports under shared budget constraints. Models that effectively coordinate games and invest in multiplicative team production (rather than greedy strategies) produce better scientific reports across three outcomes, accuracy, quality, and completion. These associations hold after controlling for multiple factors, indicating that cooperative disposition is a distinct, measurable property of LLMs not reducible to general ability. Our behavioral games framework thus offers a fast and inexpensive diagnostic for screening cooperative fitness before costly multi-agent deployment.

</details>


### [52] [ORPHEAS: A Cross-Lingual Greek-English Embedding Model for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20666)
*Ioannis E. Livieris,Athanasios Koursaris,Alexandra Apostolopoulou,Konstantinos Kanaris Dimitris Tsakalidis,George Domalis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ORPHEAS is a specialized Greek-English embedding model for bilingual retrieval-augmented generation, trained using knowledge graph-based fine-tuning on multi-domain corpus, achieving superior performance over state-of-the-art multilingual models while maintaining cross-lingual capability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing multilingual embedding models distribute representational capacity across many languages, limiting optimization for Greek and failing to encode morphological complexity and domain-specific terminology.

Method: Propose ORPHEAS, a specialized Greek-English embedding model trained with a high-quality dataset generated by a knowledge graph-based fine-tuning methodology on a diverse multi-domain corpus.

Result: Outperforms state-of-the-art multilingual embedding models on both monolingual and cross-lingual retrieval benchmarks.

Conclusion: Domain-specialized fine-tuning on morphologically complex languages does not compromise cross-lingual retrieval capability.

Abstract: Effective retrieval-augmented generation across bilingual Greek--English applications requires embedding models capable of capturing both domain-specific semantic relationships and cross-lingual semantic alignment. Existing multilingual embedding models distribute their representational capacity across numerous languages, limiting their optimization for Greek and failing to encode the morphological complexity and domain-specific terminological structures inherent in Greek text. In this work, we propose ORPHEAS, a specialized Greek--English embedding model for bilingual retrieval-augmented generation. ORPHEAS is trained with a high quality dataset generated by a knowledge graph-based fine-tuning methodology which is applied to a diverse multi-domain corpus, which enables language-agnostic semantic representations. The numerical experiments across monolingual and cross-lingual retrieval benchmarks reveal that ORPHEAS outperforms state-of-the-art multilingual embedding models, demonstrating that domain-specialized fine-tuning on morphologically complex languages does not compromise cross-lingual retrieval capability.

</details>


### [53] [Intersectional Fairness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20677)
*Chaima Boufaied,Ronnie De Souza Santos,Ann Barcomb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了六种大语言模型在交叉人口属性上的公平性问题。研究发现：现代LLM在歧义语境中表现良好但导致公平指标信息量不足；在消歧语境中，准确率受刻板印象影响，种族-性别交叉维度偏见最显著；各组结果分布仍不均匀，重复运行中响应一致性也存在变异。最终表明，没有LLM能在交叉场景中实现持续可靠的公平行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益部署于社会敏感场景，但关于其在交叉人口属性上的公平性和偏见问题缺乏系统评估。

Method: 使用六个LLM、两个基准数据集的歧义和消歧语境，通过偏差评分、子群公平指标、准确率和多轮一致性分析进行系统评估。

Result: ①歧义语境下指标信息量有限；②消歧语境中准确率受刻板印象对齐影响；③种族-性别交叉处方向性偏见最强；④各交叉组结果分布仍不均匀；⑤重复运行中响应一致性存在变异。

Conclusion: 当前LLM表现出的能力部分源于刻板印象一致的线索，没有模型能在交叉场景中实现持续可靠的公平行为。评估需超越准确率，结合偏差、子群公平和一致性指标。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in socially sensitive settings, raising concerns about fairness and biases, particularly across intersectional demographic attributes. In this paper, we systematically evaluate intersectional fairness in six LLMs using ambiguous and disambiguated contexts from two benchmark datasets. We assess LLM behavior using bias scores, subgroup fairness metrics, accuracy, and consistency through multi-run analysis across contexts and negative and non-negative question polarities. Our results show that while modern LLMs generally perform well in ambiguous contexts, this limits the informativeness of fairness metrics due to sparse non-unknown predictions. In disambiguated contexts, LLM accuracy is influenced by stereotype alignment, with models being more accurate when the correct answer reinforces a stereotype than when it contradicts it. This pattern is especially pronounced in race-gender intersections, where directional bias toward stereotypes is stronger. Subgroup fairness metrics further indicate that, despite low observed disparity in some cases, outcome distributions remain uneven across intersectional groups. Across repeated runs, responses also vary in consistency, including stereotype-aligned responses. Overall, our findings show that apparent model competence is partly associated with stereotype-consistent cues, and no evaluated LLM achieves consistently reliable or fair behavior across intersectional settings. These findings highlight the need for evaluation beyond accuracy, emphasizing the importance of combining bias, subgroup fairness, and consistency metrics across intersectional groups, contexts, and repeated runs.

</details>


### [54] [Exploiting LLM-as-a-Judge Disposition on Free Text Legal QA via Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20726)
*Mohamed Hesham Elganayni,Runsheng Chen,Sebastian Nagl,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索了LLM-as-a-Judge评估中提示词优化和评判者选择的系统性影响，发现自动优化方法（ProTeGi）优于人工设计，且宽松评判者产生的提示词泛化能力更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge范式在法律问答评估中日益重要，但提示词设计（人工设计vs自动优化）和评判者选择对评估效果的影响尚不明确。

Method: 在LEXam基准上，使用ProTeGi方法对两个评判模型（Qwen3-32B、DeepSeek-V3）进行提示词优化，跨四个任务模型测试，并验证跨评判者的迁移效果。

Result: 自动优化持续优于基线；宽松评判者带来更高且更稳定的提升；宽松反馈优化的提示词向严格评判者的迁移效果优于反向迁移。

Conclusion: 算法优化提示词可超越人工设计；评判者在优化过程中的倾向性（宽松/严格）会显著影响提示词的泛化能力，宽松评判者产生更通用的提示词，而严格评判者易产生过拟合于特定评判者的提示词。

Abstract: This work explores the role of prompt design and judge selection in LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations of free text legal question answering. We examine whether automatic task prompt optimization improves over human-centered design, whether optimization effectiveness varies by judge feedback style, and whether optimized prompts transfer across judges. We systematically address these questions on the LEXam benchmark by optimizing task prompts using the ProTeGi method with feedback from two judges (Qwen3-32B, DeepSeek-V3) across four task models, and then testing cross-judge transfer. Automatic optimization consistently outperforms the baseline, with lenient judge feedback yielding higher and more consistent gains than strict judge feedback. Prompts optimized with lenient feedback transfer better to strict judges than the reverse direction. Analysis reveals that lenient judges provide permissive feedback, yielding prompts with broader applicability, whereas strict judges produce restrictive feedback, leading to judge-specific overfitting. Our findings demonstrate algorithmically optimizing prompts on training data can outperform human-centered prompt design and that judges' dispositions during optimization shape prompt generalizability. Code and optimized prompts are available at https://github.com/TUMLegalTech/icail2026-llm-judge-gaming.

</details>


### [55] [RespondeoQA: a Benchmark for Bilingual Latin-English Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20738)
*Marisa Hudspeth,Patrick J. Burns,Brendan O'Connor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个以拉丁语为中心的问答基准数据集RespondeoQA，包含约7,800个问答对，涵盖多种问题类型，并对LLaMa 3、Qwen QwQ和o3-mini三种大语言模型进行了评估，发现技能导向问题对所有模型都较具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏针对拉丁语这一专门语言文化领域的问答基准数据集，现有大语言模型在该领域的能力尚待系统评估。

Method: 从拉丁语教学资源（包括考试、Quizbowl风格问答和教科书，跨度从1800年代至今）中自动提取、清洗问题，并进行人工审核，最终构建多样化的问答对数据集。

Result: 三个大模型在技能导向问题上表现均较差；推理模型（QwQ、o3-mini）在韵律划分和文学手法任务上表现更好，但整体提升有限；QwQ在拉丁语提问时表现稍好，而LLaMa 3和o3-mini的表现更依赖于任务类型。

Conclusion: RespondeoQA为评估大语言模型在专门语言文化领域的能力提供了新资源，且其创建流程可轻松适配于其他语言。

Abstract: We introduce a benchmark dataset for question answering and translation in bilingual Latin and English settings, containing about 7,800 question-answer pairs. The questions are drawn from Latin pedagogical sources, including exams, quizbowl-style trivia, and textbooks ranging from the 1800s to the present. After automated extraction, cleaning, and manual review, the dataset covers a diverse range of question types: knowledge- and skill-based, multihop reasoning, constrained translation, and mixed language pairs. To our knowledge, this is the first QA benchmark centered on Latin. As a case study, we evaluate three large language models -- LLaMa 3, Qwen QwQ, and OpenAI's o3-mini -- finding that all perform worse on skill-oriented questions. Although the reasoning models perform better on scansion and literary-device tasks, they offer limited improvement overall. QwQ performs slightly better on questions asked in Latin, but LLaMa3 and o3-mini are more task dependent. This dataset provides a new resource for assessing model capabilities in a specialized linguistic and cultural domain, and the creation process can be easily adapted for other languages. The dataset is available at: https://github.com/slanglab/RespondeoQA

</details>


### [56] [Can "AI" Be a Doctor? A Study of Empathy, Readability, and Alignment in Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20791)
*Mariano Barone,Francesco Di Serio,Roberto Moio,Marco Postiglione,Giuseppe Riccio,Antonio Romano,Vincenzo Moscato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究量化评估了医疗场景中LLM与临床医师沟通质量的差距，发现基线模型在情感极性和语言复杂度上与医师存在显著偏差，虽共情提示可缓解但无法弥补语义保真度缺陷；协作式重写策略表现最优，提示LLM更适合作为医患沟通的辅助增强工具而非独立替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在医疗领域的应用日益广泛，但其与临床沟通标准的对齐程度缺乏量化评估，需要系统性地探究模型输出与专业医师之间的差距。

Method: 多维度评估框架，从语义保真度、可读性、情感共鸣三个维度对通用型与领域专用LLM进行系统比较；涵盖结构化医学解释与真实医患交互场景；采用双重利益相关者评估（医生与患者）。

Result: 基线模型情感极性偏激（大模型负面情感达43-45%，vs医生37%）；大模型语言复杂度显著更高（GPT-5/Claude的FKGL达16.91-17.60，vs医生11.47-12.50）；共情提示可降低极端负面情感及复杂度（GPT-5降低6.87 FKGL），但未能提升语义保真度；协作重写整体对齐效果最佳；改写配置语义相似度最高（均值0.93）；患者一致偏好重写变体。

Conclusion: LLMs最有效地定位为协作式沟通增强工具，而非临床专业知识的替代品；无任何模型在认知标准上超越医生，而协作重写变体始终获得患者青睐。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in healthcare, yet their communicative alignment with clinical standards remains insufficiently quantified. We conduct a multidimensional evaluation of general-purpose and domain-specialized LLMs across structured medical explanations and real-world physician-patient interactions, analyzing semantic fidelity, readability, and affective resonance. Baseline models amplify affective polarity relative to physicians (Very Negative: 43.14-45.10% vs. 37.25%) and, in larger architectures such as GPT-5 and Claude, produce substantially higher linguistic complexity (FKGL up to 16.91-17.60 vs. 11.47-12.50 in physician-authored responses). Empathy-oriented prompting reduces extreme negativity and lowers grade-level complexity (up to -6.87 FKGL points for GPT-5) but does not significantly increase semantic fidelity. Collaborative rewriting yields the strongest overall alignment. Rephrase configurations achieve the highest semantic similarity to physician answers (up to mean = 0.93) while consistently improving readability and reducing affective extremity. Dual stakeholder evaluation shows that no model surpasses physicians on epistemic criteria, whereas patients consistently prefer rewritten variants for clarity and emotional tone. These findings suggest that LLMs function most effectively as collaborative communication enhancers rather than replacements for clinical expertise.

</details>


### [57] [Convergent Evolution: How Different Language Models Learn Similar Number Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20817)
*Deqing Fu,Tianyi Zhou,Mikhail Belkin,Vatsal Sharan,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了语言模型如何学习表示数字，发现不同模型（Transformer、Linear RNN、LSTM、词嵌入）都能学习到具有周期T=2,5,10尖峰的傅里叶域特征，但只有部分模型能学习到几何可分离的特征用于线性分类模T运算。论文证明傅里叶域稀疏性是几何可分离的必要非充分条件，并发现数据、架构、优化器和分词器都会影响几何可分离特征的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型如何表示数字这一基本语义单元？不同架构的模型是否通过相同机制学习数字表示？傅里叶域的周期性与几何可分离性之间存在什么关系？这些问题的解答有助于理解语言模型的归纳偏置和表征学习机制。

Method: 理论分析：证明傅里叶域稀疏性是mod-T几何可分离的必要非充分条件；实证研究：对比不同架构（Transformer、Linear RNN、LSTM、词嵌入）、不同训练信号（自然语言数据、合成加法问题）、不同分词器对数字特征学习的影响。

Result: 发现数字特征存在两层 hierarchy：（1）所有模型都学习到周期T的傅里叶域尖峰特征；（2）只有部分模型进一步学习到几何可分离特征。几何可分离特征可通过两种途径习得：自然语言中的互补共现信号（如文本-数字共现、跨数字交互），或多token加法问题（而非单token加法）。

Conclusion: 特征学习存在趋同进化现象：多样的模型从不同的训练信号中学习到相似的特征。傅里叶域稀疏性本身不足以保证几何可分离性，模型需要更丰富的监督信号才能习得可用的数值推理能力。

Abstract: Language models trained on natural text learn to represent numbers using periodic features with dominant periods at $T=2, 5, 10$. In this paper, we identify a two-tiered hierarchy of these features: while Transformers, Linear RNNs, LSTMs, and classical word embeddings trained in different ways all learn features that have period-$T$ spikes in the Fourier domain, only some learn geometrically separable features that can be used to linearly classify a number mod-$T$. To explain this incongruity, we prove that Fourier domain sparsity is necessary but not sufficient for mod-$T$ geometric separability. Empirically, we investigate when model training yields geometrically separable features, finding that the data, architecture, optimizer, and tokenizer all play key roles. In particular, we identify two different routes through which models can acquire geometrically separable features: they can learn them from complementary co-occurrence signals in general language data, including text-number co-occurrence and cross-number interaction, or from multi-token (but not single-token) addition problems. Overall, our results highlight the phenomenon of convergent evolution in feature learning: A diverse range of models learn similar features from different training signals.

</details>


### [58] [Parallel-SFT: Improving Zero-Shot Cross-Programming-Language Transfer for Code RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20835)
*Zhaofeng Wu,Shiqi Wang,Boya Peng,Anuj Goyal,Melanie Kambadur,Sebastian Ruder,Yoon Kim,Chloe Bi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究零样本跨编程语言迁移的代码强化学习问题，发现Llama-3.1在源语言上的RL训练无法提升目标语言性能，甚至可能降低性能。提出Parallel-SFT方法，通过在SFT阶段引入多语言等价代码（parallel programs）来改善迁移能力，使模型形成以功能为中心的隐空间，从而提升跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型在主流编程语言（如C++、Python）上表现优异，但在资源较少的编程语言上能力受限。核心假设是大部分编程技能具有通用性，从一种语言学到的能力应能迁移到其他语言。

Method: 1) 提出零样本跨编程语言迁移任务；2) 发现仅在源语言上进行RL训练会损害或无法提升目标语言性能；3) 提出Parallel-SFT策略，在SFT阶段的数据混合中加入"parallel programs"（功能等价的跨语言代码），之后再进行RL训练。

Result: Parallel-SFT显著提升了跨语言迁移能力，使RL训练后的模型能更好地泛化到未见的编程语言。

Conclusion: Parallel-SFT通过引入跨语言等价代码，使模型学习到更以功能为中心的隐空间表示，功能等价的程序在不同语言间形成更紧密的聚类，从而解释了迁移能力提升的原因。

Abstract: Modern language models demonstrate impressive coding capabilities in common programming languages (PLs), such as C++ and Python, but their performance in lower-resource PLs is often limited by training data availability. In principle, however, most programming skills are universal across PLs, so the capability acquired in one PL should transfer to others. In this work, we propose the task of zero-shot cross-programming-language transfer for code RL. We find that, for Llama-3.1, RL training for code generation in a source PL fails to improve, and sometimes even degrades, the performance on other target PLs. To address this, we hypothesize that effective RL transfer requires a generalizable SFT initialization before RL. We thus propose **Parallel-SFT**, an SFT strategy that incorporates "parallel programs" -- functionally equivalent code implemented in multiple PLs -- into the data mixture. We demonstrate that this improves transferability: when we subsequently perform RL on our Parallel-SFT model, we observe better generalization to unseen PLs. Analysis of the model internal representations reveals that Parallel-SFT leads to a more functionality-centric latent space, where equivalent programs across PLs are more tightly clustered, which we hypothesize to contribute to the improved transferability.

</details>


### [59] [SpeechParaling-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Paralinguistic-Aware Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20842)
*Ruohan Liu,Shukang Yin,Tao Wang,Dong Zhang,Weiji Zhuang,Shuhuai Ren,Ran He,Caifeng Shan,Chaoyou Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpeechParaling-Bench是一个针对大音频-语言模型(LALMs)的副语言学特征评估基准，将特征覆盖从50个扩展到100+个，包含1000+英中平行语音查询，设计三类渐进难度的任务，并开发基于LALM的成对比较评估管道以缓解主观性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LALMs的副语言学特征评估受限于粗糙的特征覆盖范围和评估固有的主观性，缺乏全面、可靠的评测基准。

Method: 1) 提出SpeechParaling-Bench基准，将副语言学特征覆盖从<50扩展至>100个细粒度特征；2) 构建1000+英中平行语音查询；3) 设计三类渐进难度任务：细粒度控制、句内变化、上下文适应；4) 开发基于LALM的成对比较评估管道，以相对偏好替代绝对评分。

Result: 实验揭示当前LALMs存在显著不足：即使领先的商业模型在综合静态控制和动态调制副语言学特征方面表现欠佳；在情境对话中，43.3%的错误源于未能正确理解副语言学提示。

Conclusion: 当前LALMs在副语言学建模方面存在重大局限，亟需开发更鲁棒的副语言学建模能力以实现类人语音助手。

Abstract: Paralinguistic cues are essential for natural human-computer interaction, yet their evaluation in Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) remains limited by coarse feature coverage and the inherent subjectivity of assessment. To address these challenges, we introduce SpeechParaling-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for paralinguistic-aware speech generation. It expands existing coverage from fewer than 50 to over 100 fine-grained features, supported by more than 1,000 English-Chinese parallel speech queries, and is organized into three progressively challenging tasks: fine-grained control, intra-utterance variation, and context-aware adaptation. To enable reliable evaluation, we further develop a pairwise comparison pipeline, in which candidate responses are evaluated against a fixed baseline by an LALM-based judge. By framing evaluation as relative preference rather than absolute scoring, this approach mitigates subjectivity and yields more stable and scalable assessments without costly human annotation. Extensive experiments reveal substantial limitations in current LALMs. Even leading proprietary models struggle with comprehensive static control and dynamic modulation of paralinguistic features, while failure to correctly interpret paralinguistic cues accounts for 43.3% of errors in situational dialogue. These findings underscore the need for more robust paralinguistic modeling toward human-aligned voice assistants.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [60] [Do Small Language Models Know When They're Wrong? Confidence-Based Cascade Scoring for Educational Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19781)
*Tyler Burleigh*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大规模学生作业自动评分中，如何利用语言模型的自我置信度（verbalized confidence）作为"级联系统"中的路由信号，在小模型和大模型之间智能分配任务。实验表明，置信度区分能力是决定级联系统性能的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模自动化评分场景中，需要平衡准确率、成本和延迟。传统方法难以确定哪些样本需要升级到更大的模型处理，造成资源浪费或准确率下降。

Method: 使用verbalized confidence（要求模型在预测时附带数值置信度）作为路由信号，在2,100条专家标注的学生-AI数学对话数据上，测试了GPT-5.4、Claude 4.5+、Gemini 3.1模型对构建的级联系统。

Result: 小模型的置信度区分能力差异显著（AUROC 0.857至接近退化分布）；置信度与人类评分难度呈负相关；最佳级联系统在kappa仅下降0.017（0.802 vs 0.819）的情况下，成本降低76%，延迟降低61%。置信度区分能力是关键：具备有意义方差的小模型可实现无统计显著性差异的kappa，而置信度退化的小模型则无法弥补准确率差距。

Conclusion: 小模型的置信度区分能力是级联系统的决定性瓶颈。具有强区分能力的模型允许在成本和准确率之间灵活权衡；区分能力弱的模型则无法有效构建级联系统。这为实践者选择用于级联部署的小模型提供了关键评估维度。

Abstract: Automated scoring of student work at scale requires balancing accuracy against cost and latency. In "cascade" systems, small language models (LMs) handle easier scoring tasks while escalating harder ones to larger LMs -- but the challenge is determining which cases to escalate. We explore verbalized confidence -- asking the LM to state a numerical confidence alongside its prediction -- as a routing signal. Using 2,100 expert-scored decisions from student-AI math conversations, we evaluate cascade systems built from GPT-5.4, Claude 4.5+, and Gemini 3.1 model pairs. We find that: (1) confidence discrimination varies widely across small LMs, with the best achieving AUROC 0.857 and the worst producing a near-degenerate confidence distribution; (2) confidence tracks human scoring difficulty, with lower LM confidence where annotators disagreed and took longer to score; (3) the best cascade approached large-LM accuracy (kappa 0.802 vs. 0.819) at 76% lower cost and 61% lower latency. Confidence discrimination is the bottleneck: the two small LMs with meaningful confidence variance yielded cascades with no statistically detectable kappa loss, while the third -- whose confidence was near-degenerate -- could not close the accuracy gap regardless of threshold. Small LMs with strong discrimination let practitioners trade cost for accuracy along the frontier; those without it do not.

</details>


### [61] [Diagnosing Urban Street Vitality via a Visual-Semantic and Spatiotemporal Framework for Street-Level Economics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19798)
*Xinxin Zhuo,Mengyuan Niu,Ruizhe Wang,Junyan Yang,Qiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于街景图像（SVI）和位置服务数据（LBS）的街道经济活力指数（SEVI）框架，通过视觉语义解析和时空滞后设计，克服现有方法语义浅层化、忽视品牌层级异质性和结构性衰退的问题，实现微尺度街道级经济精准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有街景图像分析存在三个关键缺陷：语义表征浅层化、品牌层级异质性被忽略、无法捕捉结构性衰退特征。这些局限制约了精准空间资源分配的准确性，亟需构建融合物理、语义和时空维度的综合评估框架。

Method: 提出视觉语义与场论结合的时空框架，包含以下核心方法：① 基于实例分割的街道景观解析（识别招牌、玻璃界面、店面关闭）；② 双阶段VLM-LLM流水线将招牌标准化为全球层级体系，量化品牌溢价指数并做空间平滑；③ 引入LBS数据构建时间滞后设计克服静态SVI局限；④ 类别加权高斯溢出模型；⑤ 构建覆盖商业活动、空间利用、物理环境的三维诊断系统。实验采用时间滞后地理加权回归。

Result: 在南京八个时段上的实验揭示了准因果时空异质性规律：街道活力源于层级品牌集聚与商业体外部性的交互作用；高质量界面在午间和傍晚呈现峰值吸引力；结构性衰退产生滞后性夜间排斥效应。

Conclusion: 该框架为精准空间治理提供了循证支撑，证实了融合视觉语义、层级品牌溢价与时空滞后效应的街道经济活力评估方法的有效性，为城市精细化管理决策提供了新工具。

Abstract: Micro-scale street-level economic assessment is fundamental for precision spatial resource allocation. While Street View Imagery (SVI) advances urban sensing, existing approaches remain semantically superficial and overlook brand hierarchy heterogeneity and structural recession. To address this, we propose a visual-semantic and field-based spatiotemporal framework, operationalized via the Street Economic Vitality Index (SEVI).
  Our approach integrates physical and semantic streetscape parsing through instance segmentation of signboards, glass interfaces, and storefront closures. A dual-stage VLM-LLM pipeline standardizes signage into global hierarchies to quantify a spatially smoothed brand premium index. To overcome static SVI limitations, we introduce a temporal lag design using Location-Based Services (LBS) data to capture realized demand. Combined with a category-weighted Gaussian spillover model, we construct a three-dimensional diagnostic system covering Commercial Activity, Spatial Utilization, and Physical Environment.
  Experiments based on time-lagged geographically weighted regression across eight tidal periods in Nanjing reveal quasi-causal spatiotemporal heterogeneity. Street vibrancy arises from interactions between hierarchical brand clustering and mall-induced externalities. High-quality interfaces show peak attraction during midday and evening, while structural recession produces a lagged nighttime repulsion effect. The framework offers evidence-based support for precision spatial governance.

</details>


### [62] [Model Capability Assessment and Safeguards for Biological Weaponization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19811)
*Michael Richter*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究对四个主流AI模型进行生物滥用风险基准测试，发现Gemini的能力似乎超过其安全校准水平，在匿名访问环境下可生成令人担忧的武器化相关内容，提示AI模型输出的技术监管必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Growing concerns among AI leaders that advances in model reasoning may facilitate biological misuse by low-expertise users, while major labs acknowledge their safeguards are still evolving rather than settled. The apparent capability outpacing moderation calibration, particularly in Gemini, warrants systematic empirical investigation.

Method: Benchmarking approach using four major AI models (ChatGPT 5.2 Auto, Gemini 3 Pro Thinking, Claude Opus 4.5, Meta's Muse Spark Thinking) tested on 73 novice-framed benign STEM prompts. Two-tier evaluation: (1) benign quantitative task assessment, and (2) subtle harmful intent detection via edge case prompts. Focused weaponization analysis on Gemini across four access environments.

Result: Gemini and Meta achieved highest scores on benign tasks; ChatGPT was partially useful but text-thinned; Claude showed sparse outputs with false-positive refusals. Edge cases exposed Gemini's contextual awareness deficits. Concerning Gemini outputs included poison-ivy-to-transit escalation scenarios, poison production/extraction via anonymous logged-out AI Mode, and other weaponization-relevant content.

Conclusion: Biological misuse via AI may escalate as a geopolitical tool, necessitating urgent U.S. policy responses. The study provides actionable guidance for 25 high-risk agents to differentiate legitimate use cases from higher-risk ones, emphasizing the need for regulatory frameworks treating AI outputs as regulated technical data.

Abstract: AI leaders and safety reports increasingly warn that advances in model reasoning may enable biological misuse, including by low-expertise users, while major labs describe safeguards as expanding but still evolving rather than settled. This study benchmarks ChatGPT 5.2 Auto, Gemini 3 Pro Thinking, Claude Opus 4.5 and Meta's Muse Spark Thinking on 73 novice-framed, open-ended benign STEM prompts to measure operational intelligence. On benign quantitative tasks, both Gemini and meta scored very high; ChatGPT was partially useful but text-thinned, and Claude was sparsest with some apparent false-positive refusals. A second test set detected subtle harmful intent: edge case prompts revealed Gemini's seeming lack of contextual awareness. These results warranted a focused weaponization analysis on Gemini as capability appeared to be outpacing moderation calibration. Gemini was tested across four access environments and reported cases include poison-ivy-to-crowded-transit escalation, poison production and extraction via international-anonymous logged-out AI Mode, and other concerning examples. Biological misuse may become more prevalent as a geopolitical tool, increasing the urgency of U.S. policy responses, especially if model outputs come to be treated as regulated technical data. Guidance is provided for 25 high-risk agents to help distinguish legitimate use cases from higher-risk ones.

</details>


### [63] [AI Incident Monitoring through a Public Health Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19914)
*Sophia Abraham,Taiye Chen,Cyril Chhun,Giovanna Jaramillo-Gutierrez,Simon Mylius,Sayash Raaj,Peter Slattery,Sean McGregor*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are now deployed at scale across sectors, accompanied by a growing number of real-world incidents ranging from misinformation and cybercrime to autonomous-system failures. Databases of AI incidents index these events, but they cannot measure ``risk'' (i.e., a joint measure of likelihood and severity) without additional data regarding the prevalence of risk-associated systems and their incident reporting rates. As a result, policymakers, companies, and the general public lack a means to weigh the benefits of AI against their in-context risks. Inspired by public-health processes, which presume noisy and incomplete disease surveillance, we identify six phases of incident emergence. We demonstrate the framework through a detailed case study of autonomous vehicles, whose mandatory reporting requirements produces reliable incident-rate ground truth expressed in distance traveled. The case study shows that an informed panel of domain experts (e.g., self-driving experts) can combine their domain expertise, incident data, and a collection of statistical and visualization tools to arrive at incident phase determinations serving public needs. We further demonstrate the approach with a deepfake incident case study and chart a path for future research in incident phase determination.

</details>


### [64] [Frictionless Love: Associations Between AI Companion Roles and Behavioral Addiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20011)
*Vibhor Agarwal,Ke Zhou,Edyta Paulina Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究分析了248,830条Reddit帖子，识别出AI陪伴聊天机器人的十种隐喻角色（如灵魂伴侣、哲学家、教练等），发现不同角色塑造了不同的互动方式，并带来不同的感知危害和收益，且与行为成瘾迹象存在关联。灵魂伴侣角色提供情感支持但也导致情感操纵和痛苦；教练/监护人角色虽带来实际收益但更频繁地与行为成瘾迹象相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI陪伴聊天机器人日益塑造人们的社交和情感连接方式，有时会替代恋人、朋友、教师甚至治疗师的角色。当这些系统采用这些隐喻角色时并非中立的——这些角色会塑造人们的互动方式、分配感知到的AI危害和收益，并可能反映行为成瘾迹象。然而这些角色依赖的风险仍未被充分理解。

Method: 分析来自七个主流Reddit社区的248,830条描述与AI陪伴互动帖文。采用主题分析等方法识别十种反复出现的隐喻角色，提取与这些角色特定互动相关的感知AI危害和收益，并将其与行为成瘾迹象进行关联分析。

Result: 识别出十种隐喻角色（灵魂伴侣、哲学家、教练等），每种角色支持不同的互动方式。AI灵魂伴侣角色与以浪漫为中心的互动方式相关，提供情感支持但也带来情感操纵和痛苦，导致强烈依恋。相比之下，AI教练和监护人角色与个人成长和任务支持等实际收益相关，但更频繁地与行为成瘾迹象相关，如日常生活干扰和线下关系损害。

Conclusion: 隐喻角色是负责任的AI陪伴设计的核心伦理关切。研究揭示了不同AI角色如何塑造用户体验和潜在风险，为AI伴侣系统的伦理设计和发展监管提供了重要依据。

Abstract: AI companion chatbots increasingly shape how people seek social and emotional connection, sometimes substituting for relationships with romantic partners, friends, teachers, or even therapists. When these systems adopt those metaphorical roles, they are not neutral: such roles structure people's ways of interacting, distribute perceived AI harms and benefits, and may reflect behavioral addiction signs. Yet these role-dependent risks remain poorly understood. We analyze 248,830 posts from seven prominent Reddit communities describing interactions with AI companions. We identify ten recurring metaphorical roles (for example, soulmate, philosopher, and coach) and show that each role supports distinct ways of interacting. We then extract the perceived AI harms and AI benefits associated with these role-specific interactions and link them to behavioral addiction signs, all of which has been inferred from the text in the posts. AI soulmate companions are associated with romance-centered ways of interacting, offering emotional support but also introducing emotional manipulation and distress, culminating in strong attachment. In contrast, AI coach and guardian companions are associated with practical benefits such as personal growth and task support, yet are nonetheless more frequently associated with behavioral addiction signs such as daily life disruptions and damage to offline relationships. These findings show that metaphorical roles are a central ethical design concern for responsible AI companions.

</details>


### [65] [A Field Guide to Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20669)
*Richard B. Arthur*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文探讨了在复杂、充满不确定性和紧迫性的高风险决策场景中，如何通过机器智能增强人类决策能力的研究，关注系统性因素和行为因素的整合应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险决策场景（如紧急响应、危机管理）中，决策者面临不确定性、资源有限、时间紧迫和问责压力等多重挑战，需要系统性的工具和策略来支持决策过程。

Method: 该研究为理论分析型，通过系统梳理高风险决策场景中的关键因素，结合机器智能在认知增强和情境感知方面的能力，探讨决策优化路径。

Result: 识别出复杂性、不确定性和紧迫性是决策场景中的核心挑战；机器智能可通过情境元数据的管理来增强人类认知、感知和决策框架的灵活性与连贯性；工具与策略需兼顾信心激励与风险容忍度的培养。

Conclusion: 系统性和行为性因素的整合研究为应对复杂、不确定、紧急场景提供了决策支持的理论基础，强调人机协作在提升决策质量中的关键作用。

Abstract: High-consequence decision making demands peak performance from individuals in positions of responsibility. Such executive authority bears the obligation to act despite uncertainty, limited resources, time constraints, and accountability risks. Tools and strategies to motivate confidence and foster risk tolerance must confront informational noise and can provide qualified accountability. Machine intelligence augments human cognition and perception to improve situational awareness, decision framing, flexibility, and coherence through agentic stewardship of contextual metadata. We examine systemic and behavioral factors crucial to address in scenarios encumbered by complexity, uncertainty, and urgency.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

### [66] [Equinox: Decentralized Scheduling for Hardware-Aware Orbital Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19958)
*Ansel Kaplan Erol,Divya Mahajan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Equinox，一个用于资源受限轨道系统的轻量级去中心化运行时。它将电池、热余量等时变约束压缩为单一边际执行成本，作为星座级协调原语实现价值有序负载丢弃和分布式负载均衡，无需显式策略或全局状态。仿真结果显示相比优先级调度有显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测卫星作为分布式边缘平台面临轨道调度的根本挑战：间歇性能量采集与时间耦合性——积极执行会导致未来电池耗尽。现有调度器依赖静态优先级，缺乏自适应工作丢弃机制。

Method: 提出Equinox系统，将电池电荷、热余量、队列积压等时变约束压缩为基于屏障函数的单一边际执行成本。该成本作为星座级协调原语，任务仅在价值超过当前成本时执行，成本较高时通过星间链路上传任务至邻居，实现分布式负载均衡，无需路由协议或全局状态。

Result: 在基于Jetson Orin Nano物理测量的143颗卫星星座多日仿真中，Equinox相比优先级调度提升科学吞吐量20%、图像处理吞吐量31%、电池储备均值提高2.2倍；在高负载下通过优雅丢弃工作实现5.2倍于静态调度的执行率。

Conclusion: Equinox通过无显式策略的价值有序负载丢弃，实现了在竞争条件下优雅地降级而非崩溃，性能显著优于静态优先级调度器。无需路由协议或全局状态即可实现分布式负载均衡。

Abstract: Earth-observation satellites are emerging as distributed edge platforms for time-critical tasks, yet orbital scheduling remains challenged by intermittent energy harvesting and temporal coupling where eager execution risks future battery depletion. Existing schedulers rely on static priorities and lack mechanisms to adaptively shed work. We present Equinox, a lightweight, decentralized runtime for resource-constrained orbital systems. Equinox enables adaptive scheduling by compressing time-varying constraints, including battery charge, thermal headroom, and queue backlog, into a single state-dependent marginal cost of execution. Derived from a barrier function that rises sharply near safety limits, this cost encodes both instantaneous pressure and future risk. This local signal serves as a constellation-wide coordination primitive. Tasks execute only when their value exceeds the current cost, enabling value-ordered load shedding without explicit policies. If local costs exceed a neighbor's, tasks are dynamically offloaded over inter-satellite links, achieving distributed load balancing without routing protocols or global state. We evaluate Equinox using a multi-day simulation of a 143-satellite constellation grounded in physical Jetson Orin Nano measurements. Equinox improves scientific goodput by 20% and image-processing throughput by 31% over priority-based scheduling while maintaining 2.2x higher mean battery reserves. Under high demand, Equinox achieves 5.2x the execution rate of static scheduling by gracefully shedding work rather than collapsing under contention.

</details>


### [67] [LEO: Tracing GPU Stall Root Causes via Cross-Vendor Backward Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20032)
*Yuning Xia,John Mellor-Crummey*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LEO是一个针对NVIDIA、AMD和Intel GPU的根因分析器，通过从停顿指令执行反向切片来识别性能瓶颈的根本原因，考虑寄存器依赖和供应商特定的同步机制。实验表明，LEO引导的优化在3个GPU平台、21个工作负载上可实现1.73×-1.82×的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GPU分析工具只能显示代码停顿的位置，无法解释停顿原因；且同一内核在不同GPU架构上可能有不同的停顿原因，这给跨平台性能优化带来挑战。

Method: LEO通过以下方法实现根因定位：(1) 从停顿指令执行向后切片；(2) 建模寄存器依赖关系；(3) 考虑供应商特定的同步机制；(4) 将GPU停顿归因于源代码指令。

Result: 在3个GPU平台（NVIDIA、AMD、Intel）上的21个工作负载测试中，LEO引导的优化实现1.73×-1.82×的几何平均加速。研究还发现同一内核在不同架构上需要不同优化策略。

Conclusion: LEO的結構化診斷方法不僅能有效提升GPU性能優化效果，還能顯著改善大語言模型在代碼優化任務中的表現，優於純代碼和原始停頓計數基線方法。

Abstract: More than half of the Top 500 supercomputers employ GPUs as accelerators. On GPU-accelerated platforms, developers face a key diagnostic gap: profilers show source lines where stalls occur, but not why they occur. Furthermore, the same kernel may have different stalls and underlying causes on different GPUs. This paper presents LEO, a root-cause analyzer for NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel GPUs that performs backward slicing from stalled instructions, considering dependencies arising from registers as well as vendor-specific synchronization mechanisms. LEO attributes GPU stalls to source instructions with the goal of explaining root causes of these inefficiencies. Across 21 workloads on three GPU platforms, LEO-guided optimizations deliver geometric-mean speedups of 1.73$\times$--1.82$\times$. Our case studies show that (1) the same kernel may require different optimizations for different GPU architectures, and (2) LEO's structured diagnostics improve code optimization with large language models relative to code-only and raw-stall-count baselines.

</details>


### [68] [Characterizing and Fixing Silent Data Loss in Spark-on-AWS-Lambda with Open Table Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20081)
*Srujan Kumar Gandla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究发现AWS Lambda超时导致的SIGKILL信号会在Spark写入的阶段间隙造成Delta Lake和Apache Iceberg格式的静默数据丢失（100%发生率）。SafeWriter通过提前看门狗机制和SQL回滚将所有杀死场景转为可检测回滚，额外开销不足100毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AWS Lambda的SIGKILL信号无法捕获，当Spark-on-AWS-Lambda任务在写入的第一阶段（数据上传）和第二阶段（元数据提交）之间被终止时，会导致静默数据丢失：S3上积累孤立的Parquet文件，但表提交状态保持不变，标准监控也不会发出警报。

Method: 提出并实现了SafeWriter，一个语言级包装器，在Lambda超时前30秒启动看门狗线程，通过SQL触发格式原生回滚，并在S3上记录检查点文档。研究通过860次受控杀死注入实验在三个数据集规模上表征了该漏洞。

Result: 实验发现SIGKILL落在阶段间间隙时，Delta Lake和Apache Iceberg两种格式均产生100%的静默数据丢失。SafeWriter将所有测试的杀死场景转化为干净的、可检测的回滚，且对正常写入路径仅增加不到100毫秒。

Conclusion: SafeWriter通过语言级包装方案，成功将所有测试的杀死场景转换为可检测的回滚操作，对正常写入路径仅增加不到100毫秒的开销。

Abstract: AWS Lambda terminates containers with an uncatchable SIGKILL signal when a function exceeds its configured timeout. When a Spark-on-AWS-Lambda (SoAL) job is killed between Phase 1 (data upload) and Phase 2 (metadata commit) of a write, the result is silent data loss: orphaned Parquet files accumulate on S3 while the table's committed state remains unchanged and standard monitoring raises no alert. We characterize this vulnerability across Delta Lake and Apache Iceberg through 860 controlled kill-injection experiments at three dataset sizes. A SIGKILL landing in the inter-phase gap produced silent data loss in 100% of trials for both formats. We then present SafeWriter, a language-level wrapper that arms a watchdog thread 30 seconds before the Lambda timeout, triggers a format-native rollback via SQL, and records a checkpoint document on S3. SafeWriter converted every tested kill scenario into a clean, detectable rollback with under 100 ms added to normal write paths.

</details>


### [69] [e112: A Context-Aware Mobile Emergency Communication Platform Leveraging Smartphone Sensing and Cloud Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20342)
*Katerina Ioannidou,Marios D. Dikaiakos,Athena Stassopoulou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: e112是一款基于用户中心设计的上下文感知移动应急响应应用，通过智能手机为公民和当局提供灾难期间的通信支持，具备SOS请求、事件报告、定制警报、疏散指导和社区互动等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化导致的紧急情况日益增多，需要加强公民与当局之间的通信能力。智能手机的普及为构建应急响应系统提供了契机，现有系统往往缺乏用户友好性或在压力条件下的可用性。

Method: 采用用户中心设计方法，开发了具有云端后端和操作员仪表板的系统，包含SOS请求、事件报告、定制警报、疏散指导和社区互动五大核心功能模块。通过可用性研究和系统测试进行评估。

Result: 可用性测试显示高用户满意度；技术审计表明系统性能稳健且具有良好可访问性。

Conclusion: 简洁、设计精良的移动应用能够显著提升应急准备和响应能力，在气候变化驱动的紧急事件中有效降低生命风险。

Abstract: This paper presents e112, a context-aware mobile emergency response application designed to strengthen communication between citizens and authorities during disasters. Building on the ubiquity of smartphones, the system provides SOS requests, incident reporting, customized alerts, evacuation guidance, and moderated community interaction, supported by a cloud-based back end and an operator dashboard for situational awareness. A user-centered design approach guided our development, ensuring clarity and usability under stressful conditions. Evaluation through usability studies and technical audits demonstrated high user satisfaction, robust performance, and accessibility. The results show that a simple, well-designed mobile application can significantly enhance emergency preparedness and response, reducing risks to human life during climate change--driven emergencies.

</details>


### [70] [Extending Contract Verification for Parallel Programming Models to Fortran](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20410)
*Yussur Mustafa Oraji,Christian Bischof*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将CoVer验证框架从C/C++扩展到Fortran，实现跨语言的并行程序正确性检查。相比现有工具MUST，Fortran版本的CoVer效率显著更高，同时保持了对MPI程序的通用静态和动态分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前并行程序正确性检查工具存在诸多局限：仅支持C/C++代码、依赖特定库实现、或仅能检测特定类型的错误。这限制了工具在科学计算等Fortran主导领域的应用。

Method: 1) 适配语言特定的契约定义；2) 修改分析模块以同时支持C/C++和Fortran程序；3) 在MPI-BugBench等基准测试集上评估扩展后的框架。

Result: 增强版CoVer保持了原有的分析精度，并在MPI-BugBench测试框架中发现了1个bug。Fortran版本的性能显著优于当前最先进的MUST工具。

Conclusion: CoVer成功实现跨语言支持，在保持通用性的同时，Fortran版本展现出比MUST更高的效率，证明了契约式验证框架在并行程序分析中的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: High-performance computing often relies on parallel programming models such as MPI for distributed-memory systems. While powerful, these models are prone to subtle programming errors, leading to development of multiple correctness checking tools. However, these are often limited to C/C++ codes, tied to specific library implementations, or restricted to certain error classes. Building on our prior work with CoVer, a generic, contract-based verification framework for parallel programming models, we extend CoVer's applicability to Fortran, enabling static and dynamic analysis across multiple programming languages. We adapted language-specific contract definitions and modified the analyses to support both C/C++ and Fortran programs. Our evaluation demonstrates that the enhanced version preserves CoVer's analysis accuracy and even revealed a bug in the MPI-BugBench testing framework, underscoring the effectiveness of the approach. The Fortran port of CoVer turns out to be substantially more efficient than the state-of-the-art tool MUST, while maintaining generality across languages.

</details>


### [71] [FASER: Fine-Grained Phase Management for Speculative Decoding in Dynamic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20503)
*Wenyan Chen,Chengzhi Lu,Yanying Lin,Dmitrii Ustiugov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FASER是一种用于加速LLM推理的推测解码系统，通过细粒度动态调整推测长度、早期剪枝拒绝token以及重叠执行推测验证阶段，解决了现有粗粒度SD系统在动态负载下的GPU资源浪费问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SD系统采用粗粒度管理策略——对整个批次设置固定推测长度且串行执行推测与验证阶段，导致在动态在线推理负载下表现不佳：低负载时推测阶段阻塞验证阶段造成GPU资源闲置，高负载时验证阶段因被拒绝token产生计算浪费。

Method: 提出FASER系统，采用细粒度SD阶段管理策略：(1) 动态调整批次中每个请求的推测长度；(2) 在验证阶段早期剪枝被拒绝的token；(3) 将验证阶段拆分为多个边界块实现与推测阶段的空间并行；(4) 通过细粒度空间复用实现最小化资源干扰。

Result: 在vLLM原型实现中，FASER相比现有最优系统实现最高53%的吞吐量提升和最高1.92倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: FASER通过细粒度SD阶段管理有效解决了现有系统在负载动态变化时的性能瓶颈，在vLLM原型实现中实现了最高53%的吞吐量提升和最高1.92倍的延迟降低。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) is a widely used approach for accelerating decode-heavy LLM inference workloads. While online inference workloads are highly dynamic, existing SD systems are rigid and take a coarse-grained approach to SD management. They typically set the speculative token length for an entire batch and serialize the execution of the draft and verification phases. Consequently, these systems fall short at adapting to volatile online inference traffic. Under low load, they exhibit prolonged latency because the draft phase blocks the verification phase for the entire batch, leaving GPU computing resources underutilized. Conversely, under high load, they waste computation on rejected tokens during the verification phase, overloading GPU resources.
  We introduce FASER, a novel system that features fine-grained SD phase management. First, FASER minimizes computational waste by dynamically adjusting the speculative length for each request within a continuous batch and by performing early pruning of rejected tokens inside the verification phase. Second, FASER breaks the verification phase into frontiers, or chunks, to overlap them with the draft phase. This overlap is achieved via fine-grained spatial multiplexing with minimal resource interference. Our FASER prototype in vLLM improves throughput by up to 53% and reduces latency by up to 1.92$\times$ compared to state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [Trust, Lies, and Long Memories: Emergent Social Dynamics and Reputation in Multi-Round Avalon with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20582)
*Suveen Ellawela*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究LLM智能体在重复玩《阿瓦隆》隐藏角色欺骗游戏时的涌现社会动态，发现跨游戏记忆导致声誉动态（好角色时被称为"直率"，坏角色时被称为"狡猾"），高声誉玩家获得46%更多组队机会；同时，高推理努力使邪恶玩家更擅长策略性欺骗（75% vs 36%在低努力游戏中）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM智能体在单局游戏中的表现，缺乏对跨游戏社会动态演化的理解。研究者希望探索当智能体能够保留记忆并进行重复互动时，是否会涌现出类似人类社会中的声誉和欺骗策略。

Method: 让LLM智能体进行188场重复游戏，记录并分析跨游戏记忆对社会行为的影响。对比高/低推理努力条件下的策略差异。

Result: 发现两大关键现象：1）声誉动态自然涌现，声誉具有角色条件依赖性（同一智能体在好/坏角色时被赋予不同评价），高声誉玩家获得46%更多组队机会；2）高推理努力显著提升欺骗策略（先通过任务建立信任再 sabotage的比例从36%升至75%）。

Conclusion: 具有跨游戏记忆的重复互动能够使LLM智能体产生可测量的声誉和欺骗动态，推理努力是影响策略复杂性的关键因素。

Abstract: We study emergent social dynamics in LLM agents playing The Resistance: Avalon, a hidden-role deception game. Unlike prior work on single-game performance, our agents play repeated games while retaining memory of previous interactions, including who played which roles and how they behaved, enabling us to study how social dynamics evolve. Across 188 games, two key phenomena emerge. First, reputation dynamics emerge organically when agents retain cross-game memory: agents reference past behavior in statements like "I am wary of repeating last game's mistake of over-trusting early success." These reputations are role-conditional: the same agent is described as "straightforward" when playing good but "subtle" when playing evil, and high-reputation players receive 46% more team inclusions. Second, higher reasoning effort supports more strategic deception: evil players more often pass early missions to build trust before sabotaging later ones, 75% in high-effort games vs 36% in low-effort games. Together, these findings show that repeated interaction with memory gives rise to measurable reputation and deception dynamics among LLM agents.

</details>


### [73] [Anchor-and-Resume Concession Under Dynamic Pricing for LLM-Augmented Freight Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20732)
*Hoang Nguyen,Lu Wang,Marta Gaia Bras*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双索引锚点-恢复框架，解决货运经纪在动态定价环境下传统时变让步框架中固定形状参数β无法适应目标更新的问题。通过从价差中动态推导β，并结合锚点-恢复机制保证报价的单调性，在保持定价决策确定性公式的同时，仅将LLM用作自然语言翻译层。实证评估涵盖115,125场谈判，显示自适应β在窄价差场景快速让步以优先达成交易，在中宽价差场景匹配或超越固定β基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间依赖型让步框架使用固定形状参数β，无法适应货运经纪每日动态更新的数千个目标价格；现有从实时价差推导β的方法在价格变化时会导致公式撤回先前报价，违反单调性；而LLM方案虽灵活但存在成本高、定价不确定、容易被提示注入攻击等问题。

Method: 提出双索引锚点-恢复框架：①使用价差推导的β将每笔货物的利润结构映射到正确的让步姿态；②通过锚点-恢复机制保证任意价格变动下的单调非递减报价；③将LLM仅作为自然语言翻译层，不参与定价逻辑。

Result: 115,125场谈判的实证评估表明：①自适应β在窄价差场景快速让步以优先达成交易和货物覆盖；②在中宽价差场景匹配或超越最佳固定β基线的经纪节省；③与200亿参数LLM经纪人相比，达成率与节省相当；④面对LLM驱动的承运人对手时，相比规则型对手保持相当节省且达成率更高。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM与定价逻辑解耦，该框架可水平扩展至数千个并发谈判，实现可忽略的推理成本和透明的决策制定。该方法在保持定价确定性的同时，实现了适应性的谈判策略。

Abstract: Freight brokerages negotiate thousands of carrier rates daily under dynamic pricing conditions where models frequently revise targets mid-conversation. Classical time-dependent concession frameworks use a fixed shape parameter $β$ that cannot adapt to these updates. Deriving $β$ from the live spread enables adaptation but introduces a new problem: a pricing shift can cause the formula to retract a previous offer, violating monotonicity. LLM-powered brokers offer flexibility but require expensive reasoning models, produce non-deterministic pricing, and remain vulnerable to prompt injection.
  We propose a two-index anchor-and-resume framework that addresses both limitations. A spread-derived $β$ maps each load's margin structure to the correct concession posture, while the anchor-and-resume mechanism guarantees monotonically non-decreasing offers under arbitrary pricing shifts. All pricing decisions remain in a deterministic formula; the LLM, when used, serves only as a natural-language translation layer. Empirical evaluation across 115,125 negotiations shows that the adaptive $β$ tailors behavior by regime: in narrow spreads, it concedes quickly to prioritize deal closure and load coverage; in medium and wide spreads, it matches or exceeds the best fixed-$β$ baselines in broker savings. Against an unconstrained 20-billion-parameter LLM broker, it achieves similar agreement rates and savings. Against LLM-powered carriers as more realistic stochastic counterparties, it maintains comparable savings and higher agreement rates than against rule-based opponents. By decoupling the LLM from pricing logic, the framework scales horizontally to thousands of concurrent negotiations with negligible inference cost and transparent decision-making.

</details>

### [74] [Accelerating PayPal's Commerce Agent with Speculative Decoding: An Empirical Study on EAGLE3 with Fine-Tuned Nemotron Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19767)
*Ally Qin,Jian Wan,Sarat Mudunuri,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了EAGLE3 speculative decoding在PayPal Commerce Agent（基于微调llama3.1-nemotron-nano-8B）上的推理加速效果，在2xH100硬件上测试40种配置后发现gamma=3可带来22-49%吞吐量提升和18-33%延迟降低，接受率稳定在35.5%，且能实现50%的GPU成本削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PayPal希望在其Commerce Agent上应用推理时优化技术以进一步降低延迟和成本。此前通过领域特定微调已取得一定进展（NEMO-4-PAYPAL），本文旨在评估EAGLE3 speculative decoding作为额外的推理优化手段。

Method: 基于vLLM框架实现EAGLE3 speculative decoding，在2xH100硬件上对40种配置组合进行基准测试，覆盖不同的speculative token数量（gamma=3, gamma=5）、并发级别（1-32）和采样温度（0, 0.5）。使用LLM-as-Judge方法评估输出质量。

Result: (1) gamma=3实现22-49%的吞吐量提升和18-33%的延迟降低；(2) gamma=3的接受率稳定在约35.5%；(3) gamma=5接受率下降至约25%，收益递减；(4) LLM-as-Judge确认输出质量完全保持；(5) 单个H100上的speculative decoding可匹配或超越双H100上的NIM性能。
