Across studies on Intel microprocessor stock keeping unit (SKU) life cycles and the platform-mediated adoption of open large language model repositories, CDLF delivers more accurate point forecasts and higher-quality probabilistic forecasts than classical diffusion models, Bayesian updating approaches, and other state-of-the-art machine-learning baselines.

</details>


### [111] [Towards Event-Aware Forecasting in DeFi: Insights from On-chain Automated Market Maker Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20374)
*Huaiyu Jia,Jiehshun You,Yizhi Luo,Jingyu Liu,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含890万条链上事件记录的AMM数据集，并提出UWM损失函数用于事件感知预测，在8个先进TPP架构上实现时间预测误差平均降低56.41%，同时保持事件类型预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍忽视AMM层面的微观结构动态，缺乏覆盖多协议且具有细粒度事件分类的综合数据集，以及有效的事件感知建模框架。AMM价格动态由改变储备金的链上事件（如swap）触发，而非对链外信息的连续响应，这使得事件级分析对理解AMM价格形成机制至关重要。

Method: 1) 构建数据集：收集Pendle、Uniswap v3、Aave、Morpho四个代表性AMM协议的890万条链上事件记录，包含精确的交易类型标注和区块高度时间戳；2) 提出UWM损失函数：将区块间隔回归项通过同方差不确定性加权融入传统时间点过程(TPP)目标函数；3) 在8个先进TPP架构上进行广泛实验验证。

Result: UWM损失函数在保持事件类型预测准确性的同时，将时间预测误差平均降低56.41%，为AMM生态系统中的事件感知预测建立了稳健基准。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了建模链上价格发现离散性和事件驱动特征的必要数据基础和方法论框架。所有数据集和源代码已在GitHub公开，为后续研究提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Automated Market Makers (AMMs), as a core infrastructure of decentralized finance (DeFi), uniquely drive on-chain asset pricing through a deterministic reserve ratio mechanism. Unlike traditional markets, AMM price dynamics is triggered largely by on-chain events (e.g., swap) that change the reserve ratio, rather than by continuous responses to off-chain information. This makes event-level analysis crucial for understanding price formation mechanisms in AMMs. However, existing research generally neglects the micro-structural dynamics at the AMMs level, lacking both a comprehensive dataset covering multiple protocols with fine-grained event classification and an effective framework for event-aware modeling. To fill this gap, we construct a dataset containing 8.9 million on-chain event records from four representative AMMs protocols: Pendle, Uniswap v3, Aave and Morpho, with precise annotations of transaction type and block height timestamps. Furthermore, we propose an Uncertainty Weighted Mean Squared Error (UWM) loss function, which incorporates the block interval regression term into the traditional Time-Point Process (TPP) objective function by weighting the uncertainty with homoscedasticity. Extensive experiments on eight advanced TPP architectures demonstrate that this loss function reduces the time prediction error by an average of 56.41\% while maintaining the accuracy of event type prediction, establishing a robust benchmark for event-aware prediction in the AMMs ecosystem. This work provides the necessary data foundation and methodological framework for modeling the discreteness and event-driven characteristics of on-chain price discovery. All datasets and source code are publicly available. https://github.com/yosen-king/Deep-AMM-Events

</details>


### [112] [Calibrating conditional risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20409)
*Andrey Vasilyev,Yikai Wang,Xiaocheng Li,Guanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We introduce and study the problem of calibrating conditional risk, which involves estimating the expected loss of a prediction model conditional on input features. We analyze this problem in both classification and regression settings and show that it is fundamentally equivalent to a standard regression task. For classification settings, we further establish a connection between conditional risk calibration and individual/conditional probability calibration, and develop theoretical insights for the performance metric. This reveals that while conditional risk calibration is related to existing uncertainty quantification problems, it remains a distinct and standalone machine learning problem. Empirically, we validate our theoretical findings and demonstrate the practical implications of conditional risk calibration in the learning to defer (L2D) framework. Our systematic experiments provide both qualitative and quantitative assessments, offering guidance for future research in uncertainty-aware decision-making.

</details>


### [113] [Scalable AI Inference: Performance Analysis and Optimization of AI Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20420)
*Hung Cuong Pham,Fatih Gedikli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于BentoML构建AI推理系统，采用预训练RoBERTa情感分析模型，通过gamma和指数分布模拟真实负载场景，在单节点K3s集群上评估延迟、吞吐量等性能指标，并从运行时、服务和部署多个层级进行优化，最终通过统计分析验证优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI研究普遍聚焦于模型设计与算法性能，而部署与推理环节虽对实际应用至关重要却长期被忽视。本研究填补这一研究空白，针对可扩展AI模型服务的性能与优化问题展开深入研究。

Method: 以预训练RoBERTa情感分析模型为测试对象，在三种真实负载场景下建立性能基线；采用gamma分布和指数分布模拟稳态、突发性和高强度工作负载；通过单节点K3s集群进行部署；从运行时、服务和部署多个层级实施优化策略。

Result: 在K3s集群环境下，优化的BentoML推理系统在延迟百分位数、吞吐量等关键指标上显著优于基线；系统展现出良好的可扩展性和弹性。

Conclusion: BentoML可实现高效、可扩展的AI推理服务；多层级优化策略对降低响应时间具有显著效果；单节点K3s集群部署能够有效提升系统在干扰条件下的韧性。

Abstract: AI research often emphasizes model design and algorithmic performance, while deployment and inference remain comparatively underexplored despite being critical for real-world use. This study addresses that gap by investigating the performance and optimization of a BentoML-based AI inference system for scalable model serving developed in collaboration with graphworks.ai. The evaluation first establishes baseline performance under three realistic workload scenarios. To ensure a fair and reproducible assessment, a pre-trained RoBERTa sentiment analysis model is used throughout the experiments. The system is subjected to traffic patterns following gamma and exponential distributions in order to emulate real-world usage conditions, including steady, bursty, and high-intensity workloads. Key performance metrics, such as latency percentiles and throughput, are collected and analyzed to identify bottlenecks in the inference pipeline. Based on the baseline results, optimization strategies are introduced at multiple levels of the serving stack to improve efficiency and scalability. The optimized system is then reevaluated under the same workload conditions, and the results are compared with the baseline using statistical analysis to quantify the impact of the applied improvements. The findings demonstrate practical strategies for achieving efficient and scalable AI inference with BentoML. The study examines how latency and throughput scale under varying workloads, how optimizations at the runtime, service, and deployment levels affect response time, and how deployment in a single-node K3s cluster influences resilience during disruptions.

</details>


### [114] [Unlocking the Forecasting Economy: A Suite of Datasets for the Full Lifecycle of Prediction Market: [Experiments \& Analysis]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20421)
*Huaiyu Jia,Luofeng Zhou,Wentao Zhang,Lin William Cong,Siguang Li,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对去中心化预测市场完整生命周期的持续维护数据集，基于Polymarket构建，通过统一关系数据系统整合市场元数据、交易记录和预言机事件等多源数据，总规模超过77万市场记录、9.43亿成交记录和近200万预言机事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化预测市场（如Polymarket）的市场生命周期涉及创建、代币注册、交易、预言机交互、争议和最终结算等多个阶段，但相应的数据分散在异构的链下和链上来源，缺乏统一整合的数据集。

Method: 研究团队构建了一个统一的关系数据系统，整合三个规范层：市场元数据、成交级交易记录和预言机解决事件，采用标识符解析、链上恢复和增量更新等技术，处理大规模跨源整合、不完整链接和持续同步的挑战。

Result: 最终数据集涵盖2020年10月至2026年3月，包含超过77万个市场记录、超过9.43亿条成交记录和近200万个预言机事件。

Conclusion: 该数据集通过描述性分析和两个下游案例研究（NBA结果校准和CPI预期重建）证明了其实用价值，为去中心化预测市场研究提供了可重复和可扩展的数据基础设施。

Abstract: Prediction markets are markets for trading claims on future events, such as presidential elections, and their prices provide continuously updated signals of collective beliefs. In decentralized platforms such as Polymarket, the market lifecycle spans market creation, token registration, trading, oracle interaction, dispute, and final settlement, yet the corresponding data are fragmented across heterogeneous off-chain and on-chain sources. We present the first continuously maintained dataset suite for the full lifecycle of decentralized prediction markets, built on Polymarket. To address the challenges of large-scale cross-source integration, incomplete linkage, and continuous synchronization, we build a unified relational data system that integrates three canonical layers: market metadata, fill-level trading records, and oracle-resolution events, through identifier resolution, on-chain recovery, and incremental updates. The resulting dataset spans October 2020 to March 2026 and comprises more than 770 thousand market records, over 943 million fill records, and nearly 2 million oracle events. We describe the data model, collection pipeline, and consistency mechanisms that make the dataset reproducible and extensible, and we demonstrate its utility through descriptive analyses of market activity and two downstream case studies: NBA outcome calibration and CPI expectation reconstruction.

</details>


### [115] [Surrogate Functionals for Machine-Learned Orbital-Free Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20458)
*Roman Remme,Fred A. Hamprecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出替代泛函（surrogate functionals）：一种用于轨道无关密度泛函理论（OF-DFT）的机器学习能量泛函，其训练只需基态密度，无需能量或基态外的梯度信息。通过梯度下降改进损失函数保证密度指数收敛到基态，结合自适应采样策略聚焦于推理时的优化轨迹。在QM9和QM mugs基准上达到或超越现有监督学习方法精度，同时消除了传统方法必需的O(N³)正交化步骤，提升了大系统计算的运行时间标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轨道无关密度泛函理论（OF-DFT）依赖对物理参考的普适保真度来定义能量泛函，而现有监督学习方法需要大量基态外的数据（如能量或梯度信息），计算成本高且训练复杂。

Method: 提出替代泛函方法：（1）通过梯度下降改进损失函数（gradient-descent-improvement loss）确保密度指数收敛至基态；（2）采用自适应采样策略，聚焦于推理过程中实际访问的优化轨迹区域进行学习；（3）仅使用基态密度作为训练数据，无需基态外的能量或梯度信息。

Result: 在QM9和QMugs基准数据集上，替代泛函的密度误差与现有最先进的全监督机器学习OF-DFT方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: 替代泛函方法成功消除了先前工作中必需的O(N³)正交化步骤，显著改善了大规模系统的计算效率和时间标度，为OF-DFT的机器学习方法开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce surrogate functionals: machine-learned energy functionals for orbital-free density functional theory (OF-DFT) which are defined not by universal fidelity to a physical reference, but merely by the requirement that density optimization with a fixed procedure yields the true ground-state density. Helpfully, training surrogate functionals requires only ground-state densities, no energies or gradients away from the ground state. We here propose a gradient-descent-improvement loss that guarantees exponential convergence of the density to the ground state, and combine it with an adaptive sampling scheme that concentrates learning around the optimization trajectories actually visited during inference. On the QM9 and QMugs benchmarks, surrogate functionals achieve density errors competitive with or improving upon the state of the art for fully supervised machine-learned OF-DFT, while eliminating the need for the $O(N^3)$ orthononormalization step required by prior work, yielding improved runtime scaling for larger systems.

</details>


### [116] [Efficient Test-Time Inference via Deterministic Exploration of Truncated Decoding Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20500)
*Xueyan Li,Johannes Zenn,Ekaterina Fadeeva,Guinan Su,Mrinmaya Sachan,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DLE是一种确定性解码方法，通过系统枚举解码树的不同叶子节点而非有放回采样，提升了推理效率。该方法在数学、编程和通用推理任务上均优于随机自洽方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自洽推理方法使用有放回采样，效率低下。在受限领域（如数学和代码）中，反复访问相同的高概率前缀和重复完成，浪费计算资源。

Method: 将截断采样视为修剪解码树的遍历，提出确定性方法Distinct Leaf Enumeration (DLE)，系统枚举不同叶子节点而非有放回采样。

Result: DLE在固定预算下覆盖更多截断搜索空间，探索了之前未访问的高概率分支，减少了冗余token生成。

Conclusion: DLE在数学、编程和通用推理任务上均优于随机自洽方法，有效提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Self-consistency boosts inference-time performance by sampling multiple reasoning traces in parallel and voting. However, in constrained domains like math and code, this strategy is compute-inefficient because it samples with replacement, repeatedly revisiting the same high-probability prefixes and duplicate completions. We propose Distinct Leaf Enumeration (DLE), a deterministic decoding method that treats truncated sampling as traversal of a pruned decoding tree and systematically enumerates distinct leaves instead of sampling with replacement. This strategy improves inference efficiency in two ways. Algorithmically, it increases coverage of the truncated search space under a fixed budget by exploring previously unvisited high-probability branches. Systemically, it reuses shared prefixes and reduces redundant token generation. Empirically, DLE explores higher-quality reasoning traces than stochastic self-consistency, yielding better performance on math, coding, and general reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [117] [Amortized Vine Copulas for High-Dimensional Density and Information Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20568)
*Houman Safaai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Vine Denoising Copula (VDC)，一种 amortized vine-copula框架，通过训练单一二元去噪模型并复用于所有vine边，实现高效的高维依赖建模。VDC结合IPFP/Sinkhorn投影保持精确的vine似然和copula可解释性，同时用GPU推理替代逐边优化。在合成数据和真实数据上，VDC展现出强竞争力的密度估计性能，并大幅加速高维vine拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统vine-copula方法虽具可解释性但计算成本高，而神经估计器虽灵活但结构化不足。高维依赖建模需要在表达力、似然可追踪性和计算效率之间取得平衡。

Method: VDC训练单一二元去噪模型预测密度网格，然后对每条vine边应用IPFP/Sinkhorn投影以强制非负性、归一性和均匀边缘分布约束。该方法将逐边优化替换为GPU推理，实现amortized推断。

Result: 在合成和真实数据基准上，VDC实现了强大的二元密度精度、具有竞争力的互信息(MI)和总相关性(TC)估计，并显著加速了高维vine拟合。

Conclusion: VDC使得大规模显式信息估计和依赖分解变得可行，尽管条件下游推理性能仍有提升空间。该方法在高维vine拟合的计算成本和精度之间提供了良好权衡。

Abstract: Modeling high-dimensional dependencies while keeping likelihoods tractable remains challenging. Classical vine-copula pipelines are interpretable but can be expensive, while many neural estimators are flexible but less structured. In this work, we propose Vine Denoising Copula (VDC), an amortized vine-copula pipeline that trains a single bivariate denoising model and reuses it across all vine edges. For each edge, given pseudo-observations, the model predicts a density grid. We then apply an IPFP/Sinkhorn projection that enforces non-negativity, unit mass, and uniform marginals. This keeps the exact vine likelihood and preserves the usual copula interpretation while replacing repeated per-edge optimization with GPU inference. Across synthetic and real-data benchmarks, VDC delivers strong bivariate density accuracy, competitive MI/TC estimation, and substantial speedups for high-dimensional vine fitting. In practice, these gains make explicit information estimation and dependence decomposition feasible at scales where repeated vine fitting would otherwise be costly, although conditional downstream inference remains mixed.

</details>


### [118] [A Hierarchical MARL-Based Approach for Coordinated Retail P2P Trading and Wholesale Market Participation of DERs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20586)
*Patrick Wilk,Ethan Cantor,Yikui Liu,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The ongoing shift towards decentralization of the electric energy sector, driven by the growing electrification across end-use sectors, and widespread adoption of distributed energy resources (DERs), necessitates their active participation in the electricity markets to support grid operations. Furthermore, with bi-directional energy and communication flows becoming standard, intelligent, easy-to-deploy, resource-conservative demand-side participation is expected to play a critical role in securing power grid operational flexibility and market efficiency. This work proposes a market engagement framework that leverages a hierarchical multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MARL) approach to enable individual prosumers to participate in peer-to-peer retail auctions and further aggregate these intelligent prosumers to facilitate effective DER participation in wholesale markets. Ultimately, a Stackelberg game is proposed to coordinate this hierarchical MARL-based DER market participation framework toward enhanced market performance.

</details>


### [119] [Differentially Private Clustered Federated Learning with Privacy-Preserving Initialization and Normality-Driven Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20596)
*Jie Xu,Haaris Mehmood,Rogier Van Dalen,Karthikeyan Saravanan,Mete Ozay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PINA是一种结合聚类联邦学习与差分隐私的两阶段框架，通过LoRA适配器和压缩草图解决了DP噪声导致的聚类中心初始化难题，在保护隐私的同时提升了模型准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽保留本地数据，但仍存在隐私泄露风险；实际跨设备部署中数据异构性高，导致传统FL收敛慢且泛化差；聚类联邦学习虽能缓解此问题，但与差分隐私结合面临挑战——DP噪声使客户端更新过于嘈杂，服务器无法用聚合更新初始化聚类中心。

Method: PINA采用两阶段设计：第一阶段让客户端微调轻量级LoRA适配器，私下分享压缩草图，服务器利用草图构建鲁棒聚类中心；第二阶段引入正态性驱动的聚合机制，改善收敛性和鲁棒性。

Result: 在隐私预算ε∈{2,8}下，PINA平均准确率优于最先进的DP-FL算法2.9%。

Conclusion: 该方法在提供形式化隐私保证的同时，保留了聚类联邦学习的优势，为不可信服务器场景下的隐私保护FL提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables training of a global model while keeping raw data on end-devices. Despite this, FL has shown to leak private user information and thus in practice, it is often coupled with methods such as differential privacy (DP) and secure vector sum to provide formal privacy guarantees to its participants. In realistic cross-device deployments, the data are highly heterogeneous, so vanilla federated learning converges slowly and generalizes poorly. Clustered federated learning (CFL) mitigates this by segregating users into clusters, leading to lower intra-cluster data heterogeneity. Nevertheless, coupling CFL with DP remains challenging: the injected DP noise makes individual client updates excessively noisy, and the server is unable to initialize cluster centroids with the less noisy aggregated updates. To address this challenge, we propose PINA, a two-stage framework that first lets each client fine-tune a lightweight low-rank adaptation (LoRA) adapter and privately share a compressed sketch of the update. The server leverages these sketches to construct robust cluster centroids. In the second stage, PINA introduces a normality-driven aggregation mechanism that improves convergence and robustness. Our method retains the benefits of clustered FL while providing formal privacy guarantees against an untrusted server. Extensive evaluations show that our proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art DP-FL algorithms by an average of 2.9% in accuracy for privacy budgets (epsilon in {2, 8}).

</details>


### [120] [Occupancy Reward Shaping: Improving Credit Assignment for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20627)
*Aravind Venugopal,Jiayu Chen,Xudong Wu,Chongyi Zheng,Benjamin Eysenbach,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Occupancy Reward Shaping (ORS)方法，利用最优传输从世界模型中提取 occupancy measure 的几何信息，用于奖励塑造，从而解决稀疏奖励环境下的信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在目标导向行为学习中，动作与其长期后果之间存在时间滞后，导致信用分配困难。虽然生成式世界模型能够捕获未来状态的分布信息（即时序信息），但如何从中提取信息用于信用分配尚不明确。

Method: 利用最优传输理论，从学习到的occupancy measure模型中提取世界底层几何结构，进而构建能够捕获目标到达信息的奖励函数。该方法基于势能塑造（potential-based reward shaping）框架，理论上保证不改变最优策略。

Result: 在13种不同的长时程运动和操作任务中，ORS方法性能提升2.2倍。此外，在真实的托卡马克核聚变控制任务中验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: ORS通过从世界模型中提取时序几何信息，成功缓解了稀疏奖励设置下的信用分配问题，且不改变最优策略，同时具有从仿真到真实世界的可迁移性。

Abstract: The temporal lag between actions and their long-term consequences makes credit assignment a challenge when learning goal-directed behaviors from data. Generative world models capture the distribution of future states an agent may visit, indicating that they have captured temporal information. How can that temporal information be extracted to perform credit assignment? In this paper, we formalize how the temporal information stored in world models encodes the underlying geometry of the world. Leveraging optimal transport, we extract this geometry from a learned model of the occupancy measure into a reward function that captures goal-reaching information. Our resulting method, Occupancy Reward Shaping, largely mitigates the problem of credit assignment in sparse reward settings. ORS provably does not alter the optimal policy, yet empirically improves performance by 2.2x across 13 diverse long-horizon locomotion and manipulation tasks. Moreover, we demonstrate the effectiveness of ORS in the real world for controlling nuclear fusion on 3 Tokamak control tasks.
  Code: https://github.com/aravindvenu7/occupancy_reward_shaping; Website: https://aravindvenu7.github.io/website/ors/

</details>


### [121] [GRPO-VPS: Enhancing Group Relative Policy Optimization with Verifiable Process Supervision for Effective Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20659)
*Jingyi Wang,Lei Zhu,Tengjin Weng,Song-Li Wu,Haochen Tan,Jierun Chen,Chaofan Tao,Haoli Bai,Lu Hou,Lifeng Shang,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型信念探测的验证性过程监督方法，通过追踪推理轨迹中每一步后模型对正确答案的条件概率，实现更精细的过程级信用分配，从而改进GRPO的轨迹级反馈，提升推理效率并减少过度思考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO虽然消除了对评论家模型的需求，但在中间步骤的信用分配上不加区分，限制了其识别有效推理策略的能力，并导致过度思考（overthinking）问题。现有方法依赖昂贵的蒙特卡洛 rollout 或辅助模型提供中间监督，成本较高。

Method: 通过将生成过程分割为离散步骤，在每个步骤边界追踪模型追加正确答案的条件概率，计算可解释的逐段进展测量值。该方法无需评论家模型或额外监督信号，实现无模型、可验证的过程监督。

Result: 在数学和通用领域基准测试中，相较于 GRPO 均有提升：数学任务最高提升 2.6 分准确率并减少 13.7% 推理长度；通用任务最高提升 2.4 分和 4% 长度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过模型信念探测实现可解释的过程监督，有效解决了 GRPO 的信用分配问题，展现了强泛化能力，同时保持了样本效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has advanced the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) by leveraging direct outcome verification instead of learned reward models. Building on this paradigm, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) eliminates the need for critic models but suffers from indiscriminate credit assignment for intermediate steps, which limits its ability to identify effective reasoning strategies and incurs overthinking. In this work, we introduce a model-free and verifiable process supervision via probing the model's belief in the correct answer throughout its reasoning trajectory. By segmenting the generation into discrete steps and tracking the conditional probability of the correct answer appended at each segment boundary, we efficiently compute interpretable segment-wise progress measurements to refine GRPO's trajectory-level feedback. This approach enables more targeted and sample-efficient policy updates, while avoiding the need for intermediate supervision derived from costly Monte Carlo rollouts or auxiliary models. Experiments on mathematical and general-domain benchmarks show consistent gains over GRPO across diverse models: up to 2.6-point accuracy improvements and 13.7% reasoning-length reductions on math tasks, and up to 2.4 points and 4% on general-domain tasks, demonstrating strong generalization.

</details>


### [122] [Improving clinical interpretability of linear neuroimaging models through feature whitening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20675)
*Sara Petiton,Antoine Grigis,Raphaël Vock,Edouard Duchesnay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Linear models are widely used in computational neuroimaging to identify biomarkers associated with brain pathologies. However, interpreting the learned weights remains challenging, as they do not always yield clinically meaningful insights. This difficulty arises in part from the inherent correlation between brain regions, which causes linear weights to reflect shared rather than region-specific contributions. In particular, some groups of regions, including homologous structures in the left and right hemispheres, are known to exhibit strong anatomical correlations. In this work, we leverage this prior neuroanatomical knowledge to introduce a whitening approach applied to groups of regions with known shared variance, designed to disentangle overlapping information across correlated brain measures. We additionally propose a regularized variant that allows controlled tuning of the degree of decorrelation. We evaluate this method using region-of-interest features in two psychiatric classification tasks, distinguishing individuals with bipolar disorder or schizophrenia from healthy controls. Importantly, unlike PCA or ICA which use whitening as a dimensionality reduction step, our approach decorrelates anatomically informed pairs of neuroanatomical regions while retaining the full input signal, making it specifically suited for feature interpretation rather than feature selection. Our findings demonstrate that whitening improves the interpretability of model weights while preserving predictive performance, providing a robust framework for linking linear model outputs to neurobiological mechanisms.

</details>


### [123] [Variance Is Not Importance: Structural Analysis of Transformer Compressibility Across Model Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20682)
*Samuel Salfati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过对GPT-2和Mistral 7B的40+实验，系统研究了Transformer压缩的结构性特性。发现高方差激活方向与预测方向基本无关、块线性依赖于上游分布、直接量化优于分解量化、线性程度随深度增加、约30% token计算简单。单块替换可达34倍压缩，但多块替换因误差累积失败，表明静态后训练压缩存在根本限制，自适应逐token计算是更优方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性地研究Transformer压缩的结构性特性，揭示压缩方法的理论基础和局限性。

Method: 在GPT-2 (124M)和Mistral 7B (7.24B)上进行了超过40次实验，系统性分析了光谱压缩、块级函数替换、基于旋转的量化、激活几何和自适应早退出。

Result: 识别出五个关键结构属性：(1)方差≠重要性，高方差方向与预测方向96%不相关；(2)块线性是有条件的，仅在正确上游分布下成立；(3)重建墙现象，因子分解量化会产生交叉误差；(4)深度增加线性增强，Mistral从R²=0.17(块0)升至0.93(块31)；(5)约30% token计算容易。单块线性替换实现34倍压缩，困惑度仅增加1.71；多块替换因误差累积和分布偏移而失败。

Conclusion: 静态后训练压缩存在根本性限制；自适应、逐token计算是更有效的压缩方向。

Abstract: We present a systematic empirical study of transformer compression through over 40 experiments on GPT-2 (124M parameters) and Mistral 7B (7.24B parameters). Our analysis covers spectral compression, block-level function replacement, rotation-based quantization, activation geometry, and adaptive early exit.
  We identify five structural properties relevant to compression. (1) Variance is not importance: high-variance activation directions are approximately 96 percent uncorrelated with predictive directions (measured via CCA), and projecting onto these subspaces preserves over 90 percent of variance while degrading perplexity. (2) Block linearity is conditional: transformer blocks are approximately linear (R^2 ~ 0.95 on GPT-2, 0.93 on Mistral block 31) only under the correct upstream distribution; modifying earlier blocks induces distribution shift that degrades downstream approximations. (3) The reconstruction wall: approaches that factor weights into quantized components amplify errors through cross-terms, making direct quantization strictly superior. (4) Linearity increases with depth: Mistral 7B exhibits a progression from R^2 = 0.17 (block 0) to R^2 = 0.93 (block 31), indicating a division between nonlinear feature construction and linear refinement. (5) Approximately 30 percent of tokens are computationally easy, confirmed via exit heads and KL divergence sensitivity.
  We demonstrate that single-block linear replacement achieves 34x compression with a 1.71 perplexity increase on the final block of Mistral 7B, while multi-block replacement fails due to residual error accumulation and distribution shift. These findings suggest fundamental limits to static post-training compression and motivate adaptive, per-token computation as a more effective direction.

</details>


### [124] [MGDA-Decoupled: Geometry-Aware Multi-Objective Optimisation for DPO-based LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20685)
*Andor Vári-Kakas,Ji Won Park,Natasa Tagasovska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MGDA-Decoupled，一种基于几何的多目标优化算法，通过在轻量级DPO框架内寻找考虑各目标收敛动态的共享下降方向，解决LLM对齐中的多目标平衡问题，无需强化学习或显式奖励模型即可实现更公平的目标权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对齐需要平衡多个可能冲突的目标（有益性、真实性、无害性），传统固定标量化方法会对难以优化或少数目标系统性赋予过低权重，导致程序性不公平。

Method: MGDA-Decoupled：一种基于几何的多目标优化算法，通过寻找共享下降方向同时显式考虑各目标的收敛动态，在DPO范式内实现多目标对齐，无需强化学习或显式奖励模型。

Result: 在UltraFeedback数据集上，几何感知方法（尤其是MGDA-Decoupled）在综合和各目标维度上均获得最高胜率。

Conclusion: MGDA-Decoupled在UltraFeedback数据集上实现了最高的综合胜率和各目标胜率，证明了几何感知方法在多目标LLM对齐中的有效性。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) to desirable human values requires balancing multiple, potentially conflicting objectives such as helpfulness, truthfulness, and harmlessness, which presents a multi-objective optimisation challenge. Most alignment pipelines rely on a fixed scalarisation of these objectives, which can introduce procedural unfairness by systematically under-weighting harder-to-optimise or minority objectives. To promote more equitable trade-offs, we introduce MGDA-Decoupled, a geometry-based multi-objective optimisation algorithm that finds a shared descent direction while explicitly accounting for each objective's convergence dynamics. In contrast to prior methods that depend on reinforcement learning (e.g., GAPO) or explicit reward models (e.g., MODPO), our approach operates entirely within the lightweight Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) paradigm. Experiments on the UltraFeedback dataset show that geometry-aware methods -- and MGDA-Decoupled in particular -- achieve the highest win rates against golden responses, both overall and per objective.

</details>


### [125] [Generative Flow Networks for Model Adaptation in Digital Twins of Natural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20707)
*Pascal Archambault,Houari Sahraoui,Eugene Syriani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于GFlowNet的数字孪生模型自适应方法，将模型校准问题转化为生成建模问题，能够从不完整观测中采样多个合理的参数化配置，保持不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然系统的数字孪生需要与物理系统保持同步，但物理系统具有时变、部分可观测、参数不可直接测量等特点。传统方法往往只能找到唯一最优校准，无法表达参数空间中的多解性（equifinality）问题。

Method: 将模型自适应问题形式化为生成建模问题，使用GFlowNet学习在完整模拟器配置上的概率分布，采样概率与模拟输出和观测行为的一致性奖励成正比。

Result: 在基于番茄机理性模型的温室农业案例中，该方法能够恢复适应景观的主导区域、检索出强校准假设，并保留不确定性下的多个合理解释。

Conclusion: GFlowNet为数字孪生系统的模型自适应提供了一种有效的贝叶斯式框架，能够在保持参数多解性的同时进行有效推理。

Abstract: Digital twins of natural systems must remain aligned with physical systems that evolve over time, are only partially observed, and are typically modeled by mechanistic simulators whose parameters cannot be measured directly. In such settings, model adaptation is naturally posed as a simulation-based inference problem. However, sparse and indirect observations often fail to identify a unique and optimal calibration, leaving several simulator parameterizations compatible with the available evidence. This article presents a GFlowNet-based approach to model adaptation for digital twins of natural systems. We formulate adaptation as a generative modeling problem over complete simulator configurations, so that plausible parameterizations can be sampled with probability proportional to a reward derived from agreement between simulated and observed behavior. Using a controlled environment agriculture case study based on a mechanistic tomato model, we show that the learned policy recovers dominant regions of the adaptation landscape, retrieves strong calibration hypotheses, and preserves multiple plausible configurations under uncertainty.

</details>


### [126] [COMPASS: COntinual Multilingual PEFT with Adaptive Semantic Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20720)
*Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COMPASS是一个用于多语言LLM适应的数据为中心框架，通过参数高效微调（PEFT）和自适应语义采样策略来训练语言特定适配器，解决跨语言干扰问题。其扩展版本COMPASS-ECDA支持持续学习，可在生产环境中动态更新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在不同语言间存在性能差异，朴素的 multilingual fine-tuning 往往因负向跨语言干扰而降低性能。

Method: 使用 multilingual embeddings 和聚类技术进行分布感知采样，识别训练数据与目标使用分布间的语义差距，优先选择代表性不足的语义簇数据训练轻量级语言特定适配器。

Result: 在 Phi-4-Mini、Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-7B 三个模型架构和 Global-MMLU、MMLU-ProX、OneRuler 等多语言基准测试上，COMPASS 持续优于基于语言相似性的基线方法。

Conclusion: COMPASS 为在动态环境中开发和维护高性能多语言模型提供了有效、高效且可持续的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often exhibit performance disparities across languages, with naive multilingual fine-tuning frequently degrading performance due to negative cross-lingual interference. To address this, we introduce COMPASS (COntinual Multilingual PEFT with Adaptive Semantic Sampling), a novel data-centric framework for adapting LLMs to target languages. COMPASS leverages parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) by training lightweight, language-specific adapters on a judiciously selected subset of auxiliary multilingual data. The core of our method is a distribution-aware sampling strategy that uses multilingual embeddings and clustering to identify semantic gaps between existing training data and a target usage distribution. By prioritizing auxiliary data from under-represented semantic clusters, COMPASS maximizes positive cross-lingual transfer while minimizing interference. We extend this into a continual learning framework, COMPASS-ECDA, which monitors for data distribution shifts in production and dynamically updates adapters to prevent model staleness, balancing adaptation to new data with the preservation of existing knowledge. Across three different model architectures (Phi-4-Mini, Llama-3.1-8B, and Qwen2.5-7B) and multiple challenging multilingual benchmarks (Global-MMLU, MMLU-ProX), including unseen long-context tasks (OneRuler), we demonstrate that COMPASS consistently outperforms baseline methods guided by linguistic similarity, providing an effective, efficient, and sustainable solution for developing and maintaining high-performing multilingual models in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [127] [Supplement Generation Training for Enhancing Agentic Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20727)
*Young Min Cho,Daniele Bonadiman,Divya Bhargavi,Tamer Alkhouli,Salvatore Romeo,Dongwei Jiang,Khushbu Pahwa,Yubin Ge,Etsuko Ishii,Monica Sunkara,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SGT（补充生成训练）是一种高效的大模型优化策略，通过训练小型LLM生成补充文本帮助大型LLM更好地完成任务，避免了对大模型的直接修改和重复训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前为智能体任务训练大型基础模型存在三大问题：计算成本高昂、迭代周期长、新模型不断发布导致快速过时。这使得持续微调大模型变得不切实际。

Method: 提出SGT方法，训练一个较小的LLM生成有用的补充文本，将其附加到原始输入后帮助大模型更有效地解决问题。该方法将任务特定优化与大模型解耦。

Result: 轻量级模型能够动态适应任务需求的补充内容，在不修改底层大模型的情况下提升性能。

Conclusion: SGT实现了任务特定优化与大模型的解耦，为LLM驱动的智能体在现实应用中的部署提供了更灵活、成本效益更高的解决方案。

Abstract: Training large foundation models for agentic tasks is increasingly impractical due to the high computational costs, long iteration cycles, and rapid obsolescence as new models are continuously released. Instead of post-training massive models for every new task or domain, we propose Supplement Generation Training (SGT), a more efficient and sustainable strategy. SGT trains a smaller LLM to generate useful supplemental text that, when appended to the original input, helps the larger LLM solve the task more effectively. These lightweight models can dynamically adapt supplements to task requirements, improving performance without modifying the underlying large models. This approach decouples task-specific optimization from large foundation models and enables more flexible, cost-effective deployment of LLM-powered agents in real-world applications.

</details>


### [128] [Near-Future Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20733)
*Chuanyu Qin,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Naibin Gu,Dingyu Yao,Zheng Lin,Peng Fu,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NPO通过使用同一训练过程中策略的未来检查点作为辅助轨迹源，解决了现有混合策略方法无法同时满足"足够强"（高Q值）和"足够近"（低V值）条件的问题，AutoNPO进一步实现自适应触发和最优检查点选择，在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上将平均性能从57.88提升至63.15。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合策略方法存在根本矛盾：外部教师轨迹质量高但分布远，历史训练轨迹分布近但质量有上限。两者都无法同时满足最大化有效学习信号S=Q/V所需的"足够强"和"足够近"两个条件，导致RLVR收敛加速和性能上限提升受限。

Method: 提出Near-Future Policy Optimization (NPO)混合策略方案，利用同一训练run中策略的晚检查点作为辅助轨迹源，因其本身来自相同分布故天然同时满足"更强"和"更近"的条件，直接平衡轨迹质量与方差成本。进一步提出AutoNPO，根据在线训练信号自适应触发干预并选择最大化S的引导检查点。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上使用GRPO验证：NPO将平均性能从57.88提升至62.84，AutoNPO进一步提升至63.15，同时加速收敛并提升最终性能上限。

Conclusion: NPO通过利用策略自身未来状态，开创性地解决了off-policy轨迹源的核心矛盾。AutoNPO的自动化设计使其可直接应用于实际训练流程，为RLVR后训练提供了简单有效的性能提升方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a core post-training recipe. Introducing suitable off-policy trajectories into on-policy exploration accelerates RLVR convergence and raises the performance ceiling, yet finding a source of such trajectories remains the key challenge. Existing mixed-policy methods either import trajectories from external teachers (high-quality but distributionally far) or replay past training trajectories (close but capped in quality), and neither simultaneously satisfies the strong enough (higher $Q$ , more new knowledge to learn) and close enough (lower $V$ , more readily absorbed) conditions required to maximize the effective learning signal $\mathcal{S} = Q/V$. We propose \textbf{N}ear-Future \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (\textbf{NPO}), a simple mixed-policy scheme that learns from a policy's own near-future self: a later checkpoint from the same training run is a natural source of auxiliary trajectories that is both stronger than the current policy and closer than any external source, directly balancing trajectory quality against variance cost. We validate NPO through two manual interventions, early-stage bootstrapping and late-stage plateau breakthrough, and further propose \textbf{AutoNPO},an adaptive variant that automatically triggers interventions from online training signals and selects the guide checkpoint that maximizes $S$. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct with GRPO, NPO improves average performance from 57.88 to 62.84, and AutoNPO pushes it to 63.15, raising the final performance ceiling while accelerating convergence.

</details>


### [129] [F\textsuperscript{2}LP-AP: Fast \& Flexible Label Propagation with Adaptive Propagation Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20736)
*Yutong Shen,Ruizhe Xia,Jingyi Liu,Yinqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: F²LP-AP是一种无需训练的快速标签传播框架，通过几何中位数构建类原型，并基于局部聚类系数动态调整传播参数，可在同配图和异配图上高效运行，在保持竞争力的分类精度的同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN存在计算开销大、对同配性假设依赖强的问题；传统无需训练的方法（如标签传播）缺乏对异配图结构的适应性。

Method: 通过几何中位数构建鲁棒类原型，基于局部聚类系数(LCC)动态调整传播参数，无需梯度训练即可适应局部图拓扑结构。

Result: 在多种基准数据集上达到与训练GNN相当或更优的准确率，同时在计算效率上显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种训练免费、计算高效且能适应同配和异配图结构的半监督节点分类解决方案。

Abstract: Semi-supervised node classification is a foundational task in graph machine learning, yet state-of-the-art Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are hindered by significant computational overhead and reliance on strong homophily assumptions. Traditional GNNs require expensive iterative training and multi-layer message passing, while existing training-free methods, such as Label Propagation, lack adaptability to heterophilo\-us graph structures. This paper presents \textbf{F$^2$LP-AP} (Fast and Flexible Label Propagation with Adaptive Propagation Kernel), a training-free, computationally efficient framework that adapts to local graph topology. Our method constructs robust class prototypes via the geometric median and dynamically adjusts propagation parameters based on the Local Clustering Coefficient (LCC), enabling effective modeling of both homophilous and heterophilous graphs without gradient-based training. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmark datasets demonstrate that \textbf{F$^2$LP-AP} achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to trained GNNs, while significantly outperforming existing baselines in computational efficiency.

</details>


### [130] [Relative Entropy Estimation in Function Space: Theory and Applications to Trajectory Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20775)
*Chao Wang,Luca Nepote,Giulio Franzese,Pietro Michiardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在函数空间上估计Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度的通用框架，用于评估轨迹推断（TI）方法在部分观测下的表现。该方法可扩展到实际的快照数据集，解决了单细胞基因组学中破坏性测量导致路径空间分布不可识别的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前轨迹推断方法的评估主要依赖留出边际预测，但这是一种有限且不一致的评估协议。现有评估指标在不同方法间常给出不一致的判断，需要一个原则性的标准来比较推断的动力学模型。

Method: 提出一种在函数空间上估计概率测度间KL散度的通用框架，产生可扩展到实际快照数据集的可行、数据驱动的估计器。在基准测试套件上验证估计器的准确性。

Result: 函数空间KL估计与解析KL紧密匹配。在合成和真实scRNA-seq数据集上的应用表明，当前评估指标常给出不一致的评估，而路径空间KL能够对轨迹推断方法进行一致比较，并揭示推断动态中的差异，尤其在稀疏或缺失数据区域。

Conclusion: 函数空间KL可作为部分可观测性条件下评估轨迹推断的原则性准则。

Abstract: Trajectory Inference (TI) seeks to recover latent dynamical processes from snapshot data, where only independent samples from time-indexed marginals are observed. In applications such as single-cell genomics, destructive measurements make path-space laws non-identifiable from finitely many marginals, leaving held-out marginal prediction as the dominant but limited evaluation protocol. We introduce a general framework for estimating the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KL) divergence between probability measures on function space, yielding a tractable, data-driven estimator that is scalable to realistic snapshot datasets. We validate the accuracy of our estimator on a benchmark suite, where the estimated functional KL closely matches the analytic KL. Applying this framework to synthetic and real scRNA-seq datasets, we show that current evaluation metrics often give inconsistent assessments, whereas path-space KL enables a coherent comparison of trajectory inference methods and exposes discrepancies in inferred dynamics, especially in regions with sparse or missing data. These results support functional KL as a principled criterion for evaluating trajectory inference under partial observability.

</details>


### [131] [Efficient Multi-Cohort Inference for Long-Term Effects and Lifetime Value in A/B Testing with User Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20777)
*Dario Simionato,Andrea Tonon,Mingxue Wang,Weiguo Wang,Tong Gui,Xiaoyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对流媒体平台A/B测试中短期评估忽视用户长期价值的难题，提出一种结合逆方差加权和参数衰减模型的方法，能够在短期多队列实验中同时估计长期治疗效果和残余用户生命周期价值变化，改善估计精度的同时揭示了单一指标评估的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流媒体平台中用户流失代价高昂，但A/B测试通常在有限的实验周期内评估结果，短期和预测的长期指标可能无法捕获干预对用户留存的影响，导致在短期看似有益但长期产生更低价值的错误决策。

Method: 采用逆方差加权估计量结合多队列估计以降低方差，将估计的治疗轨迹建模为参数衰减函数，从而恢复渐近治疗效果和累积价值。

Result: 提高了长尾效应和残余用户生命周期价值变化估计的精确度，并识别出仅依赖短期或长期指标会导致产品决策错误的具体场景。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在单一实验中同时评估稳态影响和用户剩余价值，改善了长尾效应估计的精确度，并识别出仅依赖短期或长期指标导致错误产品决策的场景。

Abstract: In streaming platforms churn is extremely costly, yet A/B tests are typically evaluated using outcomes observed within a limited experimental horizon. Even when both short- and predicted long-term engagement metrics are considered, they may fail to capture how a treatment affects users' retention. Consequently, an intervention may appear beneficial in the short term and neutral in the long term while still generating lower total value than the control due to users churn.
  To address this limitation, we introduce a method that estimates long-term treatment effects (LTE) and residual lifetime value change ($ΔERLV$) in short multi-cohort A/B tests under user learning. To estimate time-varying treatment effects efficiently, we introduce an inverse-variance weighted estimator that combines multiple cohorts estimates, reducing variance relative to standard approaches in the literature. The estimated treatment trajectory is then modeled as a parametric decay to recover both the asymptotic treatment effect and the cumulative value generated over time.
  Our framework enables simultaneous evaluation of steady-state impact and residual user value within a single experiment. Empirical results show improved precision in estimating LTE and $ΔERLV$ and identify scenarios in which relying on either short-term or long-term metrics alone would lead to incorrect product decisions.

</details>


### [132] [Closing the Domain Gap in Biomedical Imaging by In-Context Control Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20824)
*Ana Sanchez-Fernandez,Thomas Pinetz,Werner Zellinger,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对生物医学影像中的批次效应问题，提出CS-ARM-BN方法，利用负对照样本进行元学习自适应调整。在JUMP-CP数据集上，该方法首次成功弥合了新实验批次间的性能差距（0.935 vs 0.862），使深度学习模型在生物医学成像中的实际应用成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Batch effects in biomedical imaging cause systematic technical variations that (1) undermine experimental reproducibility and (2) are the primary cause of deep learning failure on new experimental batches, preventing practical deployment.

Method: CS-ARM-BN (Control-Stabilized Adaptive Risk Minimization via Batch Normalization) - a meta-learning adaptation method exploiting negative control samples present in every experimental batch as stable context for adaptation.

Result: On JUMP-CP dataset for MoA classification: (1) Standard ResNets drop from 0.939±0.005 to 0.862±0.060 on new batches; (2) Foundation models with Typical Variation Normalization still fail to close the gap; (3) Meta-learning approaches achieve 0.935±0.018, first to close domain gap; (4) Control samples stabilize meta-learning under strong domain shifts (e.g., different labs).

Conclusion: Meta-learning approaches (CS-ARM-BN) successfully close the domain gap for deep learning on new experimental batches (0.935±0.018), with control samples stabilizing performance under strong domain shifts. Batch effects in bioimaging can be effectively neutralized through in-context adaptation.

Abstract: The central problem in biomedical imaging are batch effects: systematic technical variations unrelated to the biological signal of interest. These batch effects critically undermine experimental reproducibility and are the primary cause of failure of deep learning systems on new experimental batches, preventing their practical use in the real world. Despite years of research, no method has succeeded in closing this performance gap for deep learning models. We propose Control-Stabilized Adaptive Risk Minimization via Batch Normalization (CS-ARM-BN), a meta-learning adaptation method that exploits negative control samples. Such unperturbed reference images are present in every experimental batch by design and serve as stable context for adaptation. We validate our novel method on Mechanism-of-Action (MoA) classification, a crucial task for drug discovery, on the large-scale JUMP-CP dataset. The accuracy of standard ResNets drops from 0.939 $\pm$ 0.005, on the training domain, to 0.862 $\pm$ 0.060 on data from new experimental batches. Foundation models, even after Typical Variation Normalization, fail to close this gap. We are the first to show that meta-learning approaches close the domain gap by achieving 0.935 $\pm$ 0.018. If the new experimental batches exhibit strong domain shifts, such as being generated in a different lab, meta-learning approaches can be stabilized with control samples, which are always available in biomedical experiments. Our work shows that batch effects in bioimaging data can be effectively neutralized through principled in-context adaptation, which also makes them practically usable and efficient.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Coding with Eyes: Visual Feedback Unlocks Reliable GUI Code Generating and Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19750)
*Zhilin Liu,Ye Huang,Ting Xie,Ruizhi Zhang,Wen Li,Lixin Duan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体在GUI代码生成中的视觉反馈缺失问题，提出InteractGUI Bench基准和VF-Coder多智能体系统，通过视觉感知与界面交互显著提升了GUI代码调试效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体在代码生成任务中取得进展，但在GUI应用中面临两个核心局限：1）GUI程序是事件驱动的，但现有方法无法模拟用户交互来触发GUI元素逻辑；2）GUI程序具有视觉属性，基于文本的方法难以评估渲染界面是否满足用户需求。

Method: 提出了VF-Coder，一个基于视觉反馈的多智能体系统。该系统通过感知视觉信息并直接与程序界面交互，能够以类似人类的方式识别逻辑问题和布局问题。

Result: 在InteractGUI Bench基准上，VF-Coder使Gemini-3-Flash的成功率从21.68%提升至28.29%，视觉评分从0.4284提升至0.5584。

Conclusion: VF-Coder通过视觉反馈机制显著提升了GUI代码调试效果，在InteractGUI Bench上将Gemini-3-Flash的成功率从21.68%提升至28.29%，视觉评分从0.4284提升至0.5584，证明了视觉反馈在GUI调试中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents have shown remarkable progress in code generation. However, current agent methods mainly rely on text-output-based feedback (e.g. command-line outputs) for multi-round debugging and struggle in graphical user interface (GUI) that involve visual information. This is mainly due to two limitations: 1) GUI programs are event-driven, yet existing methods cannot simulate user interactions to trigger GUI element logic 2) GUI programs possess visual attributes, making it difficult for text-based approaches to assess whether the rendered interface meets user needs. To systematically address these challenges, we first introduce InteractGUI Bench, a novel benchmark comprising 984 commonly used real-world desktop GUI application tasks designed for fine-grained evaluation of both interaction logic and visual structure. Furthermore, we propose VF-Coder, a vision-feedback-based multi-agent system for debugging GUI code. By perceiving visual information and directly interacting with program interfaces, VF-Coder can identify potential logic and layout issues in a human-like manner. On InteractGUI Bench, our VF-Coder approach increases the success rate of Gemini-3-Flash from 21.68% to 28.29% and raises the visual score from 0.4284 to 0.5584, indicating the effectiveness of visual feedback in GUI debugging.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Task Success: An Evidence-Synthesis Framework for Evaluating, Governing, and Orchestrating Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19818)
*Christopher Koch,Joshua Andreas Wellbrock*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Agentic AI systems plan, use tools, maintain state, and act across multi-step workflows with external effects, meaning trustworthy deployment can no longer be judged by task completion alone. The current literature remains fragmented across benchmark-centered evaluation, standards-based governance, orchestration architectures, and runtime assurance mechanisms. This paper contributes a bounded evidence synthesis across a manually coded corpus of twenty-four recent sources. The core finding is a governance-to-action closure gap: evaluation tells us whether outcomes were good, governance defines what should be allowed, but neither identifies where obligations bind to concrete actions or how compliance can later be proven. To close that gap, the paper introduces three linked artifacts: (1) a four-layer framework spanning evaluation, governance, orchestration, and assurance; (2) an ODTA runtime-placement test based on observability, decidability, timeliness, and attestability; and (3) a minimum action-evidence bundle for state-changing actions. Across sources, evaluation papers identify safety, robustness, and trajectory-level measurement as open gaps; governance frameworks define obligations but omit execution-time control logic; orchestration research positions the control plane as the locus of policy mediation, identity, and telemetry; runtime-governance work shows path-dependent behavior cannot be governed through prompts or static permissions alone; and action-safety studies show text alignment does not reliably transfer to tool actions. A worked enterprise procurement-agent scenario illustrates how these artifacts consolidate existing evidence without introducing new experimental data.

</details>


### [135] [KnowPilot: Your Knowledge-Driven Copilot for Domain Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19820)
*Zekun Xi,Yichen Nie,Ziyan Jiang,Yujie Bao,Zhenqian Xu,Zhisong Qiu,Ziwen Xu,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: KnowPilot是一个领域特定知识增强的生成式智能体开源框架，通过整合任务先验、显性知识与体验知识，结合知识检索与记忆系统，解决了生成式智能体在垂直领域应用中的专业知识匮乏问题，在领域文本生成任务上表现优异，适用于医学、金融、工业等专业场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式智能体在真实工业场景中部署时面临显著瓶颈，主要原因在于缺乏领域专业知识支撑，导致应用效果受限。

Method: KnowPilot整合三类知识资源：任务特定先验知识、结构化知识库中的显性知识，以及通过人机交互捕获的专家经验知识。在架构上，该系统结合知识检索模块与记忆系统，支持任务需求注入、私有知识库加载以及隐性专家知识的持久化存储。以领域特定写作生成为典型应用场景进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，KnowPilot在领域导向文本生成任务上取得了优越性能，并成功应用于医学、金融及工业等多个专业领域。

Conclusion: KnowPilot作为开源框架，通过融合多源知识与记忆系统，有效解决了生成式智能体在垂直领域应用中的知识匮乏问题，为医学、金融、工业等专业场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancement of generative agents, their deployment in real-world industry scenarios often encounters significant challenges due to a lack of domain-specific knowledge. To address this gap, we present KnowPilot: a Domain-Specific Knowledge Augmented Generative Agent System. KnowPilot is an open-source framework that integrates task-specific priors, explicit knowledge, and experiential knowledge to enhance agent performance in specialized applications. It combines knowledge retrieval from structured repositories with a memory system capable of capturing expert experience through human AI interaction. Taking domain-specific writing generation as a representative case, KnowPilot enables private deployment, supports injection of task requirements, loads private knowledge bases, and stores tacit expert knowledge as persistent memory. Experimental results demonstrate that KnowPilot achieves superior performance in domain-oriented text generation and is applicable across fields such as medicine, finance and industry.

</details>


### [136] [Statistical Software Engineering with Tuned Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19822)
*Nimrod Busany*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AI系统中的维护对象应从"代码+配置"扩展为"版本化治理程序空间"，包含领域定义、结构约束、资格条件、评估资产和统计发布门控。在动态环境下，团队应将模型选择、检索策略、提示结构等视为需持续治理的"调优变量"而非一次性决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI启用系统面临持续变化：提供商模型/API变更、输入分布漂移、评估集老化、质量/成本/延迟/安全等目标不断协商。实践中团队常通过临时修改应对，导致系统状态不可追溯、决策依据失效。

Method: 将AI系统选择视为"调优变量"，在SE4AI和治理调优工作的基础上，构建以治理空间为软件工程核心对象的理论框架，采用统计方法（采样评估集、估计证据、效应量边界、置信/风险阈值）进行推广决策。

Result: 提出治理空间作为软件工程对象的定位，明确统计发布门控机制的理论基础。

Conclusion: 固定分配推理不足，选择的有效性需相对于环境、评估集和策略状态来理解。AI系统工程需要系统化的治理方法论。

Abstract: The maintained artifact in an AI-enabled system is not code plus settings, but a versioned governed program space: domains, structural constraints, eligibility, evaluation assets, and a statistical release gate. AI-enabled systems operate under changing world conditions: provider models and APIs change, input distributions drift, evaluation sets age, and objectives such as quality, cost, latency, and safety are renegotiated over time. In practice, teams often respond through ad hoc changes to model choice, retrieval policy, prompt structure, and operational thresholds. Fixed-assignment reasoning is therefore insufficient: a chosen assignment is valid only relative to an environment, evaluation set, and policy state. We argue that such choices should be treated as tuned variables: program variables maintained under governance as environments and evaluation sets evolve. Building on SE4AI work and our prior work on governed tuning, this paper positions the governed space as the software-engineering object. Here, statistical means that promotion relies on sampled evaluation sets, estimated evidence, effect-size margins, and confidence/risk thresholds.

</details>


### [137] [Stateful Embedded Fuzzing with Peripheral-Accurate SystemC Virtual Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19824)
*Chiara Ghinami,Igor Pontes Tresolavy,Luis Seibt,Nils Bosbach,Rainer Leupers*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将AFL++覆盖率引导模糊测试与状态化SystemC-TLM虚拟原型相结合的框架，实现嵌入式软件的高效预硅测试。该框架直接在 peripheral 模型中注入模糊测试生成的输入，使外设能够触发自然副作用（如中断和FIFO更新），在保持代码覆盖率和执行性能的同时消除误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式软件复杂度日益增加，传统手动测试已不切实际。虽然AFL++等覆盖率引导模糊器在常规软件上表现优异，但在嵌入式场景中应用面临挑战——现有方法要么使用快速用户模式模拟器牺牲外设真实性，要么依赖需手动插桩的全系统模拟器，难以应用于大规模软件。

Method: 提出一种将AFL++与状态化SystemC-TLM虚拟原型集成的创新框架。模糊测试生成的输入直接注入外设模型，使外设能够触发自然副作用（如中断和FIFO更新），实现全系统模拟与模糊测试的有效结合。

Result: 在嵌入式工作负载上的实验结果表明，该方法在保持与先进工具相当的代码覆盖率和执行性能的同时，消除了误报。

Conclusion: 该框架提升了嵌入式系统预硅测试的有效性，为嵌入式软件的自动化测试提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of embedded software has made comprehensive manual testing impractical, motivating the use of automated techniques such as fuzzing. Coverage-guided fuzzers like AFL++ have shown strong results for conventional software but remain challenging to apply effectively in embedded contexts, where peripheral behaviors play critical roles. Existing approaches either use fast user-mode simulators, sacrificing peripheral realism, or rely on full-system simulators with manual instrumentation, limiting applicability to large-scale software. In this work, we present a novel framework that integrates AFL++ with a stateful SystemC-TLM virtual prototype to enable realistic fuzzing of embedded software. Fuzzer-generated inputs are injected directly into peripheral models, allowing peripherals to trigger natural side effects such as interrupts and FIFO updates. By integrating fuzzing with full-system simulation, our framework advances the effectiveness of pre-silicon testing for embedded systems. Results on embedded workloads show that our approach eliminates false positives while maintaining comparable code coverage and execution performance as state-of-the-art tools.

</details>


### [138] [SolidCoder: Bridging the Mental-Reality Gap in LLM Code Generation through Concrete Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19825)
*Woojin Lee,Jin-Xia Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SolidCoder通过强制模型在算法设计前考虑边界情况，并使用基于属性的测试替代器（property-based oracles）和沙箱执行来替代LLM的内部执行追踪，从而解决代码生成中的"心理-现实鸿沟"问题。在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码生成框架依赖LLM的"心理模拟"来验证代码正确性，但存在根本性缺陷：模型会产生幻觉的执行轨迹并自信地验证有bug的代码。这种"心理-现实鸿沟"在两个正交维度上表现：规范鸿沟（规划时忽略边界情况）和验证鸿沟（为有缺陷的代码幻觉出正确行为）。

Method: 提出S.O.L.I.D.架构，包含：1) 强制边界情况意识：在算法设计前识别潜在边界条件；2) 基于属性的测试替代器：在沙箱中执行代码验证属性而非依赖想象的执行轨迹。

Result: 在GPT-4o上，SolidCoder达到SOTA的pass@1性能：HumanEval 95.7%(+0.6%p)、CodeContests 77.0%(+4.3%p)、APPS 26.7%(+3.4%p)。消融实验表明边界情况意识提供最大单一增益，执行锚定能捕获规范改进无法解决的不同类别错误。

Conclusion: 同时解决规范鸿沟和验证鸿沟对于鲁棒的代码合成至关重要；边界情况意识和执行锚定是互补的，两者缺一不可；该方法可泛化到RL后训练模型。

Abstract: State-of-the-art code generation frameworks rely on mental simulation, where LLMs internally trace execution to verify correctness. We expose a fundamental limitation: the Mental-Reality Gap -- where models hallucinate execution traces and confidently validate buggy code. This gap manifests along two orthogonal dimensions: the Specification Gap (overlooking edge cases during planning) and the Verification Gap (hallucinating correct behavior for flawed code). We propose SolidCoder with a simple principle: don't imagine -- execute. The S.O.L.I.D. architecture addresses both dimensions by forcing edge-case awareness before algorithm design and replacing imagined traces with sandboxed execution using property-based oracles. With GPT-4o, SolidCoder achieves state-of-the-art pass@1 performance: 95.7% on HumanEval (+0.6%p), 77.0% on CodeContests (+4.3%p), and 26.7% on APPS (+3.4%p). Ablation reveals that edge-case awareness provides the largest individual gain, while execution grounding catches categorically different errors that specification improvements cannot address. These gains generalize to RL post-trained models, validating that bridging both gap dimensions is essential for robust code synthesis. We release our code and framework to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [139] [Co-Located Tests, Better AI Code: How Test Syntax Structure Affects Foundation Model Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19826)
*Éric Jacopin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨AI代码生成中测试代码结构（内联vs分离）对生成质量的影响。通过830+文件、12个模型、3个提供商的实证研究，发现内联测试能产生近乎完美的代码保留率(100%)和正确率(92-100%)，而分离测试则导致模型间存在显著差异(0-100%正确率)。机制分析表明，5/7开源架构中内联测试标记获得2.8-4.4倍更强的注意力权重，且该效应可推广至非Transformer架构，暗示此设计建议对未来架构变化具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代码助手日益生成附带测试的代码，但测试代码的结构选择（与实现代码内联还是分离放置）是否影响AI代码生成质量尚不明确。这是一个关乎软件工程实践与AI辅助编程深度整合的基础性问题。

Method: 大规模实证研究（830+生成文件、12模型、3提供商）结合机制分析。使用SEGA三维评估框架（确定性、保留性、正确性）比较Python doctest（内联）与Rust #[test]（分离）两种测试语法，以d-ary堆实现为测试用例。对7个开源架构（6个Transformer+1个门控线性RNN）进行因果验证（knockout和steering实验）。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 内联测试产生近乎完美的保留率(100%)和正确率(92-100%)；(2) 分离测试暴露明显的模型层级差距(正确率0-100%)，且保留率与正确率相互独立；(3) 模型行为随代际演进，有模型打破了前辈的测试抑制模式；(4) 机制层面：5/7模型中内联测试标记获得2.8-4.4倍更强的注意力权重，该共定位机制可扩展至非Transformer架构。

Conclusion: 测试语法结构是基础模型时代的重要软件设计考量：测试代码与实现代码的共置能够产生可测量的更优AI生成代码效果。该效应受模型能力和编程语言约束，建议在AI辅助编程实践中优先考虑测试代码的内联结构。

Abstract: AI coding assistants increasingly generate code alongside tests. How developers structure test code, whether inline with the implementation or in separate blocks, has traditionally been a matter of testing philosophy. We investigate whether this choice affects AI code generation quality.
  We conduct a large-scale empirical study (830+ generated files, 12 models, 3 providers) using SEGA, a three-dimensional evaluation framework measuring Determinism, Preservation, and Correctness. Comparing inline test syntax (Python doctests) against separated test syntax (Rust #[test] blocks) on a d-ary heap implementation, we find that: (1) inline tests yield near-perfect preservation (100%) and correctness (92-100%) across all models; (2) separated tests expose stark model-tier gaps (0-100% correctness) and independence between preservation and correctness; (3) model behavior evolves across generations, and notably one model breaks the test suppression pattern of its three predecessors; (4) mechanistic analysis on 7 open-source architectures (6 transformers and a gated-linear Recurrent Neural Network (RNN)) reveals inline test markers receive 2.8-4.4$\times$ stronger attention in 5/7 models, with causal validation via knockout and steering experiments on the 4 code-specialized transformers and RWKV-6; the co-location mechanism extends to a non-transformer architecture, suggesting the design recommendation is robust to future architectural shifts. In the Foundation Model era, test syntax structure is a software design concern: co-locating tests with implementation code produces measurably better AI-generated code. This arxiv long version includes appendices that further qualify the effect as bounded by both model capability and programming language.

</details>


### [140] [ViBR: Automated Bug Replay from Video-based Reports using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19905)
*Sidong Feng,Dingbang Wang,Nikola Tomic,Tingting Yu,Aldeida Aleti,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ViBR通过融合CLIP嵌入相似度与视觉-语言模型，实现了从GUI录制视频自动复现bug的全自动化方案，在测试中达到72%的复现成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI屏幕录制视频作为bug报告 artifact 日益流行，但其自动化复现存在重大挑战。现有方法依赖脆弱的图像处理启发式算法、显式触摸指示器或预构建的UI转换图，存在仪器配置复杂和应用特定设置等局限。

Method: 结合CLIP嵌入相似度进行动作边界分割，并利用视觉-语言模型(VLM)进行区域感知的GUI状态比较和引导式bug回放。

Result: ViBR成功复现了72%的bug录制，显著优于最先进的基线方法和消融变体。

Conclusion: ViBR是一种轻量级、全自动化的GUI录制回放方法，无需复杂的仪器配置或应用特定设置即可实现bug复现。

Abstract: Bug reports play a critical role in software maintenance by helping users convey encountered issues to developers. Recently, GUI screen capture videos have gained popularity as a bug reporting artifact due to their ease of use and ability to retain rich contextual information. However, automatically reproducing bugs from such recordings remains a significant challenge. Existing methods often rely on fragile image-processing heuristics, explicit touch indicators, or pre-constructed UI transition graphs, which require non-trivial instrumentation and app-specific setup. This paper presents ViBR, a lightweight and fully automated approach that reproduces bugs directly from GUI recordings. Specifically, ViBR combines CLIP-based embedding similarity for action boundary segmentation with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for region-aware GUI state comparison and guided bug replay. Experimental results show that ViBR successfully reproduces 72% of bug recordings, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines and ablation variants.

</details>


### [141] [Insights into Security-Related AI-Generated Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19965)
*Md Fazle Rabbi,Asif K. Turzo,Arifa I. Champa,Minhaz F. Zibran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对超过33,000个AI生成的PR进行了大规模分析，识别并深入研究了675个安全相关的提交。研究发现AI引入的安全弱点相对集中，许多缺陷贡献仍被合并，拒绝原因多与社交/流程因素相关，提交信息质量对审查结果影响有限，并扩展了AI生成安全贡献的拒绝原因分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编程智能体在软件工程任务中的贡献日益增长，其不断增强的自主性引发了关于安全性和可信度的质疑。需要系统性地评估这些AI生成的代码贡献在实际软件开发中的安全质量。

Method: 分析了超过33,000个AI生成的PR，其中识别出675个安全相关的提交。随后对安全相关PR进行了深入审查，重点关注：反复出现的安全弱点、审查结果和延迟时间、提交信息质量以及拒绝原因。

Result: 安全相关AI PR引入的安全弱点主要集中在一小部分类型上（正则表达式低效、注入缺陷、路径遍历）。许多有缺陷的贡献仍被合并，而拒绝通常源于社交或流程因素（如不活跃、缺少测试覆盖）。AI PR的提交信息质量对接受率或延迟的影响有限，这与先前对人类PR的研究结论不同。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了自主编码智能体在安全软件开发中的优势和不足——尽管AI PR引入的安全漏洞类型相对集中（正则表达式低效、注入漏洞、路径遍历），但许多有缺陷的贡献仍被合并。AI PR的提交信息质量对接受率和延迟的影响有限，这与先前研究中人类PR的情况形成对比。研究还扩展了现有的拒绝分类法，新增了专门针对AI生成安全贡献的类别。

Abstract: Recent years have experienced growing contributions of AI coding agents that assist human developers in various software engineering tasks. However, this growing AI-assisted autonomy raises questions about security and trust. In this paper, we analyze more than 33,000 AI-generated pull requests (PRs) and identify 675 security-related submissions made by agentic AIs. Then we examine the security-related PRs with a focus on recurring security weaknesses, review outcomes and latency, commit message quality, and rejection reasons. The results show that security-related AI PRs introduce a small set of recurring weaknesses such as regex inefficiencies, injection flaws, and path traversal. Many flawed contributions are still merged, while rejections often arise from social or process factors such as inactivity or missing test coverage. The commit message quality of AI PRs has a limited effect on acceptance or latency, in contrast to human PRs reported in previous studies. We also extend existing rejection taxonomies by adding categories that are unique to AI-generated security contributions. These findings offer new insights into the strengths and shortcomings of autonomous coding systems in secure software development.

</details>


### [142] [Automated Quantum Software and AI Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19970)
*Nazanin Siavash,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对量子软件工程(QSE)和量子人工智能(QAI)领域的半自动化/自动化方法进行了系统性文献综述，聚焦于量子及混合量子-经典应用中的软件工程和AI自动化问题，旨在识别该领域的最新趋势和未来方向，以促进量子AI软件的更广泛开发和部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算领域专业人才稀缺，软件开发者和AI专家数量有限，且在混合量子-经典应用中自动化可帮助决定应用部署位置及量子平台选择，从而显著提升生产力和效率。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述(SLR)方法，对量子软件工程(QSE)和量子人工智能(QAI)领域的半自动化/自动化方法进行系统性梳理和分析。

Result: 通过系统性文献综述，识别了该领域的近期发展趋势和未来研究方向，提供了关于量子及混合量子AI赋能软件系统的开发和部署方法的全面概述。

Conclusion: 与以往文献综述不同，本文专门聚焦于量子及混合量子-经典应用中软件工程和人工智能的自动化问题，识别了近期趋势和未来研究方向，致力于推动量子及混合量子人工智能软件的更广泛应用和部署。

Abstract: In this paper, we conduct a systematic literature review of (semi-) automated approaches to Quantum Software Engineering (QSE) and Quantum Artificial Intelligence (QAI). Prior work in the literature indicated that both Software Engineering (SE) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) practices may become more efficient by using (semi-) automated approaches. This also holds in the Quantum Computing (QC), Quantum Information Science (QIS), and Quantum Engineering (QE) world, as well as in hybrid quantum-classical applications. In fact, automation is even more crucial in such cases since there is a limited number of developers and AI experts (e.g., data scientists) who possess the required knowledge and skills in QC. Moreover, in hybrid setups, automation may help decide what part of the application should be deployed on quantum hardware and on which of the available quantum platforms, if applicable. This can be a significant help to achieve productivity leap and efficiency even for subject matter experts. Unlike prior literature reviews and surveys, this work focuses on automation in SE and AI for quantum and hybrid quantum-classical applications and identifies the recent trends and future directions through a systematic literature review. We are interested in methods and techniques that can enable a broader development and deployment of quantum and hybrid AI-enabled software systems.

</details>


### [143] [FIKA: Expanding Dependency Reachability with Executability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20015)
*Yogya Gamage,Meriem Ben Chaaben,Martin Monperrus,Benoit Baudry*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FIKA是一个通过动态执行来保证第三方库调用点可达性的自动化分析管道，解决了静态分析工具在依赖管理中精确性不足的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前第三方库依赖管理工具面临关键挑战，包括自动化版本更新、漏洞检测和破坏性更新检测。现有依赖可达性分析工具多为静态分析工具，受限于无法实际执行，因此在精确性上存在根本性局限。

Method: FIKA通过生成代码并执行，利用执行轨迹为第三方库调用点的可达性提供可执行性保证。该方法在8个Java项目中进行了实证评估，并与静态分析工具Semgrep进行了对比集成。

Result: 实验表明：测试套件平均覆盖54%的调用点，FIKA进一步将覆盖率提升20%，共展示了2363个依赖方法的可执行性；六个项目中有超过75%的调用点获得了强有力的可执行性保证；同时FIKA能够改善Semgrep的静态漏洞可达性分析结果。

Conclusion: FIKA能够通过动态执行显著提升依赖可达性分析的精确性，平均改进20%的覆盖范围，在六个项目中确保75%以上调用点可执行，并能增强Semgrep等静态工具的结果，为漏洞优先级排序提供更强保障。

Abstract: Automated third-party library analysis tools help developers by addressing key dependency management challenges, such as automating version updates, detecting vulnerabilities, and detecting breaking updates. Dependency reachability analysis aims at improving the precision of dependency management, by reducing the space of dependency issues to the ones that actually matter. Most tools for dependency reachability analysis are static and fundamentally limited by the absence of execution. In this paper, we propose FIKA, a pipeline for providing guarantees of executability for third-party library call sites. FIKA generates code that is executed, and whose execution trace provides guarantees that a third-party library call site is actually reachable. We apply our approach to a dataset of eight Java projects to empirically evaluate the effectiveness of FIKA. On average, 54% of these call sites are covered by the existing test suites, and therefore, have evidence for their executability. FIKA further improves this coverage by 20% and is able to demonstrate executability for 2363 dependency methods. In six out of eight projects, FIKA provides strong guarantees that more than 75% of call sites are executable. We further demonstrate that FIKA is capable of improving the results provided by Semgrep, a state-of-the-art static vulnerability reachability analysis tool. We show that FIKA can help prioritize the vulnerability updates with stronger guarantees of executability in cases where Semgrep yields inconclusive reachability results.

</details>


### [144] [Hallucination Inspector: A Fact-Checking Judge for API Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20202)
*Marcos Tileria,Santanu Kumar Dash,Profir-Petru Pârţachi,Earl T. Barr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出并解决了LLM在API迁移任务中的"脚手架幻觉"问题——模型生成不存在的导入、构造函数和常量等幻影符号。提出了Hallucination Inspector工具，通过静态分析AST符号并与文档知识库比对来检测幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动化软件工程（如API迁移）中广泛应用，但会生成错误的调用上下文（幻影符号），现有标准指标难以有效检测此类幻觉，导致生成的代码无法正确执行。

Method: 提出Hallucination Inspector工具：(1)从LLM生成的代码中提取AST符号；(2)基于API文档构建知识库；(3)静态验证符号是否存在于知识库中。通过轻量级评估框架实现，减少误报。

Result: 在Android API迁移任务上的初步评估表明，该方法能成功检测幻觉，并显著降低标准指标的误报率。

Conclusion: 幻影符号检测对LLM代码生成质量至关重要；基于文档的静态分析方法比概率判断更可靠；Hallucination Inspector为API迁移的幻觉检测提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in automated software engineering for tasks such as API migration. While LLMs are able to identify migration patterns, they often make mistakes and fail to produce correct glue code to invoke the new API in place of the old one. We call this issue Scaffolding Hallucination, a failure mode where models generate incorrect calling contexts by inventing Phantom Symbols -- such as imaginary imports, constructors, and constants -- that do not exist in the API specification. In this paper, we show that standard metrics cannot be relied upon to detect these instances of hallucination. We propose Hallucination Inspector, a static analysis tool to detect Scaffolding Hallucination in LLM-generated code. Our approach includes a lightweight evaluation framework that verifies symbols extracted from the abstract syntax tree against a knowledge base derived directly from software documentation for the API. A preliminary evaluation on Android API migrations demonstrates that our approach successfully identifies hallucinations and significantly reduces false positives compared to standard metrics and probabilistic judges

</details>


### [145] [Towards Secure Logging: Characterizing and Benchmarking Logging Code Security Issues with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20211)
*He Yang Yuan,Xin Wang,Kundi Yao,An Ran Chen,Zishuo Ding,Zhenhao Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了日志代码的安全问题类型，构建了包含101个真实案例的基准数据集，评估了LLM在检测和修复日志安全漏洞方面的能力。结果表明当前LLM检测能力有限（准确率12.9%-52.5%），修复能力更弱，仅依靠问题描述比结合安全模式解释更能提升检测效果，揭示了LLM在安全日志领域的应用潜力和局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志代码在软件系统中记录关键事件和行为，对调试和监控至关重要，但不当的日志实践可能泄露敏感信息或导致日志注入等攻击。先前研究多关注一般性日志缺陷，缺乏对日志代码安全问题的系统分析，特别是在利用LLM进行检测和修复方面的研究。

Method: 构建了一个全面的日志代码安全问题分类法（4大类、10个模式），并创建了包含101个真实日志安全漏洞报告的基准数据集。提出自动化评估框架，整合多种上下文知识来测试LLM在检测和修复日志安全问题上的能力。

Result: LLM在检测任务上表现中等（平均准确率12.9%至52.5%），但在生成正确代码修复方面存在明显困难。实验发现，单独的问题描述比安全模式解释或两者结合更能提升LLM的检测准确率。

Conclusion: 研究发现当前LLM在日志安全问题的检测和修复方面存在明显差距：检测能力有限（准确率12.9%-52.5%），修复能力更是有待提升。问题描述单独作用比安全模式解释或两者结合更能提升检测准确率。研究结果为开发者提供了实践指导，同时揭示了当前LLM在安全日志领域的潜力和局限性。

Abstract: Logging code plays an important role in software systems by recording key events and behaviors, which are essential for debugging and monitoring. However, insecure logging practices can inadvertently expose sensitive information or enable attacks such as log injection, posing serious threats to system security and privacy. Prior research has examined general defects in logging code, but systematic analysis of logging code security issues remains limited, particularly in leveraging LLMs for detection and repair. In this paper, we derive a comprehensive taxonomy of logging code security issues, encompassing four common issue categories and 10 corresponding patterns. We further construct a benchmark dataset with 101 real-world logging security issue reports that have been manually reviewed and annotated. We then propose an automated framework that incorporates various contextual knowledge to evaluate LLMs' capabilities in detecting and repairing logging security issues. Our experimental results reveal a notable disparity in performance: while LLMs are moderately effective at detecting security issues (e.g., the accuracy ranges from 12.9% to 52.5% on average), they face noticeable challenges in reliably generating correct code repairs. We also find that the issue description alone improves the LLMs' detection accuracy more than the security pattern explanation or a combination of both. Overall, our findings provide actionable insights for practitioners and highlight the potential and limitations of current LLMs for secure logging.

</details>


### [146] [Shift-Up: A Framework for Software Engineering Guardrails in AI-native Software Development -- Initial Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20436)
*Petrus Lipsanen,Liisa Rannikko,François Christophe,Konsta Kalliokoski,Vlad Stirbu,Tommi Mikkonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Shift-Up框架，将传统软件工程实践（BDD、C4架构建模、ADR架构决策记录）重新解读为GenAI开发中的结构化护栏，通过DSR方法论指导，旨在解决 vibe coding 带来的架构漂移、可追溯性差和可维护性降低等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI（GenAI）正在重塑软件开发范式，从手动编码转向智能体驱动实现。然而，vibe coding 虽然能实现快速原型开发，但普遍存在架构漂移（architectural drift）、可追溯性受限和维护性降低等问题，亟需系统性的控制机制。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法论（DSR），提出 Shift-Up 框架，将已有软件工程实践重新解读为 GenAI 原生开发的结构化护栏。具体包括：可执行需求（BDD）、架构建模（C4）和架构决策记录（ADR）。通过探索性评估对比三种开发方式：非结构化 vibe coding、结构化提示工程和 Shift-Up 方法，开发一个 Web 应用进行验证。

Result: 初步研究结果表明，嵌入机器可读需求和架构制品能够稳定智能体行为、减少实现漂移，并将人类工作重心转移至高层设计活动和验证工作。传统软件工程制品可作为 AI 辅助开发中的有效控制机制。

Conclusion: 传统软件工程实践可以有效转化为 GenAI 开发中的控制机制，Shift-Up 框架为 AI 原生开发提供了一种平衡敏捷性与结构化的可行路径，有助于在保持开发效率的同时提升代码质量和系统可维护性。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is reshaping software engineering by shifting development from manual coding toward agent-driven implementation. While vibe coding promises rapid prototyping, it often suffers from architectural drift, limited traceability, and reduced maintainability. Applying the design science research (DSR) methodology, this paper proposes Shift-Up, a framework that reinterprets established software engineering practices, like executable requirements (BDD), architectural modeling (C4), and architecture decision records (ADRs), as structural guardrails for GenAI-native development. Preliminary findings from our exploratory evaluation compare unstructured vibe coding, structured prompt engineering, and the Shift-Up approach in the development of a web application. These findings indicate that embedding machine-readable requirements and architectural artifacts stabilizes agent behavior, reduces implementation drift, and shifts human effort toward higher-level design and validation activities. The results suggest that traditional software engineering artifacts can serve as effective control mechanisms in AI-assisted development.

</details>


### [147] [Finding Duplicates in 1.1M BDD Steps: cukereuse, a Paraphrase-Robust Static Detector for Cucumber and Gherkin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20462)
*Ali Hassaan Mughal,Noor Fatima,Muhammad Bilal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: cukereuse 是一个开源 Python CLI 工具，用于静态检测 BDD 测试套件中的 Gherkin 步骤重复。它结合了精确哈希、Levenshtein 相似度和句子嵌入的多层检测管道，在 347 个公开 GitHub 仓库（1,113,616 个 Gherkin 步骤）上进行了评估，实现了 F1=0.822 的检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDD（行为驱动开发）测试套件存在严重的步骤文本重复问题，维护成本高昂。现有检测技术存在局限性：要么需要运行测试，要么仅适用于单一组织。因此需要一个纯静态的、抗 paraphrase 的步骤级检测器，能够适用于任意代码仓库。

Method: 提出了 cukereuse 工具，采用三层检测管道：(1) 精确哈希；(2) Levenshtein 比率；(3) sentence-transformer 句子嵌入。并构建了包含 347 个 GitHub 仓库、23,667 个 .feature 文件、1,113,616 个 Gherkin 步骤的实证语料库。使用 Fleiss' kappa=0.84 标注的 1,020 对手动标注数据进行评估。

Result: 步骤级精确重复率为 80.2%，仓库中位数重复率为 58.6%。最强检测结果（score-free 标签下）为 pair 级 F1=0.822（接近精确匹配）。主要语义 F1=0.906，但因 recall=1.000 的分层偏差被高估。词法基线（SourcererCC-style、NiCad-style）分别达到 F1=0.761 和 0.799。

Conclusion: cukereuse 成功填补了静态、paraphrase 鲁棒的步骤级重复检测空白。该工具、语料库、标注数据和管道均以开源许可证发布，为 BDD 测试维护提供了实用工具。论文还从认知维度角度对 Gherkin 语法提出了批判性分析。

Abstract: Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) suites accumulate step-text duplication whose
  maintenance cost is established in prior work. Existing detection techniques require
  running the tests (Binamungu et al., 2018-2023) or are confined to a single
  organisation (Irshad et al., 2020-2022), leaving a gap: a purely static,
  paraphrase-robust, step-level detector usable on any repository. We fill the gap
  with cukereuse, an open-source Python CLI combining exact hashing, Levenshtein
  ratio, and sentence-transformer embeddings in a layered pipeline, released alongside
  an empirical corpus of 347 public GitHub repositories, 23,667 parsed .feature
  files, and 1,113,616 Gherkin steps. The step-weighted exact-duplicate rate is 80.2
  %; the median-repository rate is 58.6 % (Spearman rho = 0.51 with size). The top
  hybrid cluster groups 20.7k occurrences across 2.2k files. Against 1,020 pairs
  manually labelled by the three authors under a released rubric (inter-annotator
  Fleiss' kappa = 0.84 on a 60-pair overlap), we report precision, recall, and F1 with
  bootstrap 95 % CIs under two protocols: the primary rubric and a score-free
  second-pass relabelling. The strongest honest pair-level number is near-exact at F1
  = 0.822 on score-free labels; the primary-rubric semantic F1 = 0.906 is inflated by
  a stratification artefact that pins recall at 1.000. Lexical baselines
  (SourcererCC-style, NiCad-style) reach primary F1 = 0.761 and 0.799. The paper also
  presents a CDN-structured critique of Gherkin (Cognitive Dimensions of Notations);
  eight of fourteen dimensions are rated problematic or unsupported. The tool, corpus,
  labelled pairs, rubric, and pipeline are released under permissive licences.

</details>


### [148] [On the Informativeness of Security Commit Messages: A Large-scale Replication Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20461)
*Syful Islam,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对安全相关提交信息的可读性进行了独立复现和扩展研究。通过分析50,673个安全相关提交，发现安全提交信息普遍缺乏信息量，且这一问题随时间推移愈发严重。不同软件生态系统之间存在显著差异，且采用Conventional Commits规范反而与更低的信息可读性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（Reis et al., 2023）表明安全相关提交信息往往缺乏信息量，但该负面结果的可重复性尚未得到验证。理解提交信息的可读性对于安全补丁的分发和部署至关重要，直接影响漏洞修复的效率。

Method: 研究团队独立复现了原始研究，使用独立重新实现的技术手段，分析了50,673个安全相关提交（时间跨度：1999年6月至2025年10月），覆盖多个软件生态系统（Linux内核、Ubuntu、Go、PyPI等），并评估了Conventional Commits规范的实际效果。

Result: 复现研究确认了原始发现：安全相关提交信息总体上信息量不足。扩展研究进一步发现：①信息可读性随时间推移持续下降；②不同生态系统间存在显著差异；③令人意外的是，符合Conventional Commits规范的提交信息反而比不符合的更低信息量。
