Our approach enables segment-specific training while maintaining DPO's computational efficiency and training stability. We demonstrate that for multiple 7B LLMs fine-tuned using HiPO and DPO on the Math Stack Exchange preference dataset, the models trained with HiPO outperform the others on a variety of common math benchmarks and achieve greater organization, logical flow, and consistency as measured by GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [184] [Stateless Decision Memory for Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20158)
*Vasundra Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在受监管领域，企业部署长时程决策智能体时，尽管有众多有状态记忆架构，但实际主导方案仍是检索增强流水线。本文认为这是因为受监管部署对四项系统属性有刚性需求（有确定性回放、可审计理由、多租户隔离、无状态水平扩展），而有状态架构在结构上必然违反这些属性。作者提出确定性投影记忆(DPM)：一个仅追加的事件日志加上决策时的一次任务条件投影。实验表明，在10个受监管决策案例中，DPM在宽松预算下与摘要记忆相当，但在预算紧张时显著超越后者（20倍压缩比下，事实精度+0.52，推理一致性+0.53），且速度快7-15倍。作者认为无状态性是解释企业偏好的关键负载承载属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管领域（核保、理赔、税审），企业部署长时程决策智能体时，尽管有状态记忆架构已有十年发展，但实际主导方案仍是检索增强流水线。这种理论与实践的鸿沟需要解释——为什么更先进的有状态架构无法进入生产环境？

Method: 提出确定性投影记忆(DPM)架构，包含两个组件：(1) 仅追加的事件日志，记录所有交互；(2) 决策时的一次任务条件投影，将日志内容投影为决策上下文。DPM在设计上满足四项系统属性：确定性重放（append-only确保可重复）、可审计理由（单次调用结构简单）、多租户隔离（无跨任务状态共享）、无状态水平扩展（决策实例间无依赖）。

Result: 在10个受监管决策案例、三种记忆预算下实验：宽松预算时DPM与摘要记忆性能相当；预算紧张时（20倍压缩比），DPM显著优于摘要记忆，事实精度+0.52（Cohen's h=1.17, p=0.0014），推理一致性+0.53（h=1.13, p=0.0034）；速度提升7-15倍（决策时仅需1次LLM调用vs N次）。确定性研究中，DPM仅暴露1次非确定性调用，摘要记忆暴露N次累积调用；审计日志方面，DPM记录2次LLM调用，摘要记忆记录83-97次（LongHorizon-Bench）。

Conclusion: 无状态性(statelessness)是解释企业选择可回放但能力较弱的检索流水线的核心负载承载属性。DPM证明无需支付检索方案的性能代价即可获得该属性。论文还提出TAMS架构选择启发法和有状态记忆在企业运维条件下的失败分析框架，为实践者提供指导。

Abstract: Enterprise deployment of long-horizon decision agents in regulated domains (underwriting, claims adjudication, tax examination) is dominated by retrieval-augmented pipelines despite a decade of increasingly sophisticated stateful memory architectures. We argue this reflects a hidden requirement: regulated deployment is load-bearing on four systems properties (deterministic replay, auditable rationale, multi-tenant isolation, statelessness for horizontal scale), and stateful architectures violate them by construction. We propose Deterministic Projection Memory (DPM): an append-only event log plus one task-conditioned projection at decision time. On ten regulated decisioning cases at three memory budgets, DPM matches summarization-based memory at generous budgets and substantially outperforms it when the budget binds: at a 20x compression ratio, DPM improves factual precision by +0.52 (Cohen's h=1.17, p=0.0014) and reasoning coherence by +0.53 (h=1.13, p=0.0034), paired permutation, n=10. DPM is additionally 7-15x faster at binding budgets, making one LLM call at decision time instead of N. A determinism study of 10 replays per case at temperature zero shows both architectures inherit residual API-level nondeterminism, but the asymmetry is structural: DPM exposes one nondeterministic call; summarization exposes N compounding calls. The audit surface follows the same one-versus-N pattern: DPM logs two LLM calls per decision while summarization logs 83-97 on LongHorizon-Bench. We conclude with TAMS, a practitioner heuristic for architecture selection, and a failure analysis of stateful memory under enterprise operating conditions. The contribution is the argument that statelessness is the load-bearing property explaining enterprise's preference for weaker but replayable retrieval pipelines, and that DPM demonstrates this property is attainable without the decisioning penalty retrieval pays.

</details>


### [185] [Mol-Debate: Multi-Agent Debate Improves Structural Reasoning in Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20254)
*Wengyu Zhang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Text-guided molecular design is a key capability for AI-driven drug discovery, yet it remains challenging to map sequential natural-language instructions with non-linear molecular structures under strict chemical constraints. Most existing approaches, including RAG, CoT prompting, and fine-tuning or RL, emphasize a small set of ad-hoc reasoning perspectives implemented in a largely one-shot generation pipeline. In contrast, real-world drug discovery relies on dynamic, multi-perspective critique and iterative refinement to reconcile semantic intent with structural feasibility. Motivated by this, we propose Mol-Debate, a generation paradigm that enables such dynamic reasoning through an iterative generate-debate-refine loop. We further characterize key challenges in this paradigm and address them through perspective-oriented orchestration, including developer-debater conflict, global-local structural reasoning, and static-dynamic integration. Experiments demonstrate that Mol-Debate achieves state-of-the-art performance against strong general and chemical baselines, reaching 59.82% exact match on ChEBI-20 and 50.52% weighted success rate on S$^2$-Bench. Our code is available at https://github.com/wyuzh/Mol-Debate.

</details>


### [186] [Memory-Augmented LLM-based Multi-Agent System for Automated Feature Generation on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20261)
*Fengxian Dong,Zhi Zheng,Xiao Han,Wei Chen,Jingqing Ruan,Tong Xu,Yong Chen,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MALMAS是一种基于大语言模型的多智能体自动化特征生成系统，通过路由智能体激活不同职责的子智能体扩展特征空间，并结合程序记忆、反馈记忆和概念记忆实现迭代优化，在多个公开数据集上验证了其相对于现有最先进方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化特征生成方法依赖预定义算子库，无法利用任务语义信息，难以生成多样化、高价值的特征用于复杂任务；现有基于LLM的方法虽引入了语义信号，但仍受限于固定的生成模式且缺乏来自学习目标的反馈机制。

Method: 提出MALMAS系统：1) 将特征生成过程分解为具有不同职责的多个智能体；2) 引入路由智能体在每次迭代中激活适当的智能体子集，进一步扩展特征空间的探索；3) 集成包含程序记忆、反馈记忆和概念记忆的记忆模块，实现迭代优化并自适应引导后续特征生成，提升特征质量和多样性。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上进行了广泛实验，与现有最先进方法进行对比，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体协作与记忆增强机制，MALMAS能够有效突破固定生成模式的限制，实现更广泛、更优质的自动化特征生成。

Abstract: Automated feature generation extracts informative features from raw tabular data without manual intervention and is crucial for accurate, generalizable machine learning. Traditional methods rely on predefined operator libraries and cannot leverage task semantics, limiting their ability to produce diverse, high-value features for complex tasks. Recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based approaches introduce richer semantic signals, but still suffer from a restricted feature space due to fixed generation patterns and from the absence of feedback from the learning objective. To address these challenges, we propose a Memory-Augmented LLM-based Multi-Agent System (\textbf{MALMAS}) for automated feature generation. MALMAS decomposes the generation process into agents with distinct responsibilities, and a Router Agent activates an appropriate subset of agents per iteration, further broadening exploration of the feature space. We further integrate a memory module comprising procedural memory, feedback memory, and conceptual memory, enabling iterative refinement that adaptively guides subsequent feature generation and improves feature quality and diversity. Extensive experiments on multiple public datasets against state-of-the-art baselines demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. The code is available at https://github.com/fxdong24/MALMAS

</details>


### [187] [ActuBench: A Multi-Agent LLM Pipeline for Generation and Evaluation of Actuarial Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20273)
*Jan-Philipp Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActuBench是一个多智能体LLM流水线，用于自动生成符合国际精算协会教育大纲的精算评估题目。该系统通过四个Adapter分离的LLM角色（出题、构造干扰项、验证修复、摘要标注）实现协作生产。评测了50个模型（8个提供商），包含100道最难单选题和100道开放式题目。结果表明：多智能体验证是核心组件、开源模型在成本-性能权衡上具有竞争力、LLM评判比传统单选题更能区分前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精算专业评估需要与IAA教育大纲对齐的高质量题目，但传统人工出题成本高、效率低。现有LLM评估存在两个问题：多智能体验证机制的重要性未充分探索，以及精算领域特定评估的空白。

Method: 1. 多智能体分工架构：使用Adapter分离四个LLM角色——出题智能体构造题目、干扰项智能体构建选项、验证智能体独立校验前两阶段并驱动修复循环、辅助智能体负责Wikipedia笔记摘要和话题标注。2. 双基准评测：100道最难单选题（实证筛选）+100道开放式题目（LLM评判）。3. 评测规模：50个语言模型，来自8个提供商。4. 可视化展示：发布可浏览网页界面展示题目、模型响应和排行榜。

Result: 1. 多智能体验证有效：独立验证器在首轮标记大部分出题，修复循环解决了其中大部分问题。2. 开源模型成本-性能突出：本地运行的Gemma~4模型和Cerebras托管的120B开源模型在近零成本区域表现优异，后者几乎达到排行榜榜首。3. MCQ与LLM评判排名差异显著：单选题框架抬高了性能天花板，LLM评判模式更能区分前沿模型。

Conclusion: ActuBench通过多智能体协作范式，证明了自动化生成高质量精算评估题目的可行性。研究表明验证智能体是关键组件，一次性修复循环能有效提升题目质量。本地开源模型和中等规模模型在成本-性能权衡上具有竞争力，刷新了精算AI应用的成本基准。多智能体协作模式值得推广到其他专业领域的自动评测系统构建。

Abstract: We present ActuBench, a multi-agent LLM pipeline for the automated generation and evaluation of advanced actuarial assessment items aligned with the International Actuarial Association (IAA) Education Syllabus. The pipeline separates four LLM roles by adapter: one agent drafts items, one constructs distractors, a third independently verifies both stages and drives bounded one-shot repair loops, and a cost-optimized auxiliary agent handles Wikipedia-note summarization and topic labelling. The items, per-model responses and complete leaderboard are published as a browsable web interface at https://actubench.de/en/, allowing readers and practitioners to inspect individual items without a repository checkout. We evaluate 50 language models from eight providers on two complementary benchmarks -- 100 empirically hardest multiple-choice items and 100 open-ended items scored by an LLM judge -- and report three headline findings. First, multi-agent verification is load-bearing: the independent verifier flags a majority of drafted items on first pass, most of which the one-shot repair loop resolves. Second, locally-hosted open-weights inference sits on the cost-performance Pareto front: a Gemma~4 model running on consumer hardware and a Cerebras-hosted 120B open-weights model dominate the near-zero-cost region, with the latter within one item of the top of the leaderboard. Third, MCQ and LLM-as-Judge rankings differ meaningfully: the MCQ scaffold inflates the performance ceiling, and Judge-mode evaluation is needed to discriminate at the frontier.

</details>


### [188] [FSFM: A Biologically-Inspired Framework for Selective Forgetting of Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20300)
*Yingjie Gu,Bo Xiong,Yijuan Guo,Chao Li,Xiaojing Zhang,Liqiang Wang,Pengcheng Ren,Qi Sun,Jingyao Ma,Shidang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: For LLM agents, memory management critically impacts efficiency, quality, and security. While much research focuses on retention, selective forgetting--inspired by human cognitive processes (hippocampal indexing/consolidation theory and Ebbinghaus forgetting curve)--remains underexplored. We argue that in resource-constrained environments, a well-designed forgetting mechanism is as crucial as remembering, delivering benefits across three dimensions: (1) efficiency via intelligent memory pruning, (2) quality by dynamically updating outdated preferences and context, and (3) security through active forgetting of malicious inputs, sensitive data, and privacy-compromising content. Our framework establishes a taxonomy of forgetting mechanisms: passive decay-based, active deletion-based, safety-triggered, and adaptive reinforcement-based. Building on advances in LLM agent architectures and vector databases, we present detailed specifications, implementation strategies, and empirical validation from controlled experiments. Results show significant improvements: access efficiency (+8.49%), content quality (+29.2% signal-to-noise ratio), and security performance (100% elimination of security risks). Our work bridges cognitive neuroscience and AI systems, offering practical solutions for real-world deployment while addressing ethical and regulatory compliance. The paper concludes with challenges and future directions, establishing selective forgetting as a fundamental capability for next-generation LLM agents operating in real-world, resource-constrained scenarios. Our contributions align with AI-native memory systems and responsible AI development.

</details>


### [189] [Self-Awareness before Action: Mitigating Logical Inertia via Proactive Cognitive Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20413)
*Fulong Fan,Peilin Liu,Fengzhe Liu,Shuyan Yang,Gang Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SABA是一种推理框架，通过引入对缺失前提的自我意识来解决大语言模型在推理过程中因早期假设错误而导致结论不稳定的问题。该框架在非交互式侦探谜题基准上取得最佳性能，并在多个公开基准上保持领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在推理任务中表现良好，但缺乏对自身知识或推理状态是否完整的认知能力。在非交互式谜题设置中，叙事固定而底层结构隐藏，模型一旦在前提不完整时形成早期假设，就会将错误传播到整个推理过程，导致结论不稳定。

Method: SABA将推理表述为结构化状态构建与障碍解决交替的递归过程：1）信息融合（Information Fusion）：将叙事整合为可验证的基础状态；2）查询驱动结构化推理（Query-driven Structured Reasoning）：识别并解决缺失或不完整的前提，将其转化为查询，通过假设构建和状态细化逐步完善推理状态。

Result: 在所有三个难度分组的非交互式侦探谜题基准测试中，SABA均达到最佳性能；在多个公开基准上也保持领先结果。

Conclusion: 通过显式引入对缺失前提的自我意识，SABA能有效避免错误假设的传播，提高推理稳定性，在复杂推理任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on many reasoning tasks, yet they often lack awareness of whether their current knowledge or reasoning state is complete. In non-interactive puzzle settings, the narrative is fixed and the underlying structure is hidden; once a model forms an early hypothesis under incomplete premises, it can propagate that error throughout the reasoning process, leading to unstable conclusions. To address this issue, we propose SABA, a reasoning framework that explicitly introduces self-awareness of missing premises before making the final decision. SABA formulates reasoning as a recursive process that alternates between structured state construction and obstacle resolution: it first applies Information Fusion to consolidate the narrative into a verifiable base state, and then uses Query-driven Structured Reasoning to identify and resolve missing or underspecified premises by turning them into queries and progressively completing the reasoning state through hypothesis construction and state refinement. Across multiple evaluation metrics, SABA achieves the best performance on all three difficulty splits of the non-interactive Detective Puzzle benchmark, and it also maintains leading results on multiple public benchmarks.

</details>


### [190] [MedSkillAudit: A Domain-Specific Audit Framework for Medical Research Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20441)
*Yingyong Hou,Xinyuan Lao,Huimei Wang,Qianyu Yao,Wei Chen,Bocheng Huang,Fei Sun,Yuxian Lv,Weiqi Lei,Xueqian Wen,Pengfei Xia,Zhujun Tan,Shengyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了MedSkillAudit框架，用于在部署前评估医疗研究AI智能体技能的质量与安全性。评估75项技能后，系统与专家共识评分的一致性(ICC=0.449)超过了人类专家间的评估一致性(ICC=0.300)，表明该审计框架具有实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体系统在医学研究中的应用增长，现有通用评估方法不足以确保医疗研究技能的科学完整性、方法学有效性和边界安全性，需要开发专门针对医学领域的审计框架。

Method: 采用分层评估框架MedSkillAudit(skill-auditor@1.0)，由两位专家独立评估75项技能(分5类，每类15项)，评分维度包括：质量分数(0-100)、发布处置(生产就绪/限制发布/测试版/拒绝)及高风险失败标记。使用ICC(2,1)和加权Cohen's kappa量化系统-专家一致性，与人类评估者间一致性进行基准比较。

Result: 平均质量评分72.4(SD=13.0)；57.3%技能低于限制发布阈值。MedSkillAudit的ICC(2,1)=0.449(95%CI:0.250-0.610)，超过人类评估者间ICC=0.300。系统-共识评分差异(SD=9.5)小于专家间差异(SD=12.4)，无方向性偏差(Wilcoxon p=0.613)。方案设计类一致性最强(ICC=0.551)；学术写作类ICC为负值(-0.567)，反映评分标准与专家判断的结构性不匹配。

Conclusion: 领域特定的部署前审计可为医疗研究智能体技能治理提供实用基础，通过针对科学用例定制的结构化审计工作流程补充通用质量检查。其中学术写作类需调整评估标准以解决rubric与专家判断的错配问题。

Abstract: Background: Agent skills are increasingly deployed as modular, reusable capability units in AI agent systems. Medical research agent skills require safeguards beyond general-purpose evaluation, including scientific integrity, methodological validity, reproducibility, and boundary safety. This study developed and preliminarily evaluated a domain-specific audit framework for medical research agent skills, with a focus on reliability against expert review. Methods: We developed MedSkillAudit (skill-auditor@1.0), a layered framework assessing skill release readiness before deployment. We evaluated 75 skills across five medical research categories (15 per category). Two experts independently assigned a quality score (0-100), an ordinal release disposition (Production Ready / Limited Release / Beta Only / Reject), and a high-risk failure flag. System-expert agreement was quantified using ICC(2,1) and linearly weighted Cohen's kappa, benchmarked against the human inter-rater baseline. Results: The mean consensus quality score was 72.4 (SD = 13.0); 57.3% of skills fell below the Limited Release threshold. MedSkillAudit achieved ICC(2,1) = 0.449 (95% CI: 0.250-0.610), exceeding the human inter-rater ICC of 0.300. System-consensus score divergence (SD = 9.5) was smaller than inter-expert divergence (SD = 12.4), with no directional bias (Wilcoxon p = 0.613). Protocol Design showed the strongest category-level agreement (ICC = 0.551); Academic Writing showed a negative ICC (-0.567), reflecting a structural rubric-expert mismatch. Conclusions: Domain-specific pre-deployment audit may provide a practical foundation for governing medical research agent skills, complementing general-purpose quality checks with structured audit workflows tailored to scientific use cases.

</details>


### [191] [Measuring the Machine: Evaluating Generative AI as Pluralist Sociotechical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20545)
*Rebecca L. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判了当前生成式AI评估中的功能主义和规范主义方法，提出Machine-Society-Human (MaSH) Loops框架，将AI评估重新定义为多元化的社会技术系统评估，强调价值观在交互中的递归建构，而非静态基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估基准存在根本性问题：功能主义将模型视为孤立预测器，规范主义关注系统"应然"状态，两者都忽视了AI评估中社会技术过程的复杂性，在多元文化背景下可能导致狭隘文化视角的固化。静态基准无法捕捉模型、用户和机构如何递归共构意义与价值观。

Method: 概念层面：提出MaSH Loops框架，将评估重构为递归的、生成性的过程。方法论层面：开发World Values Benchmark，采用基于世界价值观调查数据的分布方法、结构化提示集和锚定感知评分。实证层面：通过两个案例验证：早期GPT-3的价值漂移分析和房地产领域的社会技术评估。

Result: MaSH Loops框架成功展示了如何追踪模型、用户和机构如何递归共构意义和价值观。World Values Benchmark提供了在多元文化背景下分布性评估价值观的方法。GPT-3案例揭示了早期模型的价值漂移现象；房地产案例证明了社会技术评估在实际应用中的可行性。

Conclusion: 静态基准不足以评估生成式AI。负责任的评估需要多元化的、过程导向的框架，能够揭示谁的价值观念被践行。评估本身就是治理的场所，塑造着AI系统如何被理解、部署和信任。AI评估应从评判输出转向审视价值观如何在交互中实现。

Abstract: In measurement theory, instruments do not simply record reality; they help constitute what is observed. The same holds for generative AI evaluation: benchmarks do not just measure, they shape what models appear to be. Functionalist benchmarks treat models as isolated predictors, while prescriptive approaches assess what systems ought to be. Both obscure the sociotechnical processes through which meaning and values are enacted, risking the reification of narrow cultural perspectives in pluralist contexts.
  This thesis advances a descriptive alternative. It argues that generative AI must be evaluated as a pluralist sociotechnical system and develops Machine-Society-Human (MaSH) Loops, a framework for tracing how models, users, and institutions recursively co-construct meaning and values. Evaluation shifts from judging outputs to examining how values are enacted in interaction.
  Three contributions follow. Conceptually, MaSH Loops reframes evaluation as recursive, enactive process. Methodologically, the World Values Benchmark introduces a distributional approach grounded in World Values Survey data, structured prompt sets, and anchor-aware scoring. Empirically, the thesis demonstrates these through two cases: value drift in early GPT-3 and sociotechnical evaluation in real estate. A final chapter draws on participatory realism to argue that prompting and evaluation are constitutive interventions, not neutral observations.
  The thesis argues that static benchmarks are insufficient for generative AI. Responsible evaluation requires pluralist, process-oriented frameworks that make visible whose values are enacted. Evaluation is therefore a site of governance, shaping how AI systems are understood, deployed, and trusted.

</details>


### [192] [Self-Guided Plan Extraction for Instruction-Following Tasks with Goal-Conditional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20601)
*Zoya Volovikova,Nikita Sorokin,Dmitriy Lukashevskiy,Aleksandr Panov,Alexey Skrynnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SuperIgor是一个用于指令跟随任务的框架，通过自学习机制让语言模型生成和优化高层规划，减少对人工标注数据集的依赖。采用RL智能体与语言模型迭代协同训练，形成反馈循环使两者共同提升。在动态随机环境中的实验表明，SuperIgor agents能更严格地遵循指令，并对未见过的指令展现出强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预定义的子任务，需要大量人工标注数据集，限制了系统的灵活性和可扩展性。

Method: 引入自学习机制使语言模型能够生成和优化高层规划；采用迭代协同训练：RL agent学习遵循生成的规划，语言模型根据RL反馈和偏好进行自适应调整，形成双向反馈循环实现agent和planner的联合改进。

Result: SuperIgor agents比基线方法更严格地遵循指令指令，且对未见过的指令展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架在具有丰富动态性和随机性的环境中得到验证，为指令跟随任务提供了一种减少人工标注依赖的有效方案。

Abstract: We introduce SuperIgor, a framework for instruction-following tasks. Unlike prior methods that rely on predefined subtasks, SuperIgor enables a language model to generate and refine high-level plans through a self-learning mechanism, reducing the need for manual dataset annotation. Our approach involves iterative co-training: an RL agent is trained to follow the generated plans, while the language model adapts and modifies these plans based on RL feedback and preferences. This creates a feedback loop where both the agent and the planner improve jointly. We validate our framework in environments with rich dynamics and stochasticity. Results show that SuperIgor agents adhere to instructions more strictly than baseline methods, while also demonstrating strong generalization to previously unseen instructions.

</details>


### [193] [Large Language Models Outperform Humans in Fraud Detection and Resistance to Motivated Investor Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20652)
*Nattavudh Powdthavee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A preregistered experiment tested whether LLMs suppress fraud warnings when investors arrive already persuaded of fraudulent opportunities. Across 7 LLMs, 12 investment scenarios, and 3,360+ conversations, researchers found that contrary to predictions, AI systems maintained fraud warnings at consistent rates regardless of investor pressure, with human advisors showing 2-4x higher fraud endorsement and warning suppression under pressure.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Concern that LLMs trained on human feedback may develop human-like biases, particularly the tendency to suppress fraud warnings when dealing with already-persuaded investors—a pattern observed in human financial advisors. This raises questions about AI reliability in financial advisory contexts.

Method: Preregistered experiment with 7 leading LLMs and 12 investment scenarios covering legitimate, high-risk, and fraudulent opportunities. Combined 3,360 AI advisory conversations with a 1,201-participant human benchmark. Tested how "motivated investor framing" (investors already persuaded of fraudulent opportunities) affects fraud warnings.

Result: Counter to predictions, motivated investor framing did NOT suppress AI fraud warnings (endorsement reversal < 0.3%). Human advisors baseline fraud endorsement: 13-14%; LLMs: 0%. Humans suppressed warnings under pressure at 2-4x the rate of AI systems.

Conclusion: LLMs demonstrate superior consistency and integrity in fraud warnings compared to human advisors, with AI systems requiring substantially higher pressure to reverse their warnings. Findings suggest caution about attributing human-like biases to LLMs, and highlight that AI advisory systems may provide more reliable fraud protection in investment contexts. However, the study was limited to lay human advisors, and future research should examine professional financial advisors.

Abstract: Large language models trained on human feedback may suppress fraud warnings when investors arrive already persuaded of a fraudulent opportunity. We tested this in a preregistered experiment across seven leading LLMs and twelve investment scenarios covering legitimate, high-risk, and objectively fraudulent opportunities, combining 3,360 AI advisory conversations with a 1,201-participant human benchmark. Contrary to predictions, motivated investor framing did not suppress AI fraud warnings; if anything, it marginally increased them. Endorsement reversal occurred in fewer than 3 in 1,000 observations. Human advisors endorsed fraudulent investments at baseline rates of 13-14%, versus 0% across all LLMs, and suppressed warnings under pressure at two to four times the AI rate. AI systems currently provide more consistent fraud warnings than lay humans in an identical advisory role.

</details>


### [194] [Learning to Evolve: A Self-Improving Framework for Multi-Agent Systems via Textual Parameter Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20714)
*Shan He,Runze Wang,Zhuoyun Du,Huiyu Bai,Zouying Cao,Yu Cheng,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TPGO框架，通过将多智能体系统建模为Textual Parameter Graph，并利用文本梯度和Group Relative Agent Optimization元学习策略，使系统能够从历史优化经验中学习如何自我优化，从而实现自动化的自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动优化方法主要关注扁平化提示调整，缺乏对多智能体系统中复杂交互关系的结构感知能力；更重要的是，这些优化器是静态的，无法从经验中学习改进自身的优化策略。

Method: 1) 将MAS建模为Textual Parameter Graph (TPG)，将智能体、工具和工作流作为模块化、可优化的节点；2) 从执行轨迹中推导出"文本梯度"，以自然语言形式提供结构化反馈；3) 提出Group Relative Agent Optimization (GRAO)元学习策略，从历史优化经验中学习改进优化方法。

Result: 在GAIA和MCP-Universe等复杂基准测试上，TPGO显著提升了SOTA智能体框架的性能，实现了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: TPGO通过自动化的自我改进优化，证明了将结构化图建模与元学习相结合在多智能体系统优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Designing and optimizing multi-agent systems (MAS) is a complex, labor-intensive process of "Agent Engineering." Existing automatic optimization methods, primarily focused on flat prompt tuning, lack the structural awareness to debug the intricate web of interactions in MAS. More critically, these optimizers are static; they do not learn from experience to improve their own optimization strategies. To address these gaps, we introduce Textual Parameter Graph Optimization (TPGO), a framework that enables a multi-agent system to learn to evolve. TPGO first models the MAS as a Textual Parameter Graph (TPG), where agents, tools, and workflows are modular, optimizable nodes. To guide evolution, we derive "textual gradients," structured natural language feedback from execution traces, to pinpoint failures and suggest granular modifications. The core of our framework is Group Relative Agent Optimization (GRAO), a novel meta-learning strategy that learns from historical optimization experiences. By analyzing past successes and failures, GRAO becomes progressively better at proposing effective updates, allowing the system to learn how to optimize itself. Extensive experiments on complex benchmarks like GAIA and MCP-Universe show that TPGO significantly enhances the performance of state-of-the-art agent frameworks, achieving higher success rates through automated, self-improving optimization.

</details>


### [195] [Interval POMDP Shielding for Imperfect-Perception Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20728)
*William Scarbro,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对学习感知自主系统的运行时护盾方法，通过从有限标记数据构建感知不确定性置信区间，将系统建模为区间部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，生成保守信念集以确保有限时域内的安全性保证。实验表明该方法在四个案例中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习感知模型的自主系统可能因传感器读数误分类而做出不安全决策，需要一种防护机制来阻止可能违反安全约束的动作。

Method: 从有限标记数据构建感知结果的置信区间，将系统建模为有限区间部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Interval POMDP），提出算法计算与观测一致的保守信念集，以此构建运行时护盾。

Result: 在训练数据的高概率条件下，若真实感知不确定性在所学区间内，则护盾允许的每个动作都满足安全下界。

Conclusion: 所提方法在四个案例研究中优于现有基线方法，显著提升了系统安全性。

Abstract: Autonomous systems that rely on learned perception can make unsafe decisions when sensor readings are misclassified. We study shielding for this setting: given a proposed action, a shield blocks actions that could violate safety. We consider the common case where system dynamics are known but perception uncertainty must be estimated from finite labeled data. From these data we build confidence intervals for the probabilities of perception outcomes and use them to model the system as a finite Interval Partially Observable Markov Decision Process with discrete states and actions. We then propose an algorithm to compute a conservative set of beliefs over the underlying state that is consistent with the observations seen so far.
  This enables us to construct a runtime shield that comes with a finite-horizon guarantee: with high probability over the training data, if the true perception uncertainty rates lie within the learned intervals, then every action admitted by the shield satisfies a stated lower bound on safety. Experiments on four case studies show that our shielding approach (and variants derived from it) improves the safety of the system over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [196] [Where and What: Reasoning Dynamic and Implicit Preferences in Situated Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20749)
*Dongding Lin,Jian Wang,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SiPeR框架，通过场景转换估计和贝叶斯逆推理两种机制，解决情境会话推荐中用户偏好动态隐式、场景影响兴趣、对话中偏好演变等核心挑战，在两个基准数据集上验证了推荐准确率和响应生成质量的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的推荐系统难以处理情境会话推荐中的复杂动态偏好。用户的隐式偏好受周围场景影响，且在对话过程中不断演变，这使得推荐的时机和相关性难以把握。现有方法缺乏对场景与用户需求匹配关系的建模能力。

Method: 提出SiPeR框架，包含两个核心机制：1) 场景转换估计 - 评估当前场景是否满足用户需求，必要时引导用户转向更合适的场景；2) 贝叶斯逆推理 - 利用多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的似然度来预测用户对场景中候选物品的偏好。

Result: 在两个代表性基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，SiPeR在推荐准确率和响应生成质量两个方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SiPeR通过整合场景转换估计与贝叶斯逆推理，有效解决了情境会话推荐中的动态偏好理解难题，为该领域提供了一种有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Situated conversational recommendation (SCR), which utilizes visual scenes grounded in specific environments and natural language dialogue to deliver contextually appropriate recommendations, has emerged as a promising research direction due to its close alignment with real-world scenarios. Compared to traditional recommendations, SCR requires a deeper understanding of dynamic and implicit user preferences, as the surrounding scene often influences users' underlying interests, while both may evolve across conversations. This complexity significantly impacts the timing and relevance of recommendations. To address this, we propose situated preference reasoning (SiPeR), a novel framework that integrates two core mechanisms: (1) Scene transition estimation, which estimates whether the current scene satisfies user needs, and guides the user toward a more suitable scene when necessary; and (2) Bayesian inverse inference, which leverages the likelihood of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to predict user preferences about candidate items within the scene. Extensive experiments on two representative benchmarks demonstrate SiPeR's superiority in both recommendation accuracy and response generation quality. The code and data are available at https://github.com/DongdingLin/SiPeR.

</details>


### [197] [V-tableR1: Process-Supervised Multimodal Table Reasoning with Critic-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20755)
*Yubo Jiang,Yitong An,Xin Yang,Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi,Xuxin Cheng,Fengying Xie,Zhiguo Jiang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: V-tableR1是一个面向多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的过程监督强化学习框架，利用表格的确定性网格结构作为视觉测试平台，通过专门的批评者VLM对策略VLM生成的显式视觉思维链提供密集的步骤级反馈，并提出新型PGPO算法，在复杂表格基准测试中取得开源模型最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs仅在最终结果上训练，往往将视觉推理视为黑盒，依赖表面模式匹配而非进行严格的多步推理。虽然可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)可以强制透明推理轨迹，但将其扩展到视觉领域严重受限于将抽象逻辑锚定到连续像素空间的模糊性。

Method: 1) 利用表格的确定性网格结构作为理想的视觉测试平台；2) 采用专门的批评者VLM对策略VLM生成的显式视觉思维链提供密集的步骤级反馈；3) 提出PGPO算法，整合过程奖励、解耦策略约束和长度感知的动态采样。

Result: V-tableR1能明确惩罚视觉幻觉和捷径猜测，在复杂表格基准测试中达到开源模型最高精度，超越参数量高达18倍的模型，并相比SFT基线有明显提升。

Conclusion: 通过将多模态推理从黑盒模式匹配根本性地转向可验证的逻辑推导，V-tableR1 4B在复杂表格任务上建立了开源模型的最优精度，证明了过程监督方法在视觉推理领域的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce V-tableR1, a process-supervised reinforcement learning framework that elicits rigorous, verifiable reasoning from multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Current MLLMs trained solely on final outcomes often treat visual reasoning as a black box, relying on superficial pattern matching rather than performing rigorous multi-step inference. While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards could enforce transparent reasoning trajectories, extending it to visual domains remains severely hindered by the ambiguity of grounding abstract logic into continuous pixel space. We solve this by leveraging the deterministic grid structure of tables as an ideal visual testbed. V-tableR1 employs a specialized critic VLM to provide dense, step-level feedback on the explicit visual chain-of-thought generated by a policy VLM. To optimize this system, we propose Process-Guided Direct Alignment Policy Optimization (PGPO), a novel RL algorithm integrating process rewards, decoupled policy constraints, and length-aware dynamic sampling. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that V-tableR1 explicitly penalizes visual hallucinations and shortcut guessing. By fundamentally shifting multimodal inference from black-box pattern matching to verifiable logical derivation, V-tableR1 4B establishes state-of-the-art accuracy among open-source models on complex tabular benchmarks, outperforming models up to 18x its size and improving over its SFT baseline

</details>


### [198] [Automatic Ontology Construction Using LLMs as an External Layer of Memory, Verification, and Planning for Hybrid Intelligent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20795)
*Pavel Salovskii,Iuliia Gorshkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将大型语言模型（LLM）与外部本体知识层相结合的混合架构，通过RDF/OWL知识图谱实现持久化、可验证的语义推理，突破传统向量检索（RAG）和纯参数知识的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM系统存在关键缺陷：缺乏长期记忆、结构化理解能力弱、多步推理能力有限。

Method: 构建自动化本体构建管道，从文档、API、对话日志等多源异构数据中进行实体识别、关系抽取、规范化与三元组生成；使用SHACL和OWL约束进行验证；推理阶段将向量检索与图推理、外部工具调用整合为统一上下文。

Result: 在Tower of Hanoi等规划任务上的实验表明，本体增强显著优于基线LLM系统，并支持形式化验证，实现生成-验证-修正的闭环。

Conclusion: 该架构为构建需持久知识、可解释性和可靠决策的智能体系统、机器人和企业AI应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid architecture for intelligent systems in which large language models (LLMs) are extended with an external ontological memory layer. Instead of relying solely on parametric knowledge and vector-based retrieval (RAG), the proposed approach constructs and maintains a structured knowledge graph using RDF/OWL representations, enabling persistent, verifiable, and semantically grounded reasoning.
  The core contribution is an automated pipeline for ontology construction from heterogeneous data sources, including documents, APIs, and dialogue logs. The system performs entity recognition, relation extraction, normalization, and triple generation, followed by validation using SHACL and OWL constraints, and continuous graph updates. During inference, LLMs operate over a combined context that integrates vector-based retrieval with graph-based reasoning and external tool interaction.
  Experimental observations on planning tasks, including the Tower of Hanoi benchmark, indicate that ontology augmentation improves performance in multi-step reasoning scenarios compared to baseline LLM systems. In addition, the ontology layer enables formal validation of generated outputs, transforming the system into a generation-verification-correction pipeline.
  The proposed architecture addresses key limitations of current LLM-based systems, including lack of long-term memory, weak structural understanding, and limited reasoning capabilities. It provides a foundation for building agent-based systems, robotics applications, and enterprise AI solutions that require persistent knowledge, explainability, and reliable decision-making.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [A Reproducibility Study of Metacognitive Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19899)
*Gabriel Iturra-Bocaz,Petra Galuscakova*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This reproducibility study validates MetaRAG's effectiveness while revealing challenges from closed-source LLM updates, missing implementation details, and unreleased prompts. Key findings show reranking significantly boosts performance and MetaRAG outperforms SIM-RAG in robustness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multi-retrieval RAG systems struggle to determine when to stop searching once sufficient information has been gathered.

Method: Reproduced MetaRAG following original experimental setup; extended by evaluating PointWise and ListWise rerankers; compared with SIM-RAG using lightweight critic model.

Result: MetaRAG's relative improvements over baselines confirmed, but absolute scores are lower than originally reported; reranking substantially improves performance; MetaRAG is more robust than SIM-RAG with additional retrieval features.

Conclusion: MetaRAG is partially reproduced, gains substantially from reranking, and is more robust than SIM-RAG when extended with additional retrieval features.

Abstract: Recently, Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) has shifted focus to multi-retrieval approaches to tackle complex tasks such as multi-hop question answering. However, these systems struggle to decide when to stop searching once enough information has been gathered. To address this, \citet{zhou2024metacognitive} introduced Metacognitive Retrieval Augmented Generation (MetaRAG), a framework inspired by metacognition that enables Large Language Models to critique and refine their reasoning. In this reproducibility paper, we reproduce MetaRAG following its original experimental setup and extend it in two directions: (i) by evaluating the effect of PointWise and ListWise rerankers, and (ii) by comparing with SIM-RAG, which employs a lightweight critic model to stop retrieval. Our results confirm MetaRAG's relative improvements over standard RAG and reasoning-based baselines, but also reveal lower absolute scores than reported, reflecting challenges with closed-source LLM updates, missing implementation details, and unreleased prompts. We show that MetaRAG is partially reproduced, gains substantially from reranking, and is more robust than SIM-RAG when extended with additional retrieval features.

</details>


### [200] [From Hidden Profiles to Governable Personalization: Recommender Systems in the Age of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20065)
*Jiahao Liu,Mingzhe Han,Guanming Liu,Weihang Wang,Dongsheng Li,Hansu Gu,Peng Zhang,Tun Lu,Ning Gu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型如何重塑个性化推荐系统的架构，指出核心问题不仅在于LLM提升推荐质量，更在于其改变了用户表征的生产、暴露和使用方式。作者提出从隐式平台画像转向可治理个性化的范式转变，并识别出五个相互关联的研究前沿，强调未来推荐系统必须让用户能够理解、塑造和治理其个性化体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个性化依赖平台特定用户模型，虽优化预测但对被描述的人群不可及。随着LLM助手日益成为搜索、购物、旅游等内容访问的中介，现有安排正在向新的个性化架构转变。

Method: 提出从"隐藏的平台画像"转向"可治理的个性化"的框架，将五个研究前沿定位为相互关联的设计问题，而非孤立的技术挑战。这些前沿涵盖透明隐私保护用户建模、意图翻译与对齐、跨域表征与记忆设计、可信商业化以及所有权访问问责机制。

Result: 识别出推荐系统的五个研究前沿：(1)透明且保护隐私的用户建模；(2)意图翻译与对齐；(3)跨域表征与记忆设计；(4)助手中介环境中的可信商业化；(5)所有权、访问与问责的运营机制。

Conclusion: 从隐式平台画像向可治理个性化的范式转变，强调未来推荐系统的发展不仅依赖更好的推理能力，更取决于构建用户能够理解、塑造和治理的个性化系统。

Abstract: Personalization has traditionally depended on platform-specific user models that are optimized for prediction but remain largely inaccessible to the people they describe. As LLM-based assistants increasingly mediate search, shopping, travel, and content access, this arrangement may be giving way to a new personalization stack in which user representation is no longer confined to isolated platforms. In this paper, we argue that the key issue is not simply that large language models can enhance recommendation quality, but that they reconfigure where and how user representations are produced, exposed, and acted upon. We propose a shift from hidden platform profiling toward governable personalization, where user representations may become more inspectable, revisable, portable, and consequential across services. Building on this view, we identify five research fronts for recommender systems: transparent yet privacy-preserving user modeling, intent translation and alignment, cross-domain representation and memory design, trustworthy commercialization in assistant-mediated environments, and operational mechanisms for ownership, access, and accountability. We position these not as isolated technical challenges, but as interconnected design problems created by the emergence of LLM agents as intermediaries between users and digital platforms. We argue that the future of recommender systems will depend not only on better inference, but on building personalization systems that users can meaningfully understand, shape, and govern.

</details>


### [201] [SAKE: Self-aware Knowledge Exploitation-Exploration for Grounded Multimodal Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20146)
*Jielong Tang,Xujie Yuan,Jiayang Liu,Jianxing Yu,Xiao Dong,Lin Chen,Yunlai Teng,Shimin Di,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAKE是一个端到端的智能体框架，通过自监督推理和自适应搜索工具调用，协同利用内部知识和外部知识来解决社交媒体场景下的接地多模态命名实体识别（GMNER）问题，采用包含监督微调和强化学习的两阶段训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体平台上GMNER面临长尾、快速演化和未见实体的挑战。现有方法存在两极分化问题：基于外部知识检索的方法易引入噪声导致精度下降，而仅依赖MLLM内部知识的方法受限于知识边界且易产生幻觉。

Method: 提出两阶段训练范式：第一阶段通过Difficulty-aware Search Tag Generation方法，利用多次前向采样量化模型实体级不确定性，生成SAKE-SeCoT高质量思维链数据集进行监督微调；第二阶段采用智能体强化学习，结合混合奖励函数惩罚不必要的检索，使模型从刚性搜索模仿进化为真正基于自知的决策能力。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的社交媒体基准数据集上验证了SAKE的有效性。

Conclusion: SAKE成功实现了内部知识利用与外部知识探索的协调统一，通过自感知推理和自适应工具调用克服了传统方法的局限性，为开放世界社交媒体场景下的GMNER提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Grounded Multimodal Named Entity Recognition (GMNER) aims to extract named entities and localize their visual regions within image-text pairs, serving as a pivotal capability for various downstream applications. In open-world social media platforms, GMNER remains challenging due to the prevalence of long-tailed, rapidly evolving, and unseen entities. To tackle this, existing approaches typically rely on either external knowledge exploration through heuristic retrieval or internal knowledge exploitation via iterative refinement in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, heuristic retrieval often introduces noisy or conflicting evidence that degrades precision on known entities, while solely internal exploitation is constrained by the knowledge boundaries of MLLMs and prone to hallucinations. To address this, we propose SAKE, an end-to-end agentic framework that harmonizes internal knowledge exploitation and external knowledge exploration via self-aware reasoning and adaptive search tool invocation. We implement this via a two-stage training paradigm. First, we propose Difficulty-aware Search Tag Generation, which quantifies the model's entity-level uncertainty through multiple forward samplings to produce explicit knowledge-gap signals. Based on these signals, we construct SAKE-SeCoT, a high-quality Chain-of-Thought dataset that equips the model with basic self-awareness and tool-use capabilities through supervised fine-tuning. Second, we employ agentic reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward function that penalizes unnecessary retrieval, enabling the model to evolve from rigid search imitation to genuine self-aware decision-making about when retrieval is truly necessary. Extensive experiments on two widely used social media benchmarks demonstrate SAKE's effectiveness.

</details>


### [202] [HaS: Accelerating RAG through Homology-Aware Speculative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20452)
*Peng Peng,Weiwei Lin,Wentai Wu,Xinyang Wang,Yongheng Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) expands the knowledge boundary of large language models (LLMs) at inference by retrieving external documents as context. However, retrieval becomes increasingly time-consuming as the knowledge databases grow in size. Existing acceleration strategies either compromise accuracy through approximate retrieval, or achieve marginal gains by reusing results of strictly identical queries. We propose HaS, a homology-aware speculative retrieval framework that performs low-latency speculative retrieval over restricted scopes to obtain candidate documents, followed by validating whether they contain the required knowledge. The validation, grounded in the homology relation between queries, is formulated as a homologous query re-identification task: once a previously observed query is identified as a homologous re-encounter of the incoming query, the draft is deemed acceptable, allowing the system to bypass slow full-database retrieval. Benefiting from the prevalence of homologous queries under real-world popularity patterns, HaS achieves substantial efficiency gains. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HaS reduces retrieval latency by 23.74% and 36.99% across datasets with only a 1-2% marginal accuracy drop. As a plug-and-play solution, HaS also significantly accelerates complex multi-hop queries in modern agentic RAG pipelines. Source code is available at: https://github.com/ErrEqualsNil/HaS.

</details>


### [203] [Break the Optimization Barrier of LLM-Enhanced Recommenders: A Theoretical Analysis and Practical Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20490)
*Zhangchi Zhu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 现有LLM增强推荐方法会阻碍骨干模型优化，导致高训练损失。本研究通过理论分析发现两个关键原因：范数差异大和语义-协作角聚类错位。提出TF-LLMER框架，包含嵌入归一化和Rec-PCA降维两个组件，在理论和实验上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM增强推荐方法会严重阻碍骨干模型的优化，导致训练损失高且难以降低。现有方法在注入LLM表示时存在优化困难的问题。

Method: 1. 建立局部优化曲率的综合理论分析，识别出两个关键原因：范数差异大和语义-协作角聚类错位。2. 提出TF-LLMER框架，包含两个组件：(1) 项目嵌入归一化，消除范数驱动的非稳定性；(2) Rec-PCA方法，通过对齐项目-项目共现图注入协作结构，同时保留语义信息。

Result: TF-LLMER在多个数据集上显著优于现有最先进方法，理论和实验均验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 通过嵌入归一化和协作感知降维，有效解决了LLM增强推荐系统的优化困难问题，为该领域提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-enhanced recommendation models inject LLM representations into backbone recommenders to exploit rich item text without inference-time LLM cost. However, we find that existing LLM-enhanced methods significantly hinder the optimization of backbone models, resulting in high training losses that are difficult to reduce. To address it, we establish a comprehensive theoretical analysis of local optimization curvature and identify two key causes: 1) large norm disparity and 2) semantic-collaboration misaligned angular clustering of LLM representations. Guided by these insights, we propose Training-Friendly LLM-Enhanced Recommender (TF-LLMER), a lightweight framework with two key components. First, we highlight the necessity of item embedding normalization to eliminate norm-driven instability and achieve provable control over optimization conditioning. Second, we introduce Rec-PCA, a recommendation-aware dimensionality reduction method that injects collaborative structure into the representation transformation to resolve semantic-collaboration misaligned angular clustering. It jointly optimizes semantic information retention and alignment with an item-item co-occurrence graph constructed from interaction histories. The graph captures collaborative structure, and alignment is promoted by penalizing total variation over the graph. Both theory and extensive experiments demonstrate that TF-LLMER significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/woriazzc/TF-LLMER.

</details>


### [204] [Self-Aware Vector Embeddings for Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Neuroscience-Inspired Framework for Temporal, Confidence-Weighted, and Relational Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20598)
*Naizhong Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SmartVector addresses static embedding limitations in RAG by adding temporal/relational awareness and confidence decay mechanisms, inspired by hippocampal-neocortical memory consolidation, achieving near-double accuracy and significantly reduced stale answers on versioned queries.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Modern RAG systems treat vector embeddings as static, context-free artifacts with no temporal validity, trustworthiness, or dependency awareness. This leads to poor performance (58% accuracy) on versioned technical queries, returning semantically similar but temporally invalid content.

Method: SmartVector augments dense embeddings with temporal awareness, confidence decay, and relational awareness, replacing cosine similarity with a four-signal scoring system (semantic relevance, temporal validity, live confidence, graph-relational importance). A background consolidation agent detects contradictions, builds dependency edges, and propagates updates via GNN-style messages. Confidence is governed by a closed-form function combining Ebbinghaus exponential decay, user-feedback reconsolidation, and logarithmic access reinforcement.

Result: On a synthetic versioned-policy benchmark (258 vectors, 138 queries): top-1 accuracy improves from 31.0% to 62.0%; stale-answer rate drops from 35.0% to 13.3%; Expected Calibration Error reduces from 0.470 to 0.244; re-embedding cost per single-word edit reduced by 77%; robust across 0-75% contradiction-injection rates.

Conclusion: SmartVector provides a robust solution for versioned knowledge retrieval, nearly doubling accuracy while significantly reducing stale answers, calibration error, and re-embedding costs, with consistent performance across varying contradiction rates.

Abstract: Modern retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat vector embeddings as static, context-free artifacts: an embedding has no notion of when it was created, how trustworthy its source is, or which other embeddings depend on it. This flattening of knowledge has a measurable cost: recent work on VersionRAG reports that conventional RAG achieves only 58% accuracy on versioned technical queries, because retrieval returns semantically similar but temporally invalid content. We propose SmartVector, a framework that augments dense embeddings with three explicit properties -- temporal awareness, confidence decay, and relational awareness -- and a five-stage lifecycle modeled on hippocampal-neocortical memory consolidation. A retrieval pipeline replaces pure cosine similarity with a four-signal score that mixes semantic relevance, temporal validity, live confidence, and graph-relational importance. A background consolidation agent detects contradictions, builds dependency edges, and propagates updates along those edges as graph-neural-network-style messages. Confidence is governed by a closed-form function combining an Ebbinghaus-style exponential decay, user-feedback reconsolidation, and logarithmic access reinforcement. We formalize the model, relate it to temporal knowledge graph embedding, agentic memory architectures, and uncertainty-aware RAG, and present a reference implementation. On a reproducible synthetic versioned-policy benchmark of 258 vectors and 138 queries, SmartVector roughly doubles top-1 accuracy over plain cosine RAG (62.0% vs. 31.0% on a held-out split), drops stale-answer rate from 35.0% to 13.3%, cuts Expected Calibration Error by nearly 2x (0.244 vs. 0.470), reduces re-embedding cost per single-word edit by 77%, and is robust across contradiction-injection rates from 0% to 75%.

</details>


### [205] [Coverage, Not Averages: Semantic Stratification for Trustworthy Retrieval Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20763)
*Andrew Klearman,Radu Revutchi,Rohin Garg,Rishav Chakravarti,Samuel Marc Denton,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文将检索评估形式化为统计估计问题，发现当前基于启发式查询集构建的评估方法存在内在偏差。通过提出语义分层（semantic stratification）框架，基于实体聚类组织文档并系统生成缺失层级的查询，从而获得形式化的语义覆盖保证和可解释的失败模式洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前检索增强生成（RAG）的评估依赖于启发式构建的查询集，存在隐藏的内在偏差，导致评估指标不可靠，限制了RAG系统的准确性和鲁棒性提升。

Method: 提出语义分层框架：基于实体聚类将文档组织到可解释的全局语义空间中，系统性识别缺失层级并生成对应查询，实现形式化的语义覆盖保证和可解释的失败模式可视化。

Result: 实验验证表明：该框架可暴露系统性覆盖缺口，识别解释检索性能差异的结构性信号，实现比聚合指标更稳定透明的评估。

Conclusion: 语义分层评估框架为检索系统提供了理论上更可靠的评估范式，支持比传统聚合指标更值得信赖的决策。

Abstract: Retrieval quality is the primary bottleneck for accuracy and robustness in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Current evaluation relies on heuristically constructed query sets, which introduce a hidden intrinsic bias. We formalize retrieval evaluation as a statistical estimation problem, showing that metric reliability is fundamentally limited by the evaluation-set construction. We further introduce \emph{semantic stratification}, which grounds evaluation in corpus structure by organizing documents into an interpretable global space of entity-based clusters and systematically generating queries for missing strata. This yields (1) formal semantic coverage guarantees across retrieval regimes and (2) interpretable visibility into retrieval failure modes.
  Experiments across multiple benchmarks and retrieval methods validate our framework. The results expose systematic coverage gaps, identify structural signals that explain variance in retrieval performance, and show that stratified evaluation yields more stable and transparent assessments while supporting more trustworthy decision-making than aggregate metrics.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [3DPipe: A Pipelined GPU Framework for Scalable Generalized Spatial Join over Polyhedral Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19982)
*Lyuheng Yuan,Da Yan,Akhlaque Ahmad,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出3DPipe，一个用于多面体对象可扩展3D空间连接的流水线GPU框架。通过在过滤和精炼两阶段利用GPU并行、多层剪枝策略减少候选集、分块流式处理超越GPU内存限制，以及流水线执行重叠各计算环节，3DPipe实现了最高9.0倍于TDBase的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LiDAR目标检测、3D数字病理等应用的快速发展，3D数据量急剧增长，对3D数据集的空间连接操作需求日益迫切。然而，现有空间连接技术主要针对2D数据设计，3D空间连接研究尚不充分，计算成本高昂。

Method: 3DPipe采用流水线GPU框架实现3D空间连接，包含三个关键技术：1) 在过滤和精炼两阶段均充分利用GPU并行计算能力；2) 多层剪枝策略有效减少候选集；3) 分块流式处理突破GPU内存限制。系统通过流水线执行重叠CPU数据准备、主机-设备数据传输和GPU计算三个环节。

Result: 实验表明，3DPipe相比当前最先进的GPU解决方案TDBase实现了最高9.0倍的加速比，同时保持了优异的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 3DPipe为多面体对象的大规模3D空间连接提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，在性能上显著优于现有GPU解决方案，并具有良好可扩展性。该工作填补了3D空间连接研究中GPU加速的空白，为LiDAR目标检测、3D数字病理等应用提供了重要的基础设施支撑。

Abstract: Spatial join is a fundamental operation in spatial databases. With the rapid growth of 3D data in applications such as LiDAR-based object detection and 3D digital pathology, there is an increasing need to support spatial join over 3D datasets. However, existing techniques are largely designed for 2D data, leaving 3D spatial join underexplored and computationally expensive. We present 3DPipe, a pipelined GPU framework for scalable spatial join over polyhedral objects. 3DPipe exploits GPU parallelism across both filtering and refinement stages, incorporates a multi-level pruning strategy for efficient candidate reduction, and employs chunked streaming to handle datasets exceeding GPU memory. Its pipelined execution overlaps CPU data preparation, host-device data transfer, and GPU computation to improve throughput. Experiments show that 3DPipe achieves up to 9.0$\times$ speedup over the state-of-the-art GPU solution, TDBase, while maintaining excellent scalability. 3DPipe is open-sourced at https://github.com/lyuheng/3dpipe.

</details>


### [207] [A GPU-Accelerated Framework for Multi-Attribute Range Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20121)
*Zhonggen Li,Haoran Yu,Yifan Zhu,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Garfield是一个GPU加速的多属性范围过滤ANNS框架，通过GMG轻量索引和硬件感知执行管道解决现有方案的索引膨胀和CPU计算瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范围过滤近似最近邻搜索（RFANNS）对现代向量数据库日益重要，但现有方案存在严重索引膨胀和高构建开销问题，且仅依赖CPU进行索引和查询处理，未能利用GPU强大算力。

Method: 1) 提出GMG索引：对数据进行单元划分，构建局部图索引，添加常数级跨单元边，保证线性存储和索引复杂度；2) 集群引导排序策略：重排查询相关单元，支持GPU上高效的逐单元遍历，跨单元复用候选项作为入口点；3) 单元导向核外管道：动态调度单元以最小化批次中活跃查询数，重叠GPU计算与CPU-GPU索引流传输。

Result: 索引体积缩小4.4倍，吞吐量提升119.8倍，优于现有最优RFANNS方法。

Conclusion: Garfield通过轻量级索引设计和GPU感知执行管道，有效克服了现有RFANNS方案的索引膨胀和高构建开销瓶颈。GMG索引的常数级跨单元边设计保证了线性存储和索引开销，集群引导排序策略实现了高效的GPU逐单元遍历，单元导向的核外管道则支持超GPU内存数据集的处理。

Abstract: Range-filtered approximate nearest neighbor search (RFANNS) is increasingly critical for modern vector databases. However, existing solutions suffer from severe index inflation and construction overhead. Furthermore, they rely exclusively on CPUs for the heavy indexing and query processing, failing to leverage the powerful computational capabilities of GPUs. In this paper, we present Garfield, a GPU-accelerated framework for multi-attribute range filtered ANNS that overcomes these bottlenecks through designing a lightweight index structure and hardware-aware execution pipeline. Garfield introduces the GMG index, which partitions data into cells and builds local graph indexes. By adding a constant number of cross-cell edges, it guarantees linear storage and indexing overhead. For queries, Garfield utilizes a cluster-guided ordering strategy that reorders query-relevant cells, enabling a highly efficient cell-by-cell traversal on the GPU that aggressively reuses candidates as entry points across cells. To handle datasets exceeding GPU memory, Garfield features a cell-oriented out-of-core pipeline. It dynamically schedules cells to minimize the number of active queries per batch and overlaps GPU computation with CPU-to-GPU index streaming. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Garfield reduces index size by 4.4x, while delivering 119.8x higher throughput than state-of-the-art RFANNS methods.

</details>


### [208] [An Agentic Approach to Metadata Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20144)
*Jiani Zhang,Sercan O. Arik,Cosmin Arad,Fatma Ozcan,Alon Halevy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出Metadata Reasoner，一种用于数据源发现的智能体方法。通过结合表格搜索引擎和自主元数据推理，在真实数据集KramaBench上达到83.16% F1-score，比现有最佳方法提升32个百分点；在含噪声的BIRD数据集上保持85.5% F1-score和99%低质量数据规避率，证明了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-driven autonomous agents performing complex multi-step tasks face a critical bottleneck in discovering relevant data sources, compounded by the sheer volume of available data and the nuanced semantics requiring consideration of many aspects.

Method: The Metadata Reasoner is an agentic approach combining: (1) a table-search engine for candidate retrieval, and (2) autonomous metadata consultation to evaluate whether candidates meet task requirements, with goal of finding sufficient yet minimal data source sets.

Result: KramaBench: 83.16% average F1-score (32 percentage points above baselines); BIRD benchmark: 85.5% average F1-score in noisy environment, 99% success rate in avoiding low-quality data.

Conclusion: The Metadata Reasoner demonstrates a substantial improvement over existing methods, achieving 32 percentage points higher F1-score on real-world data and maintaining high robustness (85.5% F1, 99% success rate) in noisy environments with redundant/low-quality tables.

Abstract: As LLM-driven autonomous agents evolve to perform complex, multi-step tasks that require integrating multiple datasets, the problem of discovering relevant data sources becomes a key bottleneck. Beyond the challenge posed by the sheer volume of available data sources, data-source selection is difficult because the semantics of data are extremely nuanced and require considering many aspects of the data. To address this, we introduce the Metadata Reasoner, an agentic approach to metadata reasoning, designed to identify a small set of data sources that are both sufficient and minimal for a given analytical task. The Metadata Reasoner leverages a table-search engine to retrieve candidate tables, and then autonomously consults various aspects of the available metadata to determine whether the candidates fit the requirements of the task. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the Metadata Reasoner through a series of empirical studies. Evaluated on the real-world KramaBench datasets for data selection, our approach achieves an average F1-score of 83.16%, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by a substantial margin of 32 percentage points. Furthermore, evaluations on a newly-created synthetic benchmark based on the BIRD data lake reveal that the Metadata Reasoner is highly robust against redundant and low-quality tables that may be in the data lake. In this noisy environment, it maintains an average of 85.5% F1-score for selecting the right datasets and demonstrates a 99% success rate in avoiding low-quality data.

</details>


### [209] [Pre-Execution Query Slot-Time Prediction in Cloud Data Warehouses: A Feature-Scoped Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20145)
*Prashant Kumar Pathak*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出基于机器学习的方法，在查询执行前预测BigQuery计算槽时间，通过融合SQL操作符复杂度评分、数据卷特征和查询文本TF-IDF特征，实现比基线方法30-37%的误差降低。但对于长尾查询(>=20分钟)效果不佳，可能因运行时因素不可观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据仓库按槽时间计费，但查询成本高度可变且难以预估，导致预算超支和调度性能下降。传统静态查询规划器启发式方法无法捕获复杂的SQL结构、数据偏斜和工作负载竞争等问题。

Method: 1) 仅使用执行前可观测信号：SQL操作符成本导出的查询复杂度评分、计划器估算的数据量特征和工作负载元数据、查询文本的文本特征；2) 使用HistGradientBoostingRegressor对数变换后的槽时间建模；3) 文本特征采用TF-IDF + TruncatedSVD-512管道；4) 刻意排除运行时因素（槽池利用率、缓存状态、实际偏斜）；5) 训练集：7个部署环境的749个查询；测试集：2个保留环境的746个查询。

Result: 全工作负载MAE 1.17槽分钟，RMSE 4.71，可解释方差74%；成本显著查询(>=0.01分钟)MAE 3.10 vs 预测均值4.95 vs 预测中位数4.54，误差降低30-37%；长尾查询(>=20分钟)性能未优于平凡基线。

Conclusion: 长尾查询性能不足支持假设：其主要由当前特征范围外的运行时因素主导。提出复杂度路由双模型架构作为实用改进方向，并识别出弥补长尾差距的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Cloud data warehouses bill compute based on slot-time consumed. In shared multi-tenant environments, query cost is highly variable and hard to estimate before execution, causing budget overruns and degraded scheduling. Static query-planner heuristics fail to capture complex SQL structure, data skew, and workload contention. We present a feature-scoped machine learning approach that predicts BigQuery slot-time before execution using only pre-execution observable signals: a structured query complexity score derived from SQL operator costs, data volume features from planner estimates and workload metadata, and textual features from query text. We deliberately exclude runtime factors (slot-pool utilization, cache state, realized skew) unknowable at submission. The model uses a HistGradientBoostingRegressor trained on log-transformed slot-time, with a TF-IDF + TruncatedSVD-512 text pipeline fused with numeric and categorical features. Trained on 749 queries across seven deployment environments and evaluated out-of-distribution on 746 queries from two held-out environments, the model achieves MAE 1.17 slot-minutes, RMSE 4.71, and 74% explained variance on the full workload. On cost-significant queries (slot-time >= 0.01 min, N=282) the model achieves MAE 3.10 versus 4.95 for a predict-mean baseline and 4.54 for predict-median, a 30-37% reduction. On long-tail queries (>= 20 min, N=22) the model does not outperform trivial baselines, consistent with the hypothesis that long-tail queries are dominated by unobserved runtime factors outside the current feature scope. A complexity-routed dual-model architecture is described as a practical refinement, and directions for closing the long-tail gap are identified as future work.

</details>

### [210] [Blossom VI: A Practical Minimum Weight Perfect Matching Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20351)
*Pavel Arkhipov,Vladimir Kolmogorov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We implement an algorithm for solving the minimum weight perfect matching problem. Our code significantly outperforms the current state-of-the-art Blossom V algorithm on those families of instances where Blossom V takes superlinear time. In practice, our implementation shows almost-linear runtime on every family of instances on which we have tested it.
  Our algorithm relies on solving the maximum-cardinality unweighted matching problems during its primal phase. Following the state-of-the-art cherry blossom algorithm, we use cherry trees instead of traditional alternating trees and cherry blossoms instead of traditional blossoms. We shrink cherry blossoms rather than traditional blossoms into supernodes. This strategy allows us to deal with much shallower supernodes.

</details>


### [211] [Nearly Optimal Bounds for Computing Decision Tree Splits in Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20394)
*Hoang Ta,Hoa T. Vu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据流中决策树分裂的近似问题，对回归和分类任务分别给出了接近最优的空间界。回归任务得到单轮算法，空间复杂度为Õ(M²/ε)；分类任务得到单轮算法，空间复杂度为Õ(1/ε)。两类问题均提供了匹配的空间下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树学习是机器学习基础任务，数据流模型下空间效率至关重要。现有方法存在两轮限制或空间次优的问题，需要设计单轮且空间最优的算法。

Method: 利用损失函数的Lipschitz性质，结合水库采样(reservoir sampling)和支持范围查询的Count-Min草图，通过精心设计的数据结构实现单轮空间高效算法。

Result: 回归：单轮算法达到Õ(M²/ε)空间，改进原两轮算法；分类：单轮算法达到Õ(1/ε)空间，改进原Õ(1/ε²)算法；两类问题均给出匹配的空间下界Ω(M²/ε)和Ω(1/ε)。

Conclusion: 在数据流模型下，决策树分裂近似问题已达到接近最优的空间-精度权衡，为该领域建立了理论基础。

Abstract: We establish nearly optimal upper and lower bounds for approximating decision tree splits in data streams. For regression with labels in the range $\{0,1,\ldots,M\}$, we give a one-pass algorithm using $\tilde{O}(M^2/ε)$ space that outputs a split within additive $ε$ error of the optimal split, improving upon the two-pass algorithm of Pham et al. (ISIT 2025). Furthermore, we provide a matching one-pass lower bound showing that $Ω(M^2/ε)$ space is indeed necessary.
  For classification, we also obtain a one-pass algorithm using $\tilde{O}(1/ε)$ space for approximating the optimal Gini split, improving upon the previous $\tilde{O}(1/ε^2)$-space algorithm. We complement these results with matching space lower bounds: $Ω(1/ε)$ for Gini impurity and $Ω(1/ε)$ for misclassification (which matches the upper bound obtained by sampling).
  Our algorithms exploit the Lipschitz property of the loss functions and use reservoir sampling along with Count--Min sketches with range queries. Our lower bounds follow from careful reductions from the INDEX problem.

</details>


### [212] [Cluster Vertex Deletion on Chordal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20457)
*Yixin Cao,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出一种在弦图上多项式时间算法解决cluster vertex deletion问题，使用基于团树的动态规划和亚模集合函数最小化，解决了多个近期文献中提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决弦图上cluster vertex deletion问题的多项式时间算法这一开放问题，该问题此前被多个研究团队在2018-2024年间反复提出，涉及到理论计算机科学、数学规划和算法领域。

Method: 在弦图的团树(clique tree)上进行动态规划，将最优子问题值的计算规约为亚模集合函数(submodular set function)的最小化问题。

Result: 成功设计出弦图上cluster vertex deletion问题的多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 该算法解决了弦图上cluster vertex deletion问题的计算复杂性这一悬而未决的理论问题，具有重要的理论贡献。

Abstract: We present a polynomial-time algorithm for the cluster vertex deletion problem on chordal graphs, resolving an open question posed in different contexts by Cao et al. [Theoretical Computer Science, 2018], Aprile et al. [Mathematical Programming, 2023], Chakraborty et al. [Discrete Applied Mathematics, 2024], and Hsieh et al. [Algorithmica, 2024]. We use dynamic programming over clique trees and reduce the computation of the optimal subproblem value to the minimization of a submodular set function.

</details>


### [213] [Fully Dynamic Algorithms for Coloring Triangle-Free Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20648)
*Sepehr Assadi,Helia Yazdanyar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文给出了一种动态算法，可在边插入删除条件下维护三角自由图（triangle-free graph）的 O(Δ/lnΔ) 着色，其中 Δ 为最大度。算法随机化，摊销更新时间为 Δ^{o(1)}·log n，高概率抵御自适应对手。核心创新在于将熵压缩法（entropy compression method）引入动态图算法场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Johansson 的经典结果证明三角自由图可 O(Δ/lnΔ) 着色且多项式时间可找，但此前无动态维护算法。本文旨在将这一最优上界拓展至边动态更新的场景，填补文献空白。

Method: 核心工具为熵压缩法——一种源自组合学（尤其是 Lovász局部引理构造性证明）的分析方法。算法设计为随机化，通过追踪更新过程中的"熵余量"证明摊销复杂度界。方法论上，将静态分析中的熵压缩技术适配至动态对抗场景。

Result: 对 n 顶点图，算法以高概率实现摊销更新时间 O(Δ^{o(1)}·log n)，保持 O(Δ/lnΔ) 颜色数。算法可抵御自适应 adversary（即对手可根据历史查询调整插入/删除操作）。

Conclusion: 给出了三角自由图动态着色问题的最优（与静态最优一致）彩色数保证及多对数级更新时间。熵压缩法在动态算法中的成功应用表明该技术具有普适性，有望推广至其他动态组合问题。

Abstract: A celebrated result of Johansson in graph theory states that every triangle-free graph of maximum degree $Δ$ can be properly colored with $O(Δ/\lnΔ)$ colors, improving upon the "greedy bound" of $Δ+1$ coloring in general graphs. This coloring can also be found in polynomial time.
  We present an algorithm for maintaining an $O(Δ/\lnΔ)$ coloring of a dynamically changing triangle-free graph that undergoes edge insertions and deletions. The algorithm is randomized and on $n$-vertex graphs has amortized update time of $Δ^{o(1)}\log{n}$ per update with high probability, even against an adaptive adversary.
  A key to the analysis of our algorithm is an application of the entropy compression method that to our knowledge is new in the context of dynamic algorithms. This technique appears general and is likely to find other applications in dynamic problems and thus can be of its own independent interest.

</details>


### [214] [Dynamic Construction of the Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20836)
*Bernhard Haeupler,Slobodan Mitrović,Srikkanth Ramachandran,Wen-Horng Sheu,Robert Tarjan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了大量局部搜索算法可以在完全动态设置下直接扩展到自适应对抗模型，实现每个更新操作 amortized Õ(1) 次局部搜索步骤。通过 witness-tree 和 entropy compression 技术，分析了局部重采样或回溯过程的收敛性，并应用于边着色等约束满足问题的动态维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部搜索算法在静态实例上表现出色（通常只需亚线性修复步骤），但将其扩展到动态设置面临挑战——对手可以自适应地添加或删除约束。核心问题：如何在这种对抗性扰动下保持算法的收敛效率？此前该领域的动态算法（如 Christiansen SODA '26 的边着色）在某些参数 regime 下需要指数级运行时间。

Method: 利用 Moser (2009) 引入的 witness-tree 和 entropy compression 技术框架，将静态局部搜索算法的分析推广到动态对抗模型。核心思路：对违反约束的变量进行重采样或回溯，通过 witness-tree 结构追踪算法的执行历史，并借助 entropy compression 证明算法效率。

Result: 证明了简单的局部搜索过程在完全动态设置下仍保持 near-linear 的总重采样步骤数，即每个更新操作的 amortized Õ(1) 局部搜索步骤。具体应用：对于最大度 Δ = poly(log n) 和常数 ε，可维护 (1+ε)Δ-边着色，amortized 更新时间为 poly(log n)。

Conclusion: 结果具有广泛适用性，将多种局部搜索算法的静态收敛保证扩展到动态对抗设置。显著改进了此前该领域的动态算法复杂度（如边着色问题从指数时间提升到多项式时间），证明了许多已有的局部搜索算法无需修改即可在动态环境中保持高效。

Abstract: This paper proves that a wide class of local search algorithms extend as is to the fully dynamic setting with an adaptive adversary, achieving an amortized $\tilde{O}(1)$ number of local-search steps per update.
  A breakthrough by Moser (2009) introduced the witness-tree and entropy compression techniques for analyzing local resampling processes for the Lovász Local Lemma. These methods have since been generalized and expanded to analyze a wide variety of local search algorithms that can efficiently find solutions to many important local constraint satisfaction problems. These algorithms either extend a partial valid assignment and backtrack by unassigning variables when constraints become violated, or they iteratively fix violated constraints by resampling their variables. These local resampling or backtracking procedures are incredibly flexible, practical, and simple to specify and implement. Yet, they can be shown to be extremely efficient on static instances, typically performing only (sub)-linear number of fixing steps. The main technical challenge lies in proving conditions that guarantee such rapid convergence.
  This paper extends these convergence results to fully dynamic settings, where an adaptive adversary may add or remove constraints. We prove that applying the same simple local search procedures to fix old or newly introduced violations leads to a total number of resampling steps near-linear in the number of adversarial updates.
  Our result is very general and yields several immediate corollaries. For example, letting $Δ$ denote the maximum degree, for a constant $ε$ and $Δ= \text{poly}(\log n)$, we can maintain a $(1+ε) Δ$-edge coloring in $\text{poly}(\log n)$ amortized update time against an adaptive adversary. The prior work for this regime has exponential running time in $\sqrt{\log n}$ [Christiansen, SODA '26].

</details>
